Result: 在推理数据集基准上，OMVV在多种操作预算下均比任何单一固定验证器取得更高准确率与更低验证成本。

Conclusion: OMVV通过在线多验证器自适应路由，有效平衡验证成本与准确性，为LLM多步推理输出的可扩展验证提供了理论保证与实证优势。

Abstract: As large language models are increasingly deployed for multi-step reasoning, verifying the correctness of their outputs has become essential for maintaining reliability at scale. Verifying the correctness of large language model outputs is often done by querying a costly ground-truth oracle, which is impractical to invoke at every step in an online setting. Prior work addresses this by querying a single weak verifier on every step, and using its score to decide whether the costly strong verifier needs to be queried as well, reserving strong verification for only a small fraction of the steps. However, a single fixed weak verifier may not perform consistently well as the subject matter or difficulty of incoming queries changes over time, and committing to one in advance risks either overly costly or inaccurate verification. We introduce OMVV (Online Multi-Verifier Verification), an algorithm that maintains a pool of $K$ candidate weak verifiers with differing cost and verification performance, and adaptively routes each round's decision to a verifier selected via an online score combiner and an exponential-weights routing policy. OMVV provides a distribution-free, finite-time guarantee on false-accept and false-reject rates across the full pool of verifiers, and further achieves sublinear regret against the best fixed verifier in hindsight under a combined cost and consistency objective. Experiments on reasoning dataset benchmarks show that OMVV achieves higher accuracy at lower verification cost than any single fixed verifier, across a range of operating budgets.

</details>


### [67] [CreateScore: Domain-Theory-Informed Bayesian Routing for LLM-Based CV Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02972)
*Rupsa Roy*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出基于领域理论信息贝叶斯网络的CreateScore，用于简历按标准逐项路由LLM，以降低token成本并保持评分质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对所有候选人和所有标准都调用高能力LLM成本过高，因此需要一种按标准级别进行不确定性路由的低成本机制。

Method: 构建手工指定的有向无环图与Dirichlet-multinomial条件概率表，将简历证据转为后验不确定性；低不确定性由本地8B模型决策，高不确定性升级至120B参考模型；升级阈值在训练集上校准（目标70%本地解决）后冻结；在200份合成数据科学简历、5项标准上评估，并记录早期评估因截断推理输出被静默替换而失效的问题。

Result: 305项标准决策中77.7%本地解决；相对120B全量裁决，路由升级减少65.2% token，并将8B单独的精确评分一致率从32.8%提升至42.6%（95% CI 31.0-55.1%，n=61时差异不显著）；但不确定性信号未能识别8B出错项，升级项与参考不一致率16.2%，本地解决项19.4%，AUROC 0.47（95% CI 0.39-0.56），不优于随机。

Conclusion: CreateScore可作为可审计的成本削减机制，但尚不能作为针对性错误检测器，也不是自主招聘系统；建议为级联评估设置防护措施。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can support rubric-based screening of CVs, but applying a high-capability model to every candidate and criterion is costly. We present CreateScore, a domain-theory-informed Bayesian network for criterion-level LLM routing. A hand-specified directed acyclic graph with Dirichlet-multinomial conditional probability tables converts CV evidence into posterior uncertainty; low-uncertainty decisions are resolved by a local 8B model and uncertain ones are escalated to a 120B reference model. The graph is causally motivated, but the system performs standard Bayesian conditioning, not causal inference. The escalation threshold is calibrated on a training fold (target: 70% resolved locally) and then frozen. On 200 synthetic Data Science CVs (139 training and 61 test candidates, five criteria), 77.7% of criterion decisions were resolved locally (237 of 305). Relative to a reference condition in which the 120B model adjudicated every criterion, routed escalation reduced token use by 65.2% and raised exact score agreement from 32.8% (8B alone) to 42.6% (95% CI 31.0-55.1%); at n = 61 the gain was not statistically distinguishable. The uncertainty signal did not, however, identify the decisions on which the 8B model erred: disagreement with the reference was 16.2% among escalated and 19.4% among locally resolved decisions (AUROC 0.47, 95% CI 0.39-0.56), no better than random selection. We also document how an earlier evaluation was invalidated when truncated reasoning-model outputs were silently replaced by local labels, and we recommend safeguards for cascade evaluation. CreateScore is supported as an auditable cost-reduction mechanism, not yet as a targeted error detector, and is not an autonomous hiring system.

</details>


### [68] [Reliable Self-Evolution with Imperfect Proxy Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02975)
*Kangjun Noh,Soyu Kim,Kyungwoo Song*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出CISE，利用条件共形推断和在线密度比估计构建候选特定奖励区间，实现高精度自演化搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真评估昂贵时，低成本的代理奖励易产生假阳性，污染输出和后续反馈，需要统计校准的奖励区间。

Method: CISE通过条件共形推断和逐迭代在线密度比估计构建候选特定奖励区间，使用保守的区间奖励进行进化反馈，仅当所有属性区间完全位于可行域内时返回候选。

Result: 在材料科学三个自演化搜索任务中，CISE返回的所有候选在高保真评估下均为真阳性，而基线返回更多候选但包含假阳性。

Conclusion: 当下游验证预算有限时，更小但更精确的候选列表具有重要价值。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based self-evolving search is a promising approach to scientific discovery. However, high-fidelity evaluation of every candidate is prohibitively expensive in some domains. Self-evolving systems in such settings therefore rely on low-cost but imperfect proxy rewards, which may assign high scores to infeasible candidates. These false positives may contaminate both the final output and the feedback used to guide subsequent generations. This motivates statistically calibrated reward intervals for more reliable self-evolving search. We propose Conformal Interval-Driven Self-Evolution (CISE), which constructs candidate-specific reward intervals using conditional conformal inference and iteration-wise online density-ratio estimation. CISE uses conservative interval-based rewards for evolutionary feedback and returns candidates only when all required property intervals lie entirely within their respective feasible regions. We derive fixed-iteration coverage results under explicit assumptions of independence and covariate shift. We evaluate CISE on three self-evolving search tasks in materials science. In our experiments, all candidates returned by CISE are true positives under high-fidelity evaluation, whereas the baselines return more candidates but include false positives. These results highlight the value of a smaller, more precise shortlist when downstream validation budgets are limited. Our repository is available at https://github.com/MLAI-Yonsei/CISE.git.

</details>


### [69] [Relevant Evidence Decoding for Audio-Visual Hallucination Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02976)
*Hyunjae Ra,Aecheon Jung,Jungin Park,Sungeun Hong*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出免训练的相关证据解码RED，利用点互信息分解音视频贡献，按问题类型选择性增强正确模态证据，缓解音视频大模型跨模态幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音视频大模型易产生跨模态幻觉；对比解码直接迁移忽视了不同问题需要不同感知证据（音频、视频或其交互），且联合推理有时会削弱单一模态本可给出的正确预测。

Method: RED为免训练方法：用逐点互信息量化音频与视频在问题之外的预测支持，将联合贡献分解为音频、视频和残余交互成分；先进行一次仅问题推理以判定所需证据类型，再将对应PMI贡献增强到原始音视频预测上。

Result: 在三个音视频幻觉基准和三个AV-LLM上，RED相对标准解码提升准确率最高达CMM 7%、AVHBench 6.3%、SVHalluc 3.8%，首token平均相对时间为标准解码的1.5倍。

Conclusion: RED通过识别并选择性强化问题相关证据，有效减少跨模态幻觉、提升音视频推理准确率，且无需额外训练，代价仅为适度推理延迟。

Abstract: Audio-Visual Large Language Models (AV-LLMs) remain prone to cross-modal hallucinations, where one modality incorrectly affects predictions about another. Although contrastive decoding reduces hallucinations in vision-language models, its direct extension to AV-LLMs overlooks a key challenge: different questions require different perceptual evidence, including audio, video, or their interaction. Notably, we observe that joint audio-visual inference can weaken the prediction even when a model can recover the correct answer from a single informative modality. For example, when asked which instrument is heard, a model may correctly predict violin from the audio alone. Once a video showing a guitar is added, its confidence in violin may drop. In this paper, we introduce Relevant Evidence Decoding (RED), a training-free method that identifies question-relevant evidence and selectively strengthens its contribution. RED uses pointwise mutual information to quantify the predictive support provided by audio and video beyond the question alone. It decomposes their joint contribution into audio, video, and residual interaction components. A question-only inference pass determines the required evidence type, after which the model augments the original audio-visual prediction with the corresponding PMI contribution. Across three audio-visual hallucination benchmarks and three AV-LLMs, RED improves accuracy over standard decoding by up to 7% on CMM, 6.3% on AVHBench, and 3.8% on SVHalluc, with an average relative time to first token of 1.5x standard decoding.

</details>


### [70] [AIGS: Adaptive Incremental Gating System for Online Representation Learning in Non-Stationary Data Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02661)
*SiRui He,Kai Liang Lew,Chui Zi Ong,Chean Khim Toa*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出自适应增量门控系统AIGS，通过闭环状态感知机制解决在线表示学习中的稳定性-可塑性困境，在边缘计算场景下实现高效概念漂移适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web of Things和边缘计算中的实时数据流常经历潜在状态变化，现有方法采用固定更新计划或滚动窗口，在突发漂移时参数僵化、稳定时浪费计算资源，核心挑战是平衡历史知识保留与快速响应概念漂移。

Method: AIGS引入冲击比（Shock Ratio），一种内生残差反馈机制，将当前重构误差对近期变化进行归一化；该信号驱动连续可塑性控制器，在学习可塑性和记忆保留之间平滑插值，将表示学习视为闭环控制机制，保持每步严格线性O(k·d)复杂度。

Result: 在真实智慧城市动态流上实验：电力变压器温度数据集上实现预防性早期预警提前量8.31（ETTm1）和9.88（ETTm2）步；交通数据集上突变后恢复更快；高噪声天气数据集上提高异常召回并抵抗随机噪声过拟合。

Conclusion: AIGS可作为资源受限边缘监测系统的实用、即插即用适配器，避免灾难性遗忘，适合延迟敏感的边缘设备。

Abstract: Real-time data streams in Web of Things (WoT) and edge computing environments often evolve through latent regime changes. For online representation learning under strict computational constraints, the central problem is resolving the stability-plasticity dilemma: keeping useful historical knowledge while rapidly reacting to concept drift. Existing methods employ fixed update schedules or rolling windows. However, they suffer from parameter ossification during sudden shifts and waste computational resources when the stream remains stable. This paper proposes the Adaptive Incremental Gating System (AIGS), a lightweight closed-loop state-aware adaptation framework. AIGS introduces the Shock Ratio, an endogenous residual feedback mechanism that normalizes current reconstruction error against recent variation. This signal drives a Continuous Plasticity Controller that smoothly interpolates between learning plasticity and memory retention. By treating representation learning as a closed-loop control mechanism, AIGS avoids catastrophic forgetting and maintains a strictly linear $\mathcal{O}\left(k\cdot d\right)$ per-step complexity suitable for latency-sensitive edge devices. Experiments on real-world smart city dynamic streams-spanning traffic networks, meteorological systems, and industrial infrastructure-demonstrate distinct domain-dependent advantages. On Electricity Transformer Temperature datasets, AIGS achieves preventative early-warning lead times of 8.31 (ETTm1) and 9.88 (ETTm2) steps under gradual degradation. On Performance Measurement System traffic datasets, it shows significantly faster post-shift recovery after abrupt mutations. On the highly noisy Weather dataset, it improves anomaly recall while resisting stochastic noise overfitting. These findings establish AIGS as a practical, plug-and-play adapter for resource-constrained edge monitoring systems.

</details>


### [71] [LEAP: Learning Efficient Action Proposals For LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02670)
*Zhen Xu,Qizheng Zhang,Gerry Wan,Shang Zhu,Ce Zhang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出延迟框架分析动作推测的端到端加速，并据此设计LEAP方法，通过训练小型草稿模型准确预测目标动作，实现智能体最高60%的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体逐步执行任务，推理和动作串行导致rollout缓慢；现有动作推测使用现成大模型或小模型，分别存在延迟高或预测准确率低的问题，需明确决定加速效果的关键因素。

Method: 建立动作推测轮次的延迟框架，量化收益（草稿预测准确度、任务剩余步数）与成本（草稿生成、目标验证等待、工具执行）；提出LEAP，用小规模0.6B草稿模型在目标动作序列上训练，使其与目标决策高度一致，并支持在线训练无需预先收集轨迹。

Result: LEAP使智能体端到端墙钟时间加速最高60%，任务成功率无系统性变化；在多种数据集、目标模型和草稿模型上，延迟框架能解释大部分实测加速；在线训练可达到离线训练性能。

Conclusion: 动作推测的加速由草稿预测质量与任务步数权衡决定；训练小型专用草稿模型可兼顾速度与准确性，且易于实际部署。

Abstract: LLM agents are known to be slow in rollouts. An agent completes a task one step at a time. At each step, it reasons and then chooses an action to execute. The next step and action cannot start until the previous one has finished. Speculative decoding accelerates the rollouts at the reason phase by drafting and verifying the inference tokens. Recent works have also started to apply similar ideas at the action phase. These works use off-the-shelf models, usually large, to draft action proposals for target model to verify. Large drafters match the target more often but take longer to propose, while small off-the-shelf models are fast but rarely make the same decision as the target. We ask a more general question: what determines the end-to-end speedup of action speculation? To answer it, we develop a latency framework for the speculative round. The framework compares what a round gains with what it costs. The gain depends on how well the drafter predicts the target and on how many steps the task can take before it ends. The cost comes from drafting, from waiting for target verification and from executing tools. Guided by the framework, we introduce LEAP (Learning Efficient Action Proposals) which keeps the drafter small and makes it accurate by training it on the target actions sequences. With a small 0.6B model, LEAP agrees with the target on most decisions and makes agents up to 60% faster in end-to-end wall clock time, with no systematic change in task success. Across various datasets, target models and draft models, the framework accounts for most of the measured speedups. We also show the draft model can be online trained with no prior trace collection and match the performance of offline training, making LEAP practical to deploy in the real world.

</details>


### [72] [RAOA: Alternating-Operator Neural Computation with Programmable Radio Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02683)
*Toshiaki Koike-Akino*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出无线电交替算子拟设（RAOA）递归计算架构，将可编程无线电传播用作计算深度，验证其在离散优化、可编程传播模拟和预训练模型适配中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索可编程无线电传播能否不仅作为通信信道或一次性模拟变换，而是作为可重复使用的计算深度。

Method: RAOA在持久潜状态上交替执行能量导出的问题更新与混合更新，每次混合后重新计算问题场，使重复传递具有组合性；通过精确离散优化、约束可编程传播模拟及预训练模型适配三类实验评估。

Result: 离散目标中重复执行可提升解质量且不增加可学习控制数量，并可直接处理高阶交互；无源纯相位自由空间模型表明所需算子可由可编程传播近似，虽有实现误差仍保留下游行为；作为零初始化残差适配器插入时，在三个模型家族上WikiText性能接近匹配的浅层MLP，但推理任务迁移依赖具体模型。

Conclusion: 结果将交替算子计算、可编程无线电传播与神经适配统一于一个递归框架，但射频实现证据基于仿真而非硬件演示。

Abstract: Can programmable radio propagation serve as computational depth rather than only as a communication channel or one-shot analog transform? We introduce the Radio Alternating Operator Ansatz (RAOA), a recurrent computing architecture that alternates an energy-derived problem update with a mixing update over a persistent latent state. Recomputing the problem field after each mix makes repeated passes compositional even when the same learned controls are reused across depth. We evaluate this idea through exact discrete optimization, constrained programmable-propagation simulation, and pretrained-model adaptation. On discrete objectives, repeated execution can improve solution quality without increasing the learned-control count, and the same formulation handles higher-order interactions directly. A passive phase-only free-space model further shows that the required operators can be approximated by programmable propagation while retaining useful downstream behavior despite realization error. When inserted as a zero-initialized residual adapter, RAOA adapts pretrained language models with WikiText performance close to a matched shallow MLP across three model families, while reasoning-task transfer remains model-dependent. Together, these results connect alternating-operator computation, programmable radio propagation, and neural adaptation within one recurrent framework. The RF realization evidence is simulation-based rather than a hardware demonstration.

</details>


### [73] [Test-time Calibration Learning for Large Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02695)
*Zizhuo Zhang,Xiong Peng,Jingwei Sun,Rong Yao,Shixiong Kai,Mingxuan Yuan,Bo Han*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出无标签测试时校准学习框架TTCL，在无真实标签的目标任务数据上同时适应推理准确率和口头置信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将校准学习融入强化学习的方法依赖真实正确性监督，在测试时无标签且需适应新目标任务的实际场景中不适用。

Method: TTCL从多个模型生成响应中推导正确性和校准的自监督信号，实现测试时无需真实标签的校准学习，并从理论上证明其是理想校准目标的有界代理。

Result: 在数学推理和事实问答上，TTCL在八个基准上使基础模型平均相对准确率提升40.13%、ECE降低70.80%；在域偏移下的数学到事实问答设置中平均相对准确率提升20.35%、ECE降低53.83%。

Conclusion: TTCL能跨模型和任务持续提升准确率与校准，并对已校准模型在域偏移下进一步改善，尤其当源域校准迁移效果差时。

Abstract: Reliable large language models (LLMs) must not only produce accurate answers but also express confidence that faithfully reflects their probability of being correct. Such calibration is essential for identifying uncertain predictions and supporting reliable decision-making in real-world deployment. Recent studies incorporate calibration learning into reinforcement learning (RL), jointly optimizing answer correctness and verbalized confidence using ground-truth correctness supervision. However, their reliance on labeled data limits their applicability in practical test-time settings, where ground-truth labels are unavailable and calibration may need to adapt to newly encountered target tasks. To address this challenge, we propose Test-Time Calibration Learning (TTCL), a label-free framework that jointly adapts reasoning accuracy and verbalized confidence directly on unlabeled target-task data. Specifically, TTCL derives self-supervision signals for both correctness and calibration from multiple model-generated responses, enabling calibration learning at test time without ground-truth labels. Theoretical analysis further establishes TTCL as a bounded surrogate for the ideal calibration objective. Extensive experiments on mathematical reasoning and factual question answering demonstrate that TTCL consistently improves both accuracy and calibration across diverse models and tasks. On base models, TTCL achieves an average relative accuracy improvement of +40.13% and an ECE reduction of +70.80% across eight benchmarks. Moreover, TTCL can further improve both accuracy and calibration for already calibrated models under domain shift, particularly when source-domain calibration transfers poorly to target tasks. In the math-to-factQA setting, TTCL achieves an average relative accuracy gain of +20.35% and reduces ECE by +53.83%. The source code is released at https://github.com/tmlr-group/TTCL.

</details>


### [74] [DyadMem: A Long-Term Memory Benchmark of How Agents Work with Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03020)
*Yifei Tao,Xinyu Zhong,Henry Hengyuan Zhao,Fanyi Wang,Tengda Guo,Wentao Qiu,Ying Wang,Liujian Tang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 论文提出DyadMem基准，定义用户条件关系型智能体记忆（URAM），通过多会话轨迹联合标注用户侧记忆与URAM，揭示现有模型在全流程记忆任务中的显著性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要监督用户事实、偏好或跨用户可复用经验，忽视了关系特定的智能体记忆；且仅用最终答案QA评估长交互历史，评估不完整、不可靠。

Method: 提出URAM新定义，构建DyadMem基准，沿同一多会话轨迹联合标注用户侧记忆和URAM，涵盖6类记忆；包含3,065个episode、50,961个会话、61,210个QA实例，提供会话级Capture与Update金标、查询级Recall支持，以及Gold-Memory和Full-Pipeline两种QA设置。

Result: 在16个开源权重和4个专有模型上，Gold-Memory QA表现 consistently 强，但Full-Pipeline QA急剧下降；定量结果揭示前沿LLM存在Capture召回低、Recall不完整和不安全删除问题；验证URAM有效性的严格实验对全部20个模型均观察到正向效果。

Conclusion: DyadMem是一个双域、全流程的记忆基准，通过大量标注工作推动该领域发展。

Abstract: Long-term agents must remember not only what is true about a user, but also how a particular agent should work with that user as their shared history evolves. Existing benchmarks primarily supervise user facts and preferences or experience reusable across users, leaving this relationship-specific agent memory implicit. Additionally, most prior works measure the model solely with final-answer QA over long interaction histories, making the assessment still incomplete and unreliable. To this end, we introduce DyadMem with the proposed new definition User-conditioned Relational Agent Memory (URAM). DyadMem jointly annotates user-side memory and URAM along the same multi-session trajectories, resulting in 6 memory categories. To summarize, it includes 3,065 episodes, 50,961 sessions, and 61,210 QA instances, with extensive session-level Capture and Update gold annotations, query-level Recall support, and two QA settings: Gold-Memory and Full-Pipeline. Across 16 open-weight and 4 proprietary models, Gold-Memory QA is consistently strong, yet Full-Pipeline QA drops sharply. Such a gap explicitly supports our fine-grained evaluation design. Additionally, several quantitative results further reveal low Capture recall, incomplete Recall, and unsafe-deletion issues arising from even the frontier LLMs. We further conduct a rigorous experiment to validate the effectiveness of our URAM and observe the positive effects for all 20 models. In summary, DyadMem is a dual-domain, full-pipeline memory benchmark with extensive annotation efforts for advancing the domain's development.

</details>


### [75] [Learning from Evolving Errors: Adaptive Iterative Repair for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02700)
*Rui Li,Liyang He,Zheng Zhang,Zhenya Huang,Linbo Zhu,Qi Liu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出AIR-OPD自适应迭代修复框架，为在线策略自蒸馏提供错误到修复的监督信号，避免参考解答条件捷径风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略自蒸馏虽提供稠密token级反馈，但教师依赖学生无法获取的完整参考解答，仅指定目标而未指明从当前错误到目标的路径，存在解答条件捷径风险。

Method: AIR-OPD在每轮中由引导生成器针对当前错误合成修复引导，学生据此进行在线策略重试；若仍错误，则针对新错误生成新引导。固定教师以引导为特权上下文，对学生最新失败响应的错误对齐区域进行监督。采用结果感知阶段加权，偏向早期修复阶段并奖励即时重试通过验证的阶段。

Result: 在DAPO-Math-17K训练，于AIME24、AIME25、HMMT25及分布外MMLU-Pro、GPQA评估。使用自引导和外部大模型引导两种来源。Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B均取得最佳数学推理平均成绩，较最强基线提升最多3.6分，同时保持分布外基准的基座模型性能。

Conclusion: AIR-OPD通过错误到修复的迭代监督有效提升数学推理能力，且不损害分布外泛化性能。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) supplies dense token-level feedback on trajectories sampled from the student's own policy, a richer training signal than the outcome-level rewards of reinforcement learning. This feedback comes from a teacher conditioned on a full reference solution unavailable to the student. The reference solution specifies the target but not how to move from the student's current error toward it, creating a solution-conditioned shortcut risk. We introduce AIR-OPD, an adaptive iterative repair framework for on-policy distillation that provides error-to-repair supervision. Given a failed response, a guidance generator synthesizes repair guidance for the current error. The student samples an on-policy retry with this guidance. If the retry remains incorrect, the generator produces new repair guidance for the newly observed error. At each round, a fixed teacher receives the guidance as privileged context and supervises the student on an error-aligned region of its latest failed response. Outcome-aware stage weighting favors early repair stages and credits stages whose immediate retry passes verification. We train AIR-OPD on the DAPO-Math-17K dataset and evaluate on AIME24, AIME25, and HMMT25, alongside out-of-distribution tests on MMLU-Pro and GPQA. We examine two guidance sources, self-guidance from the current student policy and external guidance from a larger model. For both Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B, AIR-OPD attains the best mathematical-reasoning averages, improving over the strongest baseline by up to 3.6 points, while preserving base-model performance on the out-of-distribution benchmarks.

</details>


### [76] [SoftGene: Protein Language Model-Enhanced Soft Prompting for Interpretable Gene Set Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03029)
*Drew Ross,Arya Hadizadeh Moghaddam,Dongjie Wang,Xiaoyu Zhang,Zijun Yao*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出SoftGene框架，利用蛋白质序列信息与层次化软提示增强LLM的基因集注释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM基因集分析方法多依赖符号基因名，忽略蛋白质序列等决定分子活性与互作的生物结构信息，且注释过程依赖人工 curation 与专家解读。

Method: 采用基于ESM蛋白质语言模型的层次注意力编码器表示基因集，构建混合提示方案：将基因集嵌入生成的软提示与LLM生成的辅助上下文硬提示结合，输入本地LLM进行注释。

Result: 在GO和MSigDB两个基准数据集上评估，整合蛋白质序列表示与文本上下文整体提升基因集注释效果，但不同生物结构域中蛋白质嵌入的贡献存在差异。

Conclusion: 蛋白质序列信息与文本上下文结合可有效改进LLM基因集注释，且其作用具有结构域特异性，需按域分析。

Abstract: Gene set analysis is a cornerstone of functional genomics, yet it remains labor-intensive and heavily dependent on manual curation and expert biological interpretation. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as powerful tools for genomic reasoning and annotation, most existing approaches rely on symbolic gene names and fail to capture domain-specific biological structure, particularly protein sequence information that governs molecular activity, interactions, and downstream gene function. In this work, we propose SoftGene, a novel framework for LLM-based gene set annotation that leverages the hierarchical structure of gene sets. First, we use a hierarchical attention-based encoder built on ESM, a protein language model, to represent each gene set using protein-level amino acid sequence information. Second, we construct a hybrid prompting scheme that combines soft prompts derived from gene set embeddings with hard prompts containing auxiliary context generated by an LLM, and feed the resulting prompt into a local LLM for annotation. We evaluate our framework on two benchmark datasets: Gene Ontology (GO) and the Molecular Signatures Database (MSigDB). Our results show that integrating protein-sequence representations with textual context improves gene set annotation overall, while per-domain analyses reveal that the contribution of protein embeddings varies across biological domains.

</details>


### [77] [MuonIO: Principled Norm-Aware Descent for Embedding Tables and Language Model Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02705)
*Linkai Ma,Xinyu Luo,Mengbo Wang,Ananth Grama,Petros Drineas,Brian Bullins*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出MuonIO，将Muon风格更新统一用于语言模型的嵌入表和输出头，替代AdamW。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Muon基于谱范数惩罚的线性化更新隐藏层，却对输入嵌入和输出头使用AdamW，缺乏统一原则性处理。

Method: 针对语言模型头L（V×d）采用2→∞算子范数（受softmax输出几何Lipschitz性启发），嵌入表E（d×V）采用1→2算子范数（基于one-hot输入几何），利用‖L‖_{2→∞}=‖L^T‖_{1→2}统一为同一词表导向几何，实现单一归一化向量规则：E列归一化、L行归一化。

Result: 在1B LLaMA于C4预训练中，相比Muon，MuonIO将I/O优化器状态内存减少50%、I/O更新FLOPs减少约46%，并提升验证困惑度。

Conclusion: MuonIO为嵌入与输出层提供统一且高效的Muon风格更新，在降低内存与计算开销的同时改善模型性能。

Abstract: The Muon optimizer derives its update rule for hidden linear layers by solving a local linearization of the loss penalized by the spectral norm, motivated by an RMS-stability argument for dense linear layers. Standard Muon implementations, however, exclude the input (embedding table) and output (language model head) layers from this principled treatment, for which they use AdamW instead. We present MuonIO, a single Muon-style update for both of these layers. For the language model head $\mathbf{L} \in \mathbb{R}^{V \times d}$, we motivate the use of the $2\to\infty$ operator norm, due to the Lipschitz continuity of the softmax output geometry, while for the embedding table $\mathbf{E} \in \mathbb{R}^{d \times V}$, we draw on the $1 \to 2$ operator norm, based on the one-hot input geometry identified by Bernstein & Newhouse (2025). The identity $\lVert\mathbf{L}\rVert_{2\to\infty}=\lVert\mathbf{L}^\top\rVert_{1\to2}$ then puts both matrices in the same vocabulary-oriented geometry: MuonIO applies a single normalized-vector rule, which appears as column normalization for $\mathbf{E}$ and row normalization for $\mathbf{L}$. Empirical evaluations demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, with MuonIO reducing I/O optimizer state memory by 50% and I/O update FLOPs by $\sim$46% compared to Muon for 1B LLaMA pretraining on C4, while also improving validation perplexity.

</details>


### [78] [MOF-VERIFY: A Failure-Aware Agentic Harness for MOF Hypothesis Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03056)
*Donghyun Lee,Taehoon Lee,Geonhee Ahn,Jieun Kim,Jihyun Park,Suyeon Cho,Yoona Kim,Chaerim Shin,Hoi Ri Moon,Jonggeol Na,Sukho Hong,Jihwan Oh,Soo Kyung Kim*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 论文提出MOF-Verify基准与失败感知智能体验证框架，用于诊断并改进大语言模型在MOF假设验证中的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型被广泛用作AI材料协同科学家中的推理组件，但其验证流程可靠性不明；MOF因标识符多样、合成条件依赖强、证据异质且部分假设需计算验证，构成极具挑战的验证场景。

Method: 构建包含结构定位、合成条件验证、证据充分性验证和基于MLIP计算验证四类任务的诊断基准；T-MOF-1-3在闭卷、检索增强和预言证据设置下评估以定位知识获取、证据获取和推理失败，T-MOF-4单独评估计算验证；据此开发MOF-Verify失败感知智能体框架，在给出最终判定前针对结构、文献、证据充分性和计算瓶颈进行处理。

Result: 在多个骨干大语言模型上，MOF-Verify相较直接推理和基于检索的基线显著提升了假设验证性能。

Conclusion: 诊断基准能有效定位MOF假设验证中的失败模式，失败感知的智能体验证框架可显著提升大语言模型在材料科学假设验证中的可靠性，基准数据集已开源。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as reasoning components in AI-driven materials Co-Scientists, yet the reliability of the resulting verification pipeline remains unclear. Metal-organic frameworks (MOFs) provide a particularly challenging setting because structures may appear under different identifiers, synthesis outcomes depend strongly on experimental conditions, evidence is distributed across heterogeneous sources, and some hypotheses require computation rather than literature alone. We introduce a diagnostic benchmark with four task families covering structural grounding, synthesis-condition verification, evidence-sufficiency verification, and MLIP-based computational verification. T-MOF-1-3 are evaluated under closed-book, retrieval-enabled, and oracle-evidence settings to localize failures in knowledge access, evidence acquisition, and reasoning, while T-MOF-4 separately evaluates computational verification. Guided by these diagnosed failure modes, we develop MOF-Verify, a failure-aware agentic harness that targets structural, literature, evidence-sufficiency, and computational bottlenecks before producing a final verdict. Across multiple backbone LLMs, MOF-Verify substantially improves hypothesis-verification performance over direct inference and retrieval-based baselines. Benchmark datasets are released at https://github.com/IMMS-Ewha/MOF-Verify-Benchmark.

</details>


### [79] [Prospective Hindsight: Self-Calibrating Reinforcement Learning via Prediction-Reality Gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02740)
*Jiaxin Zhang,Xiangyu Peng,Qinglin Chen,Yu Li,Hiroaki Hayashi,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出Prospective Hindsight（PH）自校准训练原则，通过比较动作前的预期预测与反馈后的回顾评估来生成惊讶信号，加权优势以放大模型自模型不准确样本的梯度贡献，从而同时提升任务性能与校准度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程强化学习仅依赖回顾性训练信号，信用分配在观察环境后果后才发生，导致动作时刻的信念对梯度不可见，且模型自校准往往需要额外目标。

Method: PH在任意回顾性基方法上增加基于预期预测与回顾评估之差的信号；用停止梯度的惊讶加权优势放大自模型最不准确样本的梯度；预期预测器与策略共享参数并共同演化；将原则联系到特权信息差距，并证明最小化惊讶残差可沿误校准率下降路径优化。

Result: 在单轮可验证任务和多轮个人代理任务中，基于GRPO、在线策略蒸馏及其组合，PH均提升任务性能和校准度，跨模型规模一致增益；主要误校准模式在单轮为过度自信失败、多轮为信心不足成功，但同一训练原则均有效。

Conclusion: PH通过自校准训练原则将校准作为优化的副产品而非额外目标，能有效识别并纠正智能体自模型盲点，跨越不同任务和模型规模实现性能与校准的双重提升。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for long-horizon agents relies on purely retrospective training signals: credit is assigned only after observing environmental consequences, leaving the agent's belief at action time invisible to the gradient. We introduce Prospective Hindsight (PH), a self-calibrating training principle that augments any retrospective base method with a signal derived from the gap between the agent's prospective prediction (before feedback) and the retrospective evaluation (after feedback). This per-rollout surprise identifies samples where the agent's self-model is most inaccurate and amplifies their gradient contribution through a stop-gradient surprise-weighted advantage. Since the prospective predictor shares parameters with the policy, the two co-evolve, progressively shifting focus to the agent's remaining blind spots. We connect this principle to a privileged-information gap and show that minimizing the surprise residual provides a descent pathway on the agent's miscalibration rate; calibration thus emerges as a byproduct of optimization rather than from an added objective. On single-turn verifiable tasks and a multi-turn personal-agent task (under GRPO, on-policy distillation, and their combination), PH improves both task performance and calibration, with consistent gains across model scales. Notably, the dominant miscalibration mode shifts structurally between regimes, overconfident failures in single-turn, underconfident successes in multi-turn, yet the same training principle addresses both successfully.

</details>


### [80] [Predictor-Guided Latent Space Codon Optimization for Maximizing Protein Expression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03098)
*Alberto Caron,Tianyu Cui,Dmytro S. Lituiev,Mangal Prakash,Artem Moskalev,Amina Mollaysa,Bo Zhai,Hirsh Nanda,Daniel M. Poole,Zhongyin Liu,Iman Farasat,Robert Davidson,Nikolay V. Manyakov,Tommaso Mansi,Scott Oloff,Rui Liao*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出LSCO方法，将密码子优化这一离散组合问题转化为预训练mRNA语言模型潜在空间中的连续优化问题，通过梯度搜索提升蛋白质表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密码子优化对治疗性蛋白生产和mRNA疫苗至关重要，但设计空间离散且组合巨大，无法使用梯度方法；现有工具依赖启发式代理指标（如CAI或GC含量），难以准确捕捉真实表达水平。

Method: LSCO将序列映射到预训练mRNA语言模型的潜在空间，实现基于梯度的搜索；整合四部分：基于不确定性感知预测器的数据驱动表达目标、最小自由能正则项、蛋白质到密码子回译模型的自然性先验、以及保证蛋白质保真度的约束解码。

Result: 在真实湿实验抗体表达数据集上，LSCO在预测表达方面优于简单频率基线和现代深度生成基线，同时保持合适的生物物理性质。

Conclusion: LSCO通过潜在空间连续优化有效解决了密码子优化的离散难题，在真实抗体数据上展现出优于现有方法的表达预测性能，并兼顾结构稳定性与蛋白质保真度。

Abstract: Codon optimization, the process of selecting synonymous codons to improve mRNA translation efficiency and protein expression, is central to therapeutic protein production and mRNA vaccines, yet it remains a hard problem. The design space is discrete and combinatorially large, precluding gradient-based methods, and existing tools rely on heuristic proxies (e.g., Codon Adaptation Index or GC-content) that poorly capture true expression. We introduce Latent-Space Codon Optimization (LSCO), which recasts this discrete problem as a continuous one by mapping sequences into the latent space of a pretrained mRNA language model, enabling efficient gradient-based search. LSCO combines four components: a data-driven expression objective from an uncertainty-aware predictor, a Minimum-Free-Energy regularizer for structural stability, a naturalness prior from a protein-to-codon back-translation model, and constrained decoding for protein fidelity. On a real-world, wet-lab antibody expression dataset, LSCO outperforms simple frequency-based, as well as modern deep generative baselines in predicted expression, while retaining suitable biophysical properties.

</details>


### [81] [Keeping JEPA World Models Plannable When Little of the Frame Moves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03137)
*Florian Strohm,Patrick Wagner,Jannik Schwab,Marco Huber*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 构建SLIM推物基准，发现LeWM世界模型因编码器潜在表示对动作不敏感而在语言目标规划中失败，用逆动力学辅助损失修复后大幅提升成功率，并支持语言目标序列规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言目标规划需要场景能区分多个物体，但现有基准缺乏此类测试；同时需诊断世界模型在语言目标下失败的原因。

Method: 提出SLIM推物基准，包含多个小物体及配对视觉/语言目标；使用LeWM世界模型，通过探针定位编码器失败；引入逆动力学辅助损失作用于编码器潜在和预测潜在（共享头测试时丢弃）；用廉价动作敏感性探针作为必要条件；在修复潜在上训练小型语言目标头。

Result: LeWM在SLIM上成功率<1%（脚本控制器100%）；探针显示潜在几乎动作不敏感，无法解码位置，rollout等同复制；辅助损失将成功率从0.003提至0.35（困难推物0.16），PushT在双倍训练视界下也提升；控制实验归因于编码器梯度；动作敏感性探针阈值以下均失败；语言目标头在导航达0.84（视觉目标oracle 1.00），跟随交换区域，泛化未见名词；单句推物成功低，但阶段句子序列将中/困难推物从0.04提至0.25，与目标框oracle相当，且阶段切换可从潜在读取。

Conclusion: 编码器动作不敏感是世界模型语言规划失败的关键，逆动力学辅助损失可修复潜在并提升规划；动作敏感性探针可作为经验必要条件；修复潜在支持语言目标头无需重训世界模型，阶段句子序列能有效完成复杂任务。

Abstract: Specifying a goal in language rather than as a goal frame is a natural interface for planning with a latent world model, but testing it needs scenes in which language must discriminate between several objects. We build SLIM, a pushing benchmark with several small objects and paired visual and language goals on identical scenes. On SLIM a LeWM world model that solves PushT succeeds on under 1% of trials, although a scripted controller with simulator state solves every tier. Probes locate the failure in the encoder: its latent is nearly action-insensitive, neither pusher nor object positions can be decoded from it, and rollouts are no better than copying the current latent forward. One inverse-dynamics auxiliary loss, applied to encoder latents and to predicted latents through a shared head discarded at test time, restores every probe and raises success from 0.003 to 0.35 (0.16 on the hard pushing tier, where a goal-agnostic policy scores zero), and improves PushT at twice the trained horizon. Controls attribute the repair to the gradient into the encoder, and a response sweep shows that the vanilla model plans once enough of the frame responds to actions. A cheap action-sensitivity probe, computable without environment access, acts as an empirical necessary condition: all configurations below its threshold failed to plan. On the repaired latent, a small language-goal head plans from sentences without retraining the world model: it reaches 0.84 on navigation (visual-goal oracle 1.00), follows the named zone when it is swapped with a decoy, and degrades gracefully to unseen nouns. A single goal sentence rarely completes a push, but given the push as a sequence of stage sentences the head raises success on the medium and hard pushing tiers from 0.04 to 0.25, on par with the goal-frame oracle, also when the switch between stages is read from the latent alone.

</details>


### [82] [A Controlled Audit of Personal AI Memory for Rating Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02764)
*Shivam Gupta*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文通过置换用户历史评分但保留评分分布等不变性的控制实验，检验个人AI在结构化评分预测中是否真正利用历史项目-评分关联，还是仅依赖用户评分倾向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究个人AI在评分预测中是否依赖历史项目-评分关联，而非仅利用用户评分倾向。

Method: 在Coat和MovieLens数据集上，对400个用户画像和6,160个目标评分进行冻结评估；通过置换用户内历史评分（保持分布、项目支持与元数据不变），结合完整历史、原生记忆提取和匹配的数值读取器；使用Qwen和Phi模型及Mem0管道，并采用bootstrap区间进行统计检验。

Result: 在Coat上，Qwen编写的Mem0管道相比完整历史，用户宏平均绝对误差分别增加0.084（Qwen）和0.149（Phi），家族调整bootstrap区间均排除零；MovieLens效应较小且在调整后不显著。仅历史岭回归读取器在两个域上优于Qwen，在MovieLens上优于Phi，而Coat上Phi比较未定。共保留2,800次读取调用（含150次无效输出），并采用固定回退规则。

Conclusion: 该可复现诊断研究表明，提取、关联使用、输出可靠性和读取器选择需要分别评估；正确历史分配在Coat上有帮助，但MovieLens效果不明确。

Abstract: In structured rating prediction, does a personal AI use historical item-rating associations, or mainly the user's rating tendencies? We audit this distinction by permuting historical ratings within each user while preserving the exact rating distribution, item support, and metadata. We combine this control with full history, native memory extraction, and matched numerical readers in a publicly frozen evaluation of 400 held-out user profiles and 6,160 target ratings across Coat and MovieLens. On Coat, the tested Qwen-written Mem0 pipeline increases user-macro mean absolute error relative to full history by 0.084 for Qwen and 0.149 for Phi; both family-adjusted bootstrap intervals exclude zero. Correct historical assignments help both readers on Coat, but the corresponding MovieLens effects are smaller and inconclusive after adjustment. A history-only ridge reader outperforms Qwen in both domains and Phi on MovieLens, while the Coat Phi comparison is unresolved. All 2,800 reader calls, including 150 invalid outputs, are retained under a fixed fallback rule. A separate implementation verifies inputs, metrics, and all ten primary contrasts. The contribution is a reproducible diagnostic study showing why extraction, association use, output reliability, and reader choice require separate evaluation.

</details>


### [83] [Exact Memory-Time Optimization for Prefix-Cached Language Model Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02766)
*Shivam Gupta*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出Prefix-Certificate Retention（PCR）精确有限轨迹建模，将带超时的前缀状态保留优化转化为最大权闭合问题，用最小割求解，并通过断点定理和动态规划验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保留语言模型前缀状态需在重计算与存储时间之间权衡；独立优化每个缓存块会高估收益，因为块可用性依赖于其前置前缀的可用性。

Method: 定义静态、分组、访问重置超时的精确有限轨迹PCR模型；将可用前缀奖励表示为以超时阈值和前序命中证书为前提的节点；最大权闭合归约为单个最小割，图规模与块查找和超时选择数线性相关；断点定理扩展到所有非负超时且无离散化误差；针对有序超时推导网格规模线性时间的动态规划及成本认证界。

Result: 小实例穷举与39,632个Mooncake请求的时序回放验证了模型；在固定网格上，有序超时在120个轨迹-分组-价格案例中的118个达到无限制训练最优；异构保留改善了若干留出内存-时间权衡，但更细粒度训练优化未一致提升迁移。

Conclusion: 贡献是一个可处理的优化模型与可审计的保留策略基准；实验测量可用前缀块和存储时间，而非GPU延迟。

Abstract: Retaining language-model prefix states trades recomputation against storage time. Optimizing each cached block independently can overcount savings: a resident block is usable only when the required preceding prefix is also available. We introduce Prefix-Certificate Retention (PCR), an exact finite-trace formulation for static, grouped, reset-on-access timeouts. Usable-prefix rewards become nodes whose prerequisites are timeout thresholds and preceding hit certificates. The resulting maximum-weight closure reduces to one minimum cut, with graph size linear in the number of block lookups and timeout choices. A breakpoint theorem extends the construction to all nonnegative timeouts without discretization error. We also derive a linear-time-in-grid-size dynamic program for ordered timeouts and bounds that certify the cost of this restriction. Exhaustive small-instance checks and chronological replay of 39,632 public Mooncake requests validate the formulation. On the fixed grid, ordered timeouts attain the unrestricted training optimum in 118 of 120 trace-grouping-price cases. Heterogeneous retention improves several held-out memory-time tradeoffs, but finer training optimization does not uniformly improve transfer. The contribution is a tractable optimization model and an auditable benchmark for retention policies; the experiments measure usable prefix blocks and storage time, not GPU latency.

</details>


### [84] [KV$^2$: A Self-Refining KV Cache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03198)
*Johannes Wesch,Danni Liu,Jan Niehues*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出KV$^2$，一种基于选择性重构的查询无关KV缓存压缩方法，先轻量代理评分筛选信息令牌，再仅对子集重构计算最终驱逐分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文模型中KV缓存内存占用大，尤其在一个预填充上下文需服务多个查询的可复用场景下，查询无关压缩需在成本与质量间权衡：轻量估计器便宜但不准，全上下文重构评分准确但需重处理整个提示。

Method: KV$^2$采用两阶段选择性重构：先用轻量代理评分器识别上下文中有信息的令牌，然后仅重新处理该子集以计算最终驱逐分数，避免全上下文重构。

Result: 在RULER、Needle-in-a-Haystack和LongBench上，预算越紧优势越大；RULER 16K、2% KV缓存预算下平均分比次优基线提升超40个百分点；LongBench在2%-10%预算下取得最高平均分，且压缩阶段运行时间和峰值内存低于全上下文重构。

Conclusion: 可复用KV缓存压缩无需重新处理完整上下文，选择性重构即可在更低成本下实现更优质量。

Abstract: The memory footprint of the key-value (KV) cache constrains the practical use of long-context models, and it dominates cost when one prefilled context must later serve many different queries. In this reusable setting, query-agnostic compression trades cost against quality: lightweight estimators are cheap but less accurate, whereas full-context reconstruction scoring is more accurate yet reprocesses the entire prompt. We introduce KV$^2$, a query-agnostic KV-cache compression method based on selective reconstruction. KV$^2$ first uses a lightweight proxy scorer to identify informative in-context tokens, then reprocesses only this subset to compute final eviction scores. On RULER, Needle-in-a-Haystack, and LongBench, KV$^2$'s margin over baselines widens as the budget tightens: on RULER 16K at a 2% KV-cache budget it improves the average score over the next-best baseline by more than 40 percentage points, and on LongBench it attains the highest average across 2%-10% budgets at lower compression-stage runtime and peak memory than full-context reconstruction. Reusable KV-cache compression thus does not require reprocessing the full context. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/KVsquared-0B97.

</details>


### [85] [Toward SLM-based agentic task-tool intent matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03213)
*Chiara Troiani,Arash Salarian,Majed El Helou,Benjamin Ryder,Jean Diaconu,Hervé Muyal,Marcelo Yannuzzi*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 该研究探讨使用小型语言模型（SLM）作为逐调用工具-任务相关性分类器，对AI代理的每次工具调用进行自动监督，并通过提示优化、监督微调和GRPO强化学习在新建的多工具MCP数据集上优化SLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具增强型AI代理通过工具调用访问数据和操作外部系统，代理系统的横向扩展增加了交互次数，需要低延迟、可本地部署的自动逐调用监督；传统授权方案只能判断代理是否被允许调用工具，无法评估其底层认知（工具选择是否为满足任务意图的逻辑相关步骤），因此即使被允许的调用仍可能偏离任务意图，每个调用都需验证。

Method: 将SLM用作任务-工具相关性分类器，独立评估每个被选工具与既定任务的相关性并输出相关性信号供下游执行；构建了包含多工具任务且所需工具跨越不同MCP服务器的新型数据集；采用提示优化、监督微调以及通过GRPO的强化学习来优化和专门化SLM。

Result: 摘要未明确给出具体实验结果或性能数值，仅说明使用提示优化、监督微调和GRPO强化学习对SLM进行了优化与专门化。

Conclusion: SLM可作为低延迟、可本地的逐调用任务-工具相关性分类器，为工具调用提供超越传统授权方案的认知层面监督，从而检测偏离任务意图的调用；所提数据集与优化方法为这一方向提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Tool-equipped AI agents use tool calls to access data and act on external systems. Horizontal growth of agentic systems increases the number of these interactions, and further motivates the need for automated, per-call oversight that can operate at low latency and/or on-prem. Conventional authorization schemes can determine whether an agent is allowed to invoke a tool, but cannot assess the agent's underlying cognition, specifically, whether the tool selection represents a logical, relevant step toward satisfying the intent of the task or not. Consequently, an allowed call may still deviate from the task's intent: a rogue agent might deviate the calls or nudge other agents to make a combination of calls that would not align with the intent of the task. Therefore, every call needs to be verified. In this study we investigate the applicability of Small Language Models (SLMs) to this purpose: an SLM functions as a task-tool relevance classifier that evaluates every selected tool independently against the assigned task and returns a relevance signal for downstream enforcement. Equipped with a novel dataset with multi-tool tasks whose required tools span distinct Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers, we used prompt-optimization, supervised fine-tuning, and reinforcement learning through GRPO to optimize and specialize SLMs.

</details>


### [86] [LatticeSMC: Where to Spend Inference-Time Compute in Chunked Sequence Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02774)
*Xuanchen Wang,Heng Wang,Weidong Cai*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出预算匹配的分块引导采样器LatticeSMC，在块索引与去噪步的二维格点上基于Feynman-Kac模型进行重采样，在音乐到舞蹈和文本到音乐生成任务中显著提升奖励指标并保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长序列生成器逐块生成，奖励定义于完整序列，但现有推理时引导方法（best-of-N、Feynman-Kac引导、流式剪枝）每次只作用于一个轴，且比较时计算预算或回报规则不一致，缺乏统一、预算匹配的引导框架。

Method: 将分块生成建模为块索引×去噪步的二维格点上的Feynman-Kac模型，提出LatticeSMC采样器。两个伸缩性结论保证其精确性：对块可加奖励，两轴诱导相同权重，故应在前瞻成本最低处重采样；对终端奖励，任意前缀得分可定义精确中间势能，使前缀可评估奖励成为无估计、无额外去噪调用的twist。LatticeSMC在块边界基于这些势能重采样，当评分免费时也在块内重采样，返回加权采样或最佳粒子。

Result: 在计算预算匹配条件下，于音乐到舞蹈扩散和40秒文本到音乐生成任务中，32粒子时节拍对齐从0.234提升至0.441（best-of-N为0.354），提示遵循从0.470提升至0.560，同时保持留出集质量。在长程奖励上仍保持优势，人类评估者在60-77%的成对比较中偏好该方法。

Conclusion: 承诺强度应跟随当前势能中的信息，而前瞻的价值可由未来奖励的集合内可预测性预测；LatticeSMC为分块长序列生成提供了预算匹配、精确且有效的推理时引导框架。

Abstract: Long-form generators for music, motion and video produce sequences chunk by chunk, with each chunk generated by iterative denoising while rewards are defined over the full sequence. Existing inference-time steering methods typically act on one axis at a time: best-of-N at the end, Feynman-Kac steering across denoising steps, or streaming pruning across chunks, and are often compared under unmatched compute or different return rules. We introduce budget-matched chunked steering and propose LatticeSMC, a sampler derived from a Feynman-Kac model on the two-dimensional lattice of chunk index and denoising step. Two telescoping results make its design exact: for chunk-additive rewards, the two axes induce identical weights, so resampling should occur where lookahead is cheapest; for terminal rewards, any prefix score defines an exact intermediate potential, making prefix-evaluable rewards twists with no estimation or extra denoiser calls. LatticeSMC resamples on these potentials at chunk boundaries and, when scoring is free, within chunks, returning either a weighted draw or the best particle. Under matched compute, on music-to-dance diffusion and 40-second text-to-music generation, it raises beat alignment from 0.234 to 0.441 (best-of-N: 0.354) and prompt adherence from 0.470 to 0.560 at 32 particles, while preserving held-out quality. It also retains its advantage on long-range rewards and is preferred by human raters in 60-77 percent of pairwise comparisons. Finally, we show that commitment strength should follow the information in the current potential, while the value of lookahead is predicted by the within-set predictability of future reward.

</details>


### [87] [EVOL: Simulator-Guided Evolutionary Expert Synthesis for Deployment-Free Learning Path Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03273)
*Geonwoo Bang,Dongho Kim,Moohong Min*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: EVOL将机器人学中的模拟器示范学习引入学习路径推荐，通过知识追踪模拟器进化搜索合成专家示范并蒸馏训练无部署依赖的策略，解决稀疏奖励与无专家路径两大难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习用于学习路径推荐面临两个耦合障碍：一是策略需无中间反馈地承诺长度为L的概念序列，组合搜索空间随L超指数增长且仅终步有奖励；二是稀疏奖励 RL 本可用专家路径缓解，但教育数据只记录学生实际行为而非理想路径，专家路径缺失。

Method: 借鉴机器人模拟器示范学习：用知识追踪模拟器经进化搜索为每个学习者合成专家示范，并训练免部署策略将示范蒸馏为前馈学习者；EVOL 采用非对称 actor-critic，actor 在部署现实条件下进行盲规划，critic 在训练时利用特权模拟器状态。

Result: 在 ASSIST15、Junyi、EdNet 三个数据集（39-189 个概念）及 L=5、10、20 下，EVOL 超越启发式、序列、RL、图增强 RL 和 LLM 增强等 8 个基线；比较 BC、AWR、DAPG 三种模仿策略，最终性能取决于进化专家质量而非具体模仿目标。

Conclusion: EVOL 通过模拟器合成专家示范并蒸馏为可部署前馈策略，有效克服稀疏奖励与无专家路径问题，性能优于多类基线；且模仿目标选择不关键，专家示范质量才是决定因素。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for learning path recommendation (LPR) faces two coupled obstacles. First, the policy must commit to a sequence of L concepts without intermediate feedback, producing a combinatorial search space that grows super-exponentially with L and provides reward only at the final step. Second, expert learning paths would be the natural cure for sparse-reward RL, but they do not exist in educational data, because student logs record what learners did, not what they should have done. We address both obstacles by importing a recipe from simulator-based demonstration learning in robotics: the knowledge tracing simulator is used both to synthesize per-learner expert demonstrations through evolutionary search and to train a deployment-free policy that distills these demonstrations into a feed-forward learner. Our framework, EVOL, instantiates this pipeline with an asymmetric actor-critic where the actor commits to deployment-realistic blind planning while the critic exploits the privileged simulator state during training. Across three datasets (ASSIST15, Junyi, and EdNet; 39-189 concepts) and path lengths L = 5, 10, and 20, EVOL surpasses 8 baselines spanning heuristic, sequential, RL, graph-enhanced RL, and LLM-enhanced methods. We further compare three imitation strategies (BC, AWR, and DAPG) and show that final performance is governed by the quality of evolutionary experts rather than by the particular imitation objective.

</details>


### [88] [JOVE: Joint Execution and Verification for Resource-Aware LLM Task Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03296)
*Haoran Zhang,Dongjun Kim,Seohyeon Cha,Kevin S Chan,Ananthram Swami,Gustavo De Veciana,Haris Vikalo*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出JOVE在线框架，在预算和时延约束下联合分配异构LLM执行器并选择中间输出进行付费验证，实现精度与成本/时延的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂推理查询可分解为有向无环任务图并分配到异构LLM，但LLM对子任务的适用性先验未知，且执行结果无法直接判断正确性，因此需在长期预算和单查询时延约束下平衡当前执行开销与后续学习收益。

Method: JOVE将执行与验证决策建模为逐查询混合整数线性规划序列；验证异步进行并反馈以更新任务相关的LLM质量估计；引入信息增益奖励将学习价值纳入分配决策；在合理假设下证明质量学习的次线性遗憾。

Result: 在四个推理基准上，JOVE达到与标准推理基线相当的准确率，同时将平均成本和时延至少降低3.17倍。

Conclusion: JOVE通过在线联合分配LLM与选择性付费验证，在预算和时延约束下有效平衡执行与学习，显著降低成本和时延且保持竞争力准确率。

Abstract: Complex reasoning queries can be decomposed into directed acyclic task graphs and distributed across heterogeneous LLMs, reducing latency through parallelism and enabling smaller models to solve complex tasks. In practice, however, the suitability of an LLM for a given subtask may be a priori unknown, and execution alone does not reveal output correctness. We propose JOVE, an online framework that jointly assigns executor LLMs and selects intermediate outputs for paid verification. Verification runs asynchronously and is used to improve future allocations, so the system must balance spending on execution now against learning for later. We study how to optimize this trade-off under a long-term budget and a per-query latency constraint, with stochastic, initially unknown LLM service quality, invocation costs, and execution times. JOVE makes execution and verification decisions by solving a sequence of per-query mixed-integer linear programs. Online learning updates task-dependent estimates of LLM quality based on verification feedback, while an information-gain bonus incorporates the value of learning into allocation decisions. Under a natural set of assumptions, we establish sublinear quality-learning regret for JOVE. Across four reasoning benchmarks, JOVE achieves competitive accuracy against standard inference baselines while reducing average cost and latency by at least 3.17 times.

</details>


### [89] [Optimal Planning in a Dynamic World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03312)
*Devin Wild Thomas,Solomon Eyal Shimony,Wheeler Ruml,Erez Karpas,Shahaf S. Shperberg,Andrew Coles*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 该论文研究未知开始时间下的动态规划问题，提出任意开始时间规划框架，利用复合到达时间函数（cATF）紧凑表示最优计划与开始时间的函数关系，并基于启发式图搜索传播函数而非标量代价来构建cATF。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实动态环境中可行性随时间变化，且执行开始时间常未知，而现有规划方法多忽略动态性或假设开始时间已知，导致不适用。

Method: 定义任意开始时间规划设置，提出复合到达时间函数（cATF）作为数据结构，并给出基于启发式图搜索的通用规划算法，通过沿边传播函数来组装cATF。

Result: 证明cATF大小最多与问题规模成线性关系；在SIPP问题上的实验表明，依赖重规划的方法在困难问题上常失败，而使用cATF的任意开始时间算法能在获知开始时间后快速查找最优计划。

Conclusion: 通过支持时间相关计划的高效表示与推理，为动态世界中的规划奠定了基础。

Abstract: Background: We address the problem of planning when the set of feasible states or actions changes over time. For example, in the problem of path planning among moving obstacles (sometimes known as SIPP), the feasibility of being at a particular location can change as the obstacles move. Or, the action of boarding a particular train is feasible only while it is stopped at the station. This dynamism means that the optimal plan and its duration can change depending on when execution begins. In practice, execution start time is often unknown until planning has completed or another agent gives the go-ahead. However, most prior planning work either ignores dynamism or assumes a known start time. This makes it straightforward to assess state and action feasibility but is impractical for some applications. Objectives: In this paper, we relax the assumption of a known start time. We define the setting of {\em any-start-time planning} and provide algorithms for it. Methods: We present a data structure called a compound arrival time function (cATF) that compactly encodes the optimal plan as a function of start time. We provide general-purpose planning algorithms, based on heuristic graph search, that assemble cATFs by propagating functions along edges instead of scalar costs. Results: We prove that the size of a cATF is at most linear in the problem size. An experimental evaluation of an implementation for the specific problem of SIPP shows that, on difficult problems, agents that rely on replanning often fail, while any-start-time algorithms using cATFs can quickly look up the optimal plan once the execution start time is known. Conclusions: By enabling efficient representations and reasoning for time-dependent plans, this work provides a foundation for planning in dynamic worlds.

</details>


### [90] [Efficient Memory Crystallization for Graph Learning under Non-Stationary Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02795)
*Yue Hou,Ruomei Liu,Yingke Su,Junran Wu,Ke Xu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出免训练测试时框架EMC，通过闭式解将图域压缩为紧凑记忆，实现高效持续图适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖辅助生成模块合成记忆图，计算开销大且难以应对长期分布漂移。

Method: EMC基于面向记忆的分布匹配目标闭式解蒸馏每个图域为语义忠实的紧凑记忆，并通过状态演化记忆建模域间依赖。

Result: 在非平稳分布漂移图上性能优于最先进基线，平均运行时间降低87.4%，GPU内存消耗降低92.4%。

Conclusion: EMC为大规模持续图适应提供了实用且经济高效的解决方案，兼具泛化性与适应性。

Abstract: Deep graph learning models deployed in real-world systems often need to cope with non-stationary environments, where the underlying graph distribution drifts continually over time. Prevailing solutions rely on training auxiliary generative modules to synthesize memory graphs for cross-domain adaptation, which incurs substantial computational overhead and scales poorly under prolonged distribution shifts. We argue that a more economical path exists: rather than generating memory, one can crystallize it. To this end, we propose Efficient Memory Crystallization (EMC), a training-free test-time framework that distills each incoming graph domain into a compact, semantically faithful memory through a closed-form solution to a memory-oriented distribution-matching objective, thereby eliminating redundant domain information under continual covariate shifts. To preserve both generalizability and adaptability as the model traverses a long sequence of target domains, EMC further models inter-domain dependencies through state-evolving memories and admits a theoretically grounded, tighter generalization error bound than direct adaptation. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superior performance of EMC over state-of-the-art baselines on graphs under non-stationary distribution shifts, while reducing average runtime by 87.4% and GPU memory consumption by 92.4% relative to the recent competitor, making continual graph adaptation practical at scale.

</details>


### [91] [Lightweight, Rubric-Guided Trajectory Evaluation for Production AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03315)
*Linh-An Phan,MingXue Wang,Guangyu Wu,Feng Pan,Zhaoyu Pang,Yanbin Zhang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出轻量级轨迹评估架构LiteTrajEval，在有限预算下高效诊断LLM代理失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中重复运行轨迹评估成本高昂，且长轨迹中并非所有原始token都对诊断有用。

Method: 离线推导领域特定规则配置，在线预处理轨迹、标记启发式失败信号、在固定全局预算下序列化，并调用单次 rubric 引导的LLM评判器生成结构化诊断报告。

Result: 在Magentic-One和τ-bench风格数据集上，失败定位对齐人类标注比AgentRx提升约20-35个百分点（Magentic-One）和最多23个百分点（τ-retail），成本降低约6倍，评估时间减少8倍以上。

Conclusion: LiteTrajEval已部署于企业代理平台，实现预算受限下的高效准确轨迹评估。

Abstract: Trajectory evaluation is essential for improving the reliability of LLM-based agents, but production use makes it expensive to run repeatedly. Modern agents generate long traces containing tool calls, observations, retries, and external outputs, while not all raw tokens are equally useful for diagnosis. We present \textit{LiteTrajEval}, a lightweight architecture for budget-bounded trajectory evaluation. LiteTrajEval derives compact domain-specific rule profiles offline, then preprocesses each trajectory online, marks heuristic failure signals, serializes it under a fixed global budget, and invokes a single rubric-guided LLM judge to produce structured diagnostic reports. Evaluated on public Magentic-One-style and $τ$-bench-style trajectory datasets, LiteTrajEval improves failure-localization alignment with human annotations by roughly 20--35 percentage points on Magentic-One and up to 23 percentage points on $τ$-retail compared with AgentRx, while reducing cost by about 6$\times$ and evaluation time by more than 8$\times$. This solution has also been deployed in our enterprise agentic platform.

</details>


### [92] [Gated Slot Attention-2: Two-Sided Associative Memory Correction in Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02816)
*Ruijie Li,Shengnan Ding,Weimin Zhang,Derick Tang,Zhanpeng Zeng,Qinsong Zeng,Ming Chen,Jiaxi Hu,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 该论文提出Gated Slot Attention-2（GSA2），通过结合键侧校正的Gated Oja Rule-2和值侧校正的Gated Delta Rule-2，并共享潜在槽位，改进了线性注意力模型的固定大小循环记忆管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力模型虽高效，但其固定大小的循环记忆管理仍具挑战。现有工作分别探索了delta规则变体（精确校正键关联的值）和槽位架构（如Gated Slot Attention，两阶段建模键和值记忆），但这两条方向尚未被有效结合。

Method: 作者观察到delta规则提供有效记忆校正，两阶段结构提供关联两侧的自然操作方式，二者互补。据此提出Gated Oja Rule用于键侧校正，并扩展为带解耦擦除和写入控制的Gated Oja Rule-2。进而提出GSA2，通过共享潜在槽位将Gated Oja Rule-2（键侧校正）与Gated Delta Rule-2（值侧校正）结合。还推导了硬件高效的分块算法以支持并行训练。

Result: 实验表明，GSA2在多个基准上持续优于强线性注意力基线，同时保持线性时间序列建模和常数内存的循环解码。

Conclusion: GSA2通过融合键侧delta规则校正与两阶段槽位架构，有效提升了线性注意力模型的记忆管理能力，在保持效率优势的同时实现了性能改进。

Abstract: Linear attention models have emerged as efficient alternatives to standard attention, but effectively managing their fixed-size recurrent memory remains challenging. To improve memory, recent work has explored two distinct directions: delta-rule variants for precise correction of values associated with keys, and slot-based architectures such as Gated Slot Attention for modeling key and value memories in two stages. We observe that these directions are complementary--the delta rule provides effective memory correction, while the two-stage structure provides a natural way to operate on both sides of an association. Building on this insight, we introduce a new Gated Oja Rule for key-side correction and extend it with decoupled erase and write control to obtain Gated Oja Rule-2. We then introduce Gated Slot Attention-2 (GSA2), which combines Gated Oja Rule-2 for key-side correction with Gated Delta Rule-2 for value-side correction through shared latent slots. We further derive a hardware-efficient chunkwise algorithm for parallel training. Experiments demonstrate that GSA2 consistently improves over strong linear-attention baselines across benchmarks while retaining linear-time sequence modeling and constant-memory recurrent decoding.

</details>


### [93] [Refinement Buys Intelligibility, Search Buys Identity: What Test-Time Compute Buys in Masked-Diffusion TTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03320)
*Nityanand Mathur,Hamees Sayed,Ayush Pratap Singh*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 研究深度（参数量）与精炼步数（推理预算）在扩散TTS中是否等效缩放，发现二者针对不同瓶颈，需分别优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型TTS同时依赖模型深度和精炼步数，但尚不清楚二者是否能等同地提升不同能力（如可懂度与说话人身份）。

Method: 训练15个掩码扩散编解码TTS模型（19–133M参数，3个随机种子），使用2000小时语音，推理时扫描精炼步数T∈[1,16]；在174个留出说话人上以ASR词错误率衡量可懂度、以说话人验证衡量身份，并拟合可分离B(d)B(T)模型、进行Best-of-K搜索及编解码器分析。

Result: 精炼步数可弥合86.2%的可懂度差距，但仅弥合46.4%的身份差距（1.86倍不对称性，跨多种误差指标稳健）；3倍与6倍训练计划将差距从1.84降至1.36、1.23倍但不反转；Best-of-K搜索在四个独立编码器上取得64.6–79.0%的胜率；可分离模型显著优于替代模型（ΔAICc=+69.3）；62%的剩余身份缺陷来自编解码器而非生成器。

Conclusion: 精炼步数与模型深度针对不同的性能瓶颈，不应相互替代，而应分别优化。

Abstract: Diffusion language models for text-to-speech combine two forms of computation: model depth (parameters) and refinement steps (inference budget). We ask whether they scale equally across capabilities. We train 15 masked-diffusion codec TTS models varying depth (19-133M parameters, 3 seeds) on 2,000 hours of speech and sweep refinement steps T in [1,16] at inference, measuring zero-shot synthesis via ASR word error rate (intelligibility) and speaker verification (identity) on 174 held-out speakers. Against measured floors, refinement closes 86.2% of the intelligibility range but only 46.4% of the identity range - a 1.86x asymmetry robust across multiple error metrics. Retraining at 3x and 6x schedule attenuates but does not reverse this gap (1.84 to 1.36 to 1.23x), because intelligibility saturates with steps while identity continues improving. Best-of-K search recovers speaker identity where refinement fails, with 64.6-79.0% win rates across four independent encoders. Depth and steps are not interchangeable: separable B(d)B(T) fits significantly better (Delta AICc=+69.3) than substitution models. Analysis shows 62% of remaining identity deficit lies in the codec, not the generator. We conclude that refinement and depth target different bottlenecks and should be optimized separately.

</details>


### [94] [Preserving Mathematical Reasoning in Compressed Diffusion Language Models via Trajectory-Aware Low-Rank Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03326)
*Tian Liang,Zishan Shao,Yiran Chen*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 针对扩散语言模型（dLLM）压缩中校准状态与推理时部分掩码中间状态不匹配的问题，提出轨迹感知的低秩压缩目标及高效估计方法Traj-MC，在相同压缩预算下显著提升重建质量并更好地保持数学推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dLLM压缩通常在干净、完全可见的激活上校准，而推理却遍历部分掩码的中间状态，导致低秩压缩的低秩最优性刻画不准确，且校准状态选择可能影响压缩后模型的数学推理保持能力。

Method: 将低秩目标建模为在损坏程度和掩码实现上的轨迹感知目标；提出Traj-MC，通过蒙特卡洛采样估计轨迹二阶矩，并证明其具有精确的采样状态最优性和总体一致性。

Result: 在匹配压缩预算下，轨迹感知校准相比干净校准能更好重建生成轨迹，并在数学推理基准上显著保留更多数学推理能力。

Conclusion: 轨迹感知低秩最优性与dLLM压缩后保留的推理能力之间存在联系，表明校准状态的选择对压缩后数学推理保持至关重要。

Abstract: Diffusion language model (dLLM) compression faces a known challenge because calibration is typically performed on clean, fully visible activations, whereas inference traverses partially masked intermediate states. For low-rank compression, this raises two questions. First, can low-rank optimality still be characterized when approximation quality is measured over trajectory-distributed states, and second, does the choice of calibration states affect mathematical reasoning preservation under compression? We address these questions by formulating a trajectory-aware low-rank objective over corruption levels and masking realizations. To estimate this objective efficiently, we propose Traj-MC, which estimates the trajectory second moment through Monte Carlo sampling and yields exact sampled-state optimality and population consistency. Under matched compression budgets, trajectory-aware calibration improves reconstruction over the generation trajectory and preserves substantially more mathematical reasoning than clean calibration on mathematical reasoning benchmarks. Our results connect trajectory-aware low-rank optimality to the reasoning capability retained after dLLM compression. Our code is available at: https://github.com/Zishan-Shao/traj-mc.git.

</details>


### [95] [Understanding Enrichment in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02846)
*Jinwoo Kim,Shraddha Barke*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文从数学上分析了RLVR中利用提示或中间引导丰富rollout时，省略或截断重要性权重校正的后果，并提出一种SMC权重校正机制以缓解方差累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在奖励稀疏时，RLVR依赖提示或中间引导生成更多成功rollout，但这会引入分布偏差；现有方法忽略或截断重要性权重校正，导致不清楚校正究竟带来什么收益与损失。

Method: 数学证明省略或截断校正等价于隐式重新加权奖励，并将梯度误差分解为尺度、旋转和方差；提出基于序贯蒙特卡洛的权重校正机制，在稳定性和粒子序假设下将标准校正方差的指数累积缓和为加性累积；并将分析用于解释Qwen3-1.7B在OpenMathReasoning稀疏带上的微调结果。

Result: 理论分解阐明了不同误差对学习的影响；SMC校正机制在假设下将方差从指数增长转为加性增长；案例表明校正与未校正的丰富机制均有助于避免稀疏域中的崩溃。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是理解丰富与校正如何一般性地影响训练，而非断言某种操作优于未丰富的RL。

Abstract: When rewards are sparse, reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) often uses hints or intermediate guidance to generate more successful rollouts. This enrichment biases policy-gradient updates unless corrected via importance weights, but existing methods omit correction or truncate importance weights in order to avoid the high variance of correction. It thus remains unclear what exactly is gained or lost in RLVR by correcting enriched rollouts. We show, mathematically, that omitted or truncated correction implicitly reweights the defined reward, and we decompose the resulting gradient error into scale, rotation, and variance to explain their distinct effects on learning. To make correction practical, we develop a novel sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) weight correction mechanism that, under stability and particle-order assumptions, tempers the exponential compounding of standard correction variance over the length of a sample to an additive accumulation. We then apply our analysis of enrichment to interpreting the results of a fine-tune of Qwen3-1.7B on a sparse band of OpenMathReasoning, establishing a concrete mechanism of how enrichment, both corrected and uncorrected, helps avoid collapse in sparse domains. Our main contribution is to understand, in general, how enrichment and correction can affect training, as opposed to claiming that either mode of operation is superior to unenriched RL.

</details>


### [96] [Multilingual GSM-Symbolic: What determines capability transfer across languages?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03367)
*Kenneth Enevoldsen,Riley Herchert,Sofie Mosegaard,Dan Saattrup Smart,Simon Enni,Isaac Chung,Sofie Bruun,Ayush Sunil Munot,Max Müller-Eberstein,Adnan El-Assadi,Elisa Bassignana,Gianluca Barmina,Hafsteinn Einarsson,Iben Nyholm Debess,Linda Freienthal,Lukas Galke Poech,Mike Zhang,Nicolas Legrand,Vladimir Salnikov,Yevhen Kostiuk,Zafar Hussain,Sagandeep Kaur,Agnes Toftgård,Marie Mattson,Kristoffer Nielbo*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出多语言数学数据集Multilingual GSM-Symbolic，量化跨语言能力迁移的决定因素，发现模型规模、语言资源、推理和类型距离为主要影响因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前对能力如何从一种语言迁移到另一种语言了解甚少，评估依赖不可比且易饱和的数据集，且很少联合检验决定因素；识别迁移预测因素可避免穷举评估，帮助开发者针对低资源语言性能限制因素。

Method: 构建Multilingual GSM-Symbolic，涵盖30,000个匹配问答对、15种语言，利用符号模板防止过拟合并生成海量高质量变体；联合估计模型规模、语言资源水平、推理和类型距离等决定因素。

Result: 模型规模(β=1.77)、语言资源(β=0.77)、推理(β=0.67)和类型距离(β=-0.25)是最大决定因素；32B模型在马拉地语表现相当于10B英语模型；模型规模和推理缩小低高资源语言差距(β=-0.27和-0.20)，但对类型远语言效果甚微；框架解释92%语言间变异，仅23%模型-语言变异，预测未见语言性能误差6.0pp(r=.96)，加入10个目标语言模板降至4.19pp。

Conclusion: 模型规模和推理可缩小低高资源语言性能差距，但对类型远语言无效；该框架能有效预测跨语言性能，少量目标语言数据即可提升估计精度，对模型开发者有重要启示。

Abstract: We understand little about how capabilities acquired in one language carry over to another, or what governs this transfer: evaluations rely on incomparable, saturation-prone datasets and rarely examine its determinants jointly. Identifying what predicts transfer would let us avoid exhaustive evaluation across all language pairs and let developers target the factors that limit performance in low-resource languages. To evaluate cross-lingual capability transfer, we introduce Multilingual GSM-Symbolic, an extensible multilingual mathematical dataset covering 30,000 item-matched question-answer pairs and spanning 15 languages. It utilises symbolic templates to prevent overfitting and ensure generalisation by allowing generation of millions of high-quality variations from a single sample. Using Multilingual GSM-Symbolic, we quantify the largest determinants of capability as model size ($β= 1.77$), language resource level ($β= 0.77$), reasoning ($β= 0.67$) and typological distance ($β= -0.25$). This joint estimation allows these determinants to be expressed in terms of one another: a 32B model evaluated in Marathi performs like a 10B model in English. Our findings have important implications for model developers, showing that model size and reasoning narrow the performance gap between low- and high-resource languages ($β= -0.27$ and $β= -0.20$, respectively), while similar levers have little or no effect on typologically distant languages.
  Overall, our analysis framework explains 92% of between-language variation, but only 23% of the model-by-language variation, and predicts a model's performance on an unseen language within 6.0pp (r=.96). Incorporating measurements from just 10 templates in the target language reduces this to 4.19pp, enabling reasonable estimates of performance with little or no downstream dataset.

</details>


### [97] [NeuroLens: Learning Latent Embeddings of Neural Semantics from Chronic Recordings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02864)
*Hanrui Lyu,Baiyuan Chen,Tianshu Tan,Matthew R. Whiteway,Maxwell D. Melin,Ji Xia,Linyang He,Bradly C. Stadie,Anne Churchland,Liam Paninski,Yizi Zhang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出NeuroLens自监督模型，基于JEPA学习慢性神经记录的降噪语义潜表征，提升解码稳定性与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分析工具难以区分慢性神经记录中的表征可塑性与记录不稳定性，亟需能分离二者的方法。

Method: NeuroLens采用联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA），用自适应编码器将变化的神经元群体映射到公共潜空间，并用时间预测器学习可预测的未来潜状态结构，从而在潜空间预测中捕捉时间可预测结构、降低对瞬时记录特异变异的敏感性。

Result: 在小鼠和人类慢性皮层内数据上，学习到的表征改善了对决策和语义任务变量的解码；多天预训练可泛化到未来会话，实现对新神经群体的快速少样本适应，且解码稳定性优于当前最优基线。

Conclusion: NeuroLens为研究学习过程及长时间尺度上神经表征如何变化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Understanding how neural activity represents higher-order cognition and how these representations evolve over time has long been a central pursuit in neuroscience. However, current analytical tools cannot easily distinguish representational plasticity from recording instability in chronic neural recordings. Here, we introduce NeuroLens (Latent Embeddings of Neural Semantics), a self-supervised model based on the Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) framework that learns denoised, semantically informative latents from chronic neural recordings. An adaptive encoder maps changing neural populations into a common latent space, while a temporal predictor learns structure that supports prediction of future latent states. By predicting in latent space, NeuroLens captures temporally predictable structure and reduces sensitivity to transient, recording-specific variability. Across chronic intracortical data in mice and humans, the learned representations improve decoding of decision-making and semantic task variables. Multi-day pretraining enables generalization to future sessions, rapid few-shot adaptation to unseen neural populations, and more stable decoding over time than state-of-the-art baselines. Together, these results establish NeuroLens as a new paradigm for studying how neural representations change during learning and over long timescales.

</details>


### [98] [Distributionally Robust Survival Models under Subpopulation Shift and Outlier Contamination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02868)
*Seonghwi Kim,Sung Ho Jo,Minwoo Chae*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种分布鲁棒生存分析框架，联合应对潜在亚群偏移与离群点污染，通过外最小化与内最大化交替优化，稳定训练并提升最差组性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异质人群中，平均表现好的生存模型可能在特定亚群上表现差，且训练数据受离群点污染时问题更严重，现有方法难以同时处理亚群偏移与离群污染。

Method: 构建外最小化选择精炼名义分布以降低污染样本影响、内最大化聚焦最具挑战亚群的极小极大优化框架；该框架直接适配非可分解生存损失并保留样本间交互（如Cox负偏对数似然的风险集结构）；基于内最大化KKT条件推导外更新，开发交替梯度算法。

Result: 在模拟数据和两个生存分析基准上，当亚群偏移与离群污染同时发生时，方法保持鲁棒；在污染设置中稳定训练，显著提升线性与非线性生存模型的最差组性能，同时整体性能具有竞争力甚至更优。

Conclusion: 所提分布鲁棒框架能有效联合处理亚群偏移与离群污染，提升最差组鲁棒性且不牺牲整体性能，适用于线性与非线性生存模型。

Abstract: Learning robust survival models under distribution shift is an important but challenging problem in many applications. In heterogeneous populations, a model that performs well on average may still perform poorly on certain subpopulations, and this issue becomes even more severe when the training data are contaminated by outliers. In this paper, we propose a novel distributionally robust framework for survival analysis that jointly addresses latent subpopulation shift and outlier contamination. The proposed method combines an outer minimization that selects a refined nominal distribution by reducing the influence of contaminated samples and an inner maximization that focuses on the most challenging subpopulation. This formulation directly accommodates non-decomposable survival losses while preserving interactions across samples, including the risk-set structure of the Cox negative partial log-likelihood. We develop an alternating gradient-based algorithm with outer updates derived from the KKT conditions of the inner maximization. Experiments on simulated data and two survival benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed method remains robust when subpopulation shift and outlier contamination occur simultaneously. It stabilizes training in contaminated settings and substantially improves worst-group performance across both linear and nonlinear survival models, while maintaining competitive and sometimes superior overall performance.

</details>


### [99] [A Near-Zero Monitor Readout Is Not Evidence of Behavioral Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03458)
*Zhe Zhou,Tianhua Tao*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 验证奖励下的后训练会诱发奖励黑客，低监控读数不能证明行为受控；同一零中位数训练分数下，种子不同可导致从低黑客比例到近乎纯黑客的混合状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线审计中监控读数低常被当作行为受控的证据，但该假设缺乏验证；作者旨在检验低监控读数能否识别训练目标中的监控干预是否真正控制行为。

Method: 在代码生成环境中，主要漏洞在推理轨迹开始时即存在；针对同一离线门槛下通过的三类监控（域内激活探针、两种基于策略多早承诺最终答案的惩罚）训练策略，并比较不同种子与训练方式下的端点监控读数与行为表现，辅以文本级分析。

Result: 探针分数从首个训练步即处于数值下限，所有前缀训练的端点训练分数中位数为零；但固定配置下，零中位数分数对应的行为可因种子从混合低黑客比例到近乎纯奖励黑客。探针低读数源于验证位置与训练读取位置不匹配；文本分析发现前缀失败模式：通用规划与填充外壳将漏洞推迟到截断点之后而未从最终输出中消除。

Conclusion: 低测量承诺无法区分低黑客比例与延迟承诺漏洞；离线判别与低监控对齐读数不足以证明行为受控，必须进行带外行为检查；本文仅刻画端点读数而非其演化。

Abstract: Post-training with verifiable rewards can induce reward hacking, motivating the use of monitors within the training objective rather than solely for offline auditing. We show that a low monitor readout does not identify whether such an intervention controls behavior. In a code-generation environment whose dominant exploit is available at the start of the reasoning trace, we train policies against three monitors that pass the same offline gate: an in-domain activation probe and two penalties conditioned on how early the policy commits to its own final answer. The probe score is at its numerical floor from the first recorded training step, and the trained-score median is zero for every prefix-trained run at the endpoint. These readouts estimate different quantities, and we do not compare their scales; within each monitor family, however, low values do not establish behavioral control. Within one fixed configuration, prefix-trained runs with the same zero-median trained score range, by seed alone, from a mixed regime with a low hacking share to near-pure reward hacking. All probe runs reach the hacking regime, but their floor-level readout reflects a mismatch between the position where the probe was validated and the position where it was read during training, not a second instance of this ambiguity. Text-level analysis identifies a prefix failure mode: generic planning and filler shells postpone the exploit past the cut without eliminating it from the final output. Low measured commitment therefore does not distinguish a low hacking share from delayed commitment to the exploit. Offline discrimination and low monitor-aligned readouts are insufficient evidence of behavioral control; an out-of-band behavioral check is required. We characterize the endpoint readout, not its evolution. Code is available at https://github.com/zhezhou1106/spoof-cost.

</details>


### [100] [Efficient Reasoning Training Does Not Always Harm CoT Faithfulness and Monitorability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03509)
*Samuel Lewis-Lim,Xingwei Tan,Mario Sanger,Zhixue Zhao,Nikolaos Aletras*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 研究不同长度压力方法对思维链忠实性与可监控性的影响，发现忠实性下降而可监控性稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 思维链推理成本高，需训练模型用更少token解题，但担忧跳过关键步骤导致思维链不再忠实反映决策。

Method: 用三种长度压力方法（固定生成预算、每样本长度目标、组相对长度奖励）微调多种模型，评估对忠实性和可监控性的影响。

Result: 忠实性在多数设置下下降，主要因模型一致性降低；可监控性更稳健，即使思维链大幅缩短，模型仍能承认输入干预对答案的影响。

Conclusion: 效率推理对忠实性和可监控性影响不同，可监控性对长度压力更鲁棒。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning allows humans to inspect how large language models reach their answers, and oversee model behaviour. This reasoning comes at an increased inference cost, motivating efficient methods that train models to solve tasks using fewer tokens. However, a common concern is that such training may cause models to skip important reasoning steps, so the CoT no longer faithfully reflects the model's decision. It is unclear whether or when this occurs in practice, since different efficiency methods apply length pressure to models' CoT in distinct ways, and faithfully explaining a model's decision takes more tokens on some tasks than others. To understand these dynamics, we fine-tune a variety of models with three methods that apply length pressure differently, namely a fixed generation budget, a per-example length target, and a group-relative length reward. We evaluate how efficient reasoning affects CoT faithfulness (i.e., how well the CoT reflects model decisions on related inputs) and monitorability (i.e., whether the CoT reveals when input interventions alter the output). We find that it affects faithfulness and monitorability differently. Faithfulness falls in most settings, primarily because the trained models are less consistent. Monitorability is more robust, as models keep acknowledging the influence on their answer even when the CoT is much shorter.

</details>


### [101] [DyRA: Dynamic Residual Approximation for Efficient Matrix Multiplication in DNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02882)
*Daewon Chae,Hyunwon Chung,Changwoo Lee,Hun-Seok Kim*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出 DyRA，一种输入自适应方法，通过动态校正结构化权重近似引入的输出残差误差，在相同计算预算下提升矩阵乘法的近似保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低秩等结构化权重近似方法优化的是权重而非决定推理精度的输出激活，导致微小权重空间误差被输入激活放大，产生较大输出误差。

Method: DyRA 直接优化输出的低秩因子，并在推理时动态近似与校正结构化权重近似产生的输出误差，将高效结构化计算与输入相关校正相结合。

Result: 在视觉、语音与语言模型上，DyRA 持续优于仅权重结构化近似；在 DINOv3 上实现 1.5 倍端到端 GPU 加速，同时将精度退化降低 3 倍以上。

Conclusion: 直接针对输出误差进行输入自适应校正是提升结构化矩阵乘法近似精度-效率权衡的有效途径。

Abstract: Large-scale foundation models achieve strong performance across diverse tasks, but their size makes inference costly, largely due to dense matrix multiplications. Prior work reduces this cost by replacing dense weight matrices with efficient structured forms such as low-rank factorizations. However, these methods approximate weights rather than the output activations that determine inference accuracy.
