Consequently, small weight-space errors can be amplified by input activations, producing large output errors. In this work, we propose DyRA, an input-adaptive method that improves structured matrix multiplication approximation by correcting residual output errors during inference. We show that matrix multiplication can be approximated more effectively by directly optimizing low-rank factors of the output. DyRA builds on this insight by dynamically approximating and correcting the output error introduced by structured weight approximations. This combines efficient structured computation with input-dependent correction, yielding a more faithful approximation of full matrix multiplication under the same computational budget. Across vision, speech, and language models, DyRA consistently improves the accuracy-efficiency trade-off over structured weight approximations alone. Notably, DyRA achieves a 1.5$\times$ end-to-end GPU speedup for DINOv3 while reducing accuracy degradation by more than 3$\times$ relative to weight-only baselines.

</details>


### [102] [When Can We Trust the Matching Principle? Robust Deployment Geometry Under Finite-Sample and Model Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02894)
*Vishal Rajput*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出用信任比 τ=ε/γ（估计不确定性/谱分离度）量化匹配几何的可识别性，并据此设计置信度校准匹配（CCM）策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在匹配任务中，盲目匹配不可靠的几何结构会引入误差，需要在匹配与惩罚扩散之间做出原则性决策，而现有方法缺乏对匹配可靠性的量化指标与自适应策略。

Method: 定义信任比 τ=ε/γ；在线性二次匹配响应下，分析估计投影与oracle投影的drift，在Davis-Kahan分离区域 τ<1/2 内证明drift为 O(τ²)；提出CCM，将 τ 转化为策略：τ小时方向性匹配，τ大时逐步各向同性，阈值来自校准而非定理。

Result: 理论给出所选top-r部署子空间内drift随τ²缩放；实验观察到两种匹配机制，在UCI HAR嵌入上always-match在每一单元都劣于abstain。

Conclusion: 匹配应仅基于可识别的几何，否则扩散惩罚；τ可作为可靠匹配的实用判据，CCM能根据校准阈值在方向性与各向同性匹配间自适应切换。

Abstract: Match only geometry you can identify; otherwise spread the penalty. We quantify that decision by the trust ratio tau = epsilon / gamma (estimation uncertainty over spectral separation). Under the linear-quadratic Matching response, oracle-relative drift between estimated and oracle projector matching scales as tau^2 for probes in the chosen top-r deployment subspace -- O(tau^2) in the Davis-Kahan separation region tau < 1/2, with practical usefulness depending on constants. Confidence-Calibrated Matching (CCM) turns tau into a policy -- directional when tau is small, progressively isotropic when not -- with thresholds from calibration, not from the theorem (match sits in the separation region; soft is mostly heuristic). Experiments show both regimes, including UCI HAR embeddings where always-match is worse than abstain on every cell.

</details>


### [103] [Recursive Harness Self-Improvement for Frontier Reasoning Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03548)
*Wenlong Zhang,Zhengbo Jiao,Chenxu Zhang,Lekang Jiang,SiYuan Ma,Qituan Zhang,Guo Chen,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出任务-工具协同进化框架，实现推理数据合成中递归工具自我改进，生成逐步变难的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务级递归仅复用生成问题作为种子，未改变构造工具，难以随任务分布演化生成更难问题。

Method: 任务-工具协同进化框架，包含在线自我改进（将求解器失败转化为可复用技能）和任务后自我改进（每批后修订技能、提示和工作流，仅在有限成本增加内生成更难有效任务时采纳候选）；模型权重与验证标准固定。

Result: 在数学、编程和科学领域，十四轮进化后求解器平均准确率从100.0%降至54.8%；消融表明两种更新计划结合比固定工具递归或单独计划生成更难任务；数据提升下游SFT和GRPO性能，27B学生在10K数学合成样例上微调后，APEX平均16次准确率达62.5%，与部分前沿模型参考相当。

Conclusion: 支持在生成更具挑战性且具下游训练价值的数据时，同步适应合成工具与任务。

Abstract: Generating progressively harder reasoning problems requires synthesis procedures that adapt as the task distribution evolves. Existing task-level recursion reuses generated problems as seeds but leaves the construction harness unchanged. We present task-harness co-evolution, a framework for recursive harness self-improvement (RSI) in reasoning-data synthesis. Online self-improvement converts intermediate solver failures into reusable skills during generation. Post-task self-improvement revises skills, prompts, and workflows after each batch, adopting candidates only when they generate harder valid tasks within a bounded cost increase. Model weights and verification criteria remain fixed. Across mathematics, coding, and science, mean solver accuracy decreases from 100.0% to 54.8% over fourteen evolution rounds. Ablations show that combining both update schedules produces harder tasks than fixed-harness recursion or either schedule alone. The resulting data improves downstream SFT and GRPO performance. In particular, a 27B student fine-tuned on 10K synthesized mathematics examples achieves 62.5% mean-16 accuracy on APEX, competitive with selected frontier-model references. These results support adapting the synthesis harness alongside the tasks to generate increasingly challenging data with downstream training value.

</details>


### [104] [Do ResNets Route? Sparse Interaction Experts in Residual Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02907)
*Liang Yan,Siying Chen,Kaijie Chen,Bo Li,Jinghao Zhang,Mu Miao*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 该论文将训练好的ResNet视为二值残差分支掩码上的集合函数，通过Möbius反演精确分解输出为各阶残差修正与高阶交互，揭示密集ResNet隐式实现了软路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ResNet对每个输入都执行所有块，但各块的功能贡献未必与输入无关；作者希望量化并理解残差分支之间的交互结构，检验是否存在输入依赖的功能路由。

Method: 将ResNet输出建模为二值残差分支掩码的集合函数，用Möbius反演分解为各阶交互项；对平滑残差栈证明固定k阶交互随残差缩放呈O(λ^k)；对ImageNet预训练的ResNet-18和ResNet-34进行穷举交互分析。

Result: 交互质量峰值分别出现在五阶和十阶而非低阶；减小残差缩放使谱向低阶移动但改变预测；交互系数幅度集中但非硬稀疏，保预测稀疏性随深度减弱；主导交互随输入和预测类别变化，围绕共享全局核心，整体复杂度随样本难度变化不大。

Conclusion: 密集ResNet实现了隐式软路由：所有块都执行，但不同输入依赖不同的残差交互专家；路由可在功能贡献层面涌现，无需显式路由器或稀疏执行。

Abstract: Residual networks execute every block for every input, yet their functional contributions need not be input independent. We formulate a trained ResNet as a set function over binary residual-branch masks and apply Möbius inversion to decompose its output exactly into individual residual corrections and higher-order interactions. For smooth residual stacks, we show that each fixed $k$-way interaction scales as $\mathcal{O}(λ^k)$ under residual scaling. Exhaustive analysis of ImageNet-pretrained ResNet-18 and ResNet-34 reveals that interaction mass peaks at orders five and ten, respectively, rather than at low orders. Reducing the residual scale shifts both spectra toward lower orders, but also changes model predictions. The interaction coefficients are concentrated in magnitude but not hard sparse, and prediction-preserving sparsity weakens with depth. Crucially, the dominant interactions vary across inputs and predicted classes around a shared global core, while their overall complexity changes little with sample difficulty. These results show that dense ResNets implement an implicit form of soft routing: every block is executed, but different inputs rely on different residual interaction experts. Routing can therefore emerge at the level of functional contribution without an explicit router or sparse execution.

</details>


### [105] [Constraint-Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02909)
*Jinwoo Kim*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 该论文探讨在语言模型生成程序时，约束解码所施加的静态分析规则与模型训练中已学到的抑制规则存在重复；作者提出将分析外置，定义约束感知训练目标，并从理论与合成实验两方面论证其可提升参数与数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准训练已使模型学会抑制约束解码所拒绝的词元，若推理时仍执行分析过滤，则训练中重复学习这些规则是冗余的；核心问题是能否在训练中不教这些分析，而将其完全外置到推理阶段，从而获得更高效的模型。

Method: 定义满足外置需求的通用约束感知目标，将外置收益形式化为关于模型规模与数据效率的三个定理，并通过受控合成实验验证这些定理在训练动态下是否成立。

Result: 在匹配参数量与数据量的条件下，约束感知训练相较普通交叉熵训练取得更低的预测损失，表明外置带来的理论收益在训练动态中依然成立。

Conclusion: 训练目标应显式纳入生成阶段所使用的程序分析，以利用外置带来的参数与数据效率优势，为程序生成模型的高效训练提供新方向。

Abstract: When generating programs with language models, constrained decoding can apply program analyses to exclude tokens that violate syntax, scope, or typing rules. However, there is a duplication: standard training already teaches the model to suppress the tokens rejected by these analyses. This duplication leads to the question: if we will perform some analysis to filter a set tokens out during inference anyways, can we avoid teaching the model the said analysis altogether during training, and does this externalization lead to more efficient models? This paper defines a general constraint-aware objective satisfying this externalization desideratum and formalizes the benefits of externalization into three concrete theorems about model size and data efficiency. We show, through a controlled synthetic experiment, that the theorems survive training dynamics: constraint-aware training yields lower prediction loss at a matched parameter count and data compared to ordinary cross-entropy training, motivating training objectives that incorporate the analyses used during generation.

</details>


### [106] [Dynamic Expert Pruning for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02951)
*Jabin Koo,Soheil Abbasloo,Sungjae Lee,Jungseul Ok*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出动态专家剪枝（DEP），利用智能体提示文本预测每个请求所需专家，实现MoE模型在多智能体场景下的按需稀疏化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构虽仅激活少量专家，但所有专家必须常驻显存，导致内存成为部署瓶颈。现有专家剪枝方法为静态：离线校准一个固定掩码应用于所有请求。在多智能体等异构工作负载中，不同任务与角色调用不同专家，静态方法无法适配。

Method: 基于“智能体的系统提示与任务提示足以识别其所需专家”这一发现，训练一个轻量级预测器（仅需一次工作流转录数据），将提示文本通过单次前向传播映射为每请求的专用专家掩码，无需针对每个配置进行校准。

Result: 在多种任务与角色、模型规模及MoE架构上，DEP的整体准确率优于静态剪枝与合并基线；可泛化至训练中未见的工作流而无需重训练；在保留专家数较少时优势最大。

Conclusion: 多智能体系统固有的角色专业化特性使得比静态剪枝更稀疏的专家服务成为可能，DEP通过提示驱动的动态专家选择有效降低内存占用并提升异构负载下的准确率。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures scale language models efficiently by activating only a few experts per token, but the saving is confined to computation: every expert must stay resident on the accelerator, so memory bounds where these models can be deployed. Expert pruning reduces this footprint, yet existing methods are static --- a single mask, calibrated offline, is applied to the model for every subsequent request. This assumption can fail when the workload is heterogeneous, most prominently in multi-agent systems, where one backbone serves many tasks and roles at once: our analysis shows that different tasks and roles recruit different experts, while static methods assign one fixed subset to all of them. We therefore propose Dynamic Expert Pruning (DEP), which rests on a finding we establish here: an agent's system and task prompts are by themselves sufficient to identify the experts that agent and its task require, since that text already describes what the agent will do. A lightweight predictor, trained once on workflow transcripts, turns those prompts into a specialized per-request mask in a single forward pass, with no per-configuration calibration. Across diverse tasks and roles, model scales, and MoE architectures, DEP achieves better overall accuracy than static pruning and merging baselines, and generalizes to workflows unseen in training without retraining. Its margin over those baselines is largest when few experts are retained, suggesting that the role specialization inherent to multi-agent systems permits sparser serving than static pruning allows.

</details>


### [107] [Low-Cost Video--Time Priors as a Strong Baseline for EEG--fNIRS Emotion Regression on Familiar Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03618)
*Minghao Kong,Jiurun Chen,Ying Gao,Xiangbin Meng,Rongjie Wang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 在熟悉视频的情感回归任务中，视频身份和视频内时间先验是强且低成本基线，EEG-fNIRS仅提供可选残差信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在熟悉视频部署场景下，参与者特定训练响应能否被估计，以及生理信号是否能在固定融合中提供残差校正。

Method: 采用先验主导的固定融合策略，在24名被试上进行五折留一被试评估，并进行源显式消融实验分析视频身份、视频内时间及EEG-fNIRS的贡献。

Result: 内部和外部评估中融合的MAE分别相差在0.05和0.32以内；消融显示视频身份和视频内时间贡献最大，EEG-fNIRS增益较小且因被试和视频而异。

Conclusion: 视频时间先验是强且低成本基线，EEG-fNIRS可作为熟悉视频情感回归的可选残差信号。

Abstract: Continuous emotion regression estimates moment-to-moment valence and arousal while a viewer watches a video. In familiar-video deployment, responses fron training participant-specific estimate, and prior-dominating fixed fusion tests whether physiology adds residual correction. In five-fold subject-held-out evaluation on 24was within 0.05 and 0.32 MAE of fusion in the internal and external evaluations, respectively. Source-explicit ablations showed that video identity and within-video tine accounted for most of the reduction, while EG-FNIRS gains were smaller and varied across participants and videos. These results identify the video-time prior as a strong, low-cost baseline and position EEG-fNIRS as an optional residual signal for familiar-video emotion regression.

</details>


### [108] [Depth as Time in One-Step Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03626)
*Arnold Caleb Asiimwe,William Yang,Sanghyuk Chun,Esin Tureci,Olga Russakovsky*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 一步生成模型并未消除扩散的时间计算，而是将其重组到网络深度中，表现为逐层去噪与再噪现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究多步扩散的去噪轨迹在压缩为单次前向传播后如何变化，以及这种变化是否与流图训练任务相关。

Method: 通过用模型自身输出头解码中间层来探测单步生成模型（如MeanFlow、漂移模型）的逐层计算，并分析不同传输任务对深度方向去噪的影响。

Result: 发现MeanFlow存在深度即时间的现象：较短传输区间内单次前向同时出现去噪和再噪；无时间索引传输任务的模型（如漂移模型）无此现象。可压缩性更高，MeanFlow SiT-L/2可压缩16.6倍参数为单个时间条件块。

Conclusion: 扩散的时间计算在一步生成中未被消除，而是跨网络深度重新组织，且该现象与传输任务相关，并可用于模型压缩。

Abstract: The recent wave of one-step generative models, which compress the multi-step trajectory of diffusion via either distillation or learned flow maps, has reached an inflection point where they can generate high-quality images. Here, we ask a natural question that follows from these advances: what happens to the denoising trajectory of multi-step diffusion when generation is compressed into a single forward pass? We offer an empirical observation we call \textit{depth as time}: the denoising computation that multi-step diffusion performs across sampling steps appears to unfold across the depth of a single forward pass, and can be recovered by decoding intermediate layers with the model's own output head. Most interestingly, we show that this depthwise computation depends on the transport task a flow map is trained to solve. The most surprising case is MeanFlow, where probing shorter transport intervals reveals both denoising and renoising within a single network evaluation. In contrast, generators trained without a time-indexed transport task, such as drifting models, do not exhibit the same depthwise denoising. Consequently, we show that models that exhibit the depthwise denoising phenomenon are more compressible across the layerwise computation: a MeanFlow \texttt{SiT-L/2} model can be compressed by $16.6\times$ in parameters into a single time-conditioned block. We offer an explanation for this denoise-then-renoise behavior and show that, when we treat the layerwise computation explicitly as a flow, a single time-conditioned block can be trained to denoise across layers, compressing a MeanFlow \texttt{SiT-L/2} model by $16.6\times$ in parameters. Together, these results suggest that the temporal computation of diffusion is not eliminated by one-step generation, but reorganized across network depth.

</details>


### [109] [MRVQ: One Resident Index for Dimension- and Rate-Elastic Vector Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03651)
*Sean Culatana,Shang-En Huang,Kang Li*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出Matryoshka残差向量量化（MRVQ），一种针对冻结嵌入的后处理残差量化器，通过截断残差阶段和嵌入坐标，用单一驻留工件适配任意（维度，码率）组合，实现低内存弹性检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集检索服务需随延迟、质量和内存预算变化，在嵌入前缀维度和索引比特率之间切换；为每个码率单独调优量化器虽质量最优，但检索层需同时保存多个码流和量化器状态，内存开销大。

Method: MRVQ是一种后处理残差量化器，最大码率码本可通过两种方式截断：丢弃残差阶段降低码率，丢弃嵌入坐标降低维度；因此一个常驻工件即可服务所有评估的（维度，码率）对。另评估了一种低构建成本的PCA-标量设计。

Result: 在FiQA和NFCorpus、四种嵌入家族、{4,8,16}字节码上，MRVQ是评估中RAM最低的设计：比三个单独训练的QINCo2索引少17.8-22.0倍内存，比精简共享模型steelman少1.89-2.02倍。代价是每码率QINCo2在FiQA上nDCG@10高0.026-0.107；但MRVQ在相同码本大小下优于PQ、OPQ和AdANNS-OPQ。PCA-标量设计质量与RaBitQ及其扩展相当，中位构建速度快420倍。两个负面结果：QINCo2在高码率训练时崩溃；排序边界假设未达到预设接受标准。

Conclusion: MRVQ是弹性检索的低内存工作点，而非通用质量赢家。

Abstract: Dense-retrieval services must switch among embedding-prefix dimensions and index bit rates as latency, quality, and memory budgets change. Tuning a quantizer separately for each rate gives the best quality, but the retrieval tier then holds several code streams and quantizer states at once. We introduce Matryoshka Residual Vector Quantization (MRVQ), a post-hoc residual quantizer for frozen embeddings. Its maximum-rate code can be truncated two ways: dropping residual stages lowers the rate, and dropping embedding coordinates lowers the dimension. One resident artifact therefore serves every (dimension, rate) pair we evaluate. Across FiQA and NFCorpus, four embedding families, and {4, 8, 16}-byte codes, MRVQ is the lowest-RAM design we evaluate. It uses 17.8-22.0x less memory than three separately trained QINCo2 indices, and 1.89-2.02x less than a lean shared-model steelman. The saving is not free: per-rate QINCo2 is 0.026-0.107 nDCG@10 better on FiQA. But MRVQ beats PQ, OPQ, and AdANNS-OPQ at matched code size. We also evaluate a low-build-cost PCA-scalar design that attains quality comparable to RaBitQ and its extension while fitting 420x faster at the median. Finally, we report two negative results: QINCo2 collapses when trained at high rates, and a ranking-bound hypothesis misses its pre-specified acceptance criteria. MRVQ is therefore a low-memory operating point for elastic retrieval, not a universal quality winner.

</details>


### [110] [Sentry: Learning to Recover from LLM Agent Failures at Test Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02994)
*Changxiu Ji,Amy Lu,Qizheng Zhang,Kunle Olukotun*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出Sentry失败管理中间层，将失败教训视为条件知识，仅在检测到失败时按需注入上下文，从而提升LLM智能体可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体常因无效工具调用、重复动作或推理不扎实而中途失败，从失败中学习是提升可靠性的途径，但失败知识如何到达智能体与知识内容本身同样重要。

Method: 在智能体旁运行Sentry层：检测到失败时从外部剧本检索匹配教训以指导恢复；在没有任务奖励的情况下验证智能体是否恢复，仅在恢复时存储新教训；完整剧本从不进入智能体上下文。

Result: 在多个智能体基准上，Sentry平均超越最强运行时干预基线37%；在两个共同评估的基准上超越最强上下文演化基线39%；与上下文演化结合可进一步提升；学到的教训可迁移到留出任务。

Conclusion: 失败知识是条件知识，应当条件性暴露；将完整剧本暴露给智能体会降低性能，即使相关教训仍可按需获取。

Abstract: LLM agents often fail mid-task due to invalid tool calls, repeated actions, or poorly grounded reasoning, and learning from these failures is a path to reliability. We find that how failure knowledge reaches the agent matters as much as what it contains. Failure lessons are conditional: kept in the agent's context, they misfire when their failure is absent, and removing them from an evolving playbook improves performance. Runtime interventions, in contrast, act only when a failure occurs but do not learn from their repairs. We argue that failure knowledge is conditional knowledge and should be conditionally exposed, and instantiate this principle in Sentry, a failure-management layer that runs alongside the agent. When Sentry detects a failure, it retrieves matching lessons from an external playbook to guide recovery, verifies without access to task rewards whether the agent recovered, and stores a new lesson only if it did; the full playbook never enters the agent's context. Across multiple agentic benchmarks, Sentry outperforms the strongest runtime-intervention baseline on every benchmark, by 37\% on average, and the strongest context-evolution baseline by 39\% on the two benchmarks where both are evaluated; combining Sentry with context evolution yields further gains. Learned lessons transfer to held-out tasks, and controlled experiments show that exposing the full playbook to the agent lowers performance even when relevant lessons remain available on demand.

</details>


### [111] [AvoKV-E: Payload-Aware KV Cache Eviction for Long Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03007)
*Han Yu,Wenhui Zhu,Xiwen Chen,Zhipeng Wang,Hejian Sang,Han Shi,Menglin Zhou,Xuanzhao Dong,Minzhou Huang,Rui Cai,Hao Wang,Alborz Geramifard*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出免训练KV缓存淘汰策略AvoKV-E，通过延迟近期状态的可淘汰资格并按候选归一化读压力、键冗余度和值载荷潜力排序，在长推理中提升缓存效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长输出推理使KV缓存瓶颈从固定提示转向生成轨迹；现有淘汰方法仅将缓存项视为路由对象，忽略低注意力项可能携带大值载荷以及新生成状态可能被过早视为陈旧的问题。

Method: AvoKV-E免训练：先延迟近期状态的淘汰资格，再基于候选归一化读压力、键冗余度和值载荷潜力对合格项排序淘汰。

Result: 在不同模型和数据集上，匹配活动KV预算时，AvoKV-E达到或超过冗余感知、基于复现和思维自适应淘汰基线，在缓存最紧张时增益最大；组件与反事实分析关联增益于延迟观察、载荷感知评分、冗余和尺度稳健归一化。

Conclusion: 长推理KV淘汰应不仅保留可能被读取的键，还应保留维持推理轨迹的值载荷。

Abstract: Long-output reasoning shifts the KV-cache bottleneck from the fixed prompt to the generated trace. Existing reasoning-cache eviction methods largely treat cached entries as routing objects, estimating whether an old key will still be read, will recur, or can be replaced. This routing-only view overlooks two effects: low-attention entries can carry large value payloads whose removal changes future predictions, and newly generated states can appear stale before later queries have had a chance to read them. We introduce AvoKV-E, a training-free eviction policy that first delays eligibility for recent states and then ranks eligible entries using candidate-normalized read pressure, key redundancy, and value-payload potential. According to empirical evaluation across different models and datasets, AvoKV-E matches or exceeds redundancy-aware, recurrence-based, and thought-adaptive eviction baselines at matched active-KV budgets, with its largest gains in the tightest-cache regime. Component and counterfactual analyses further connect these gains to delayed observation, payload-aware scoring, redundancy, and scale-robust normalization. Together, the results show that long-reasoning KV eviction should preserve not only keys that are likely to be read, but also the value payloads that sustain the reasoning trajectory.

</details>


### [112] [Tailoring the Quantization Space for 1-Bit KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03027)
*Minsoo Cheong,Donghyun Son,Sungjoo Yoo*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: TaSQ通过定制向量量化目标空间，在极低比特下压缩KV缓存，显著提升长上下文LLM推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM推理中KV缓存成为主要内存瓶颈，现有向量量化方法在1比特等极端压缩下性能严重下降。

Method: 提出TaSQ，结合查询引导的通道加权、跨头归一化和协方差感知的通道分组，定制VQ目标空间，且变换与RoPE兼容并可合并到投影权重和码本中，保持传统VQ查找结构，额外开销可忽略。

Result: 在通用、长链思维推理和长上下文检索基准上，TaSQ持续优于现有低比特KV缓存VQ基线并保持推理稳定性；在单张RTX 6000 Ada GPU上，其SGLang实现支持高达14倍的批量大小，峰值吞吐量比BF16基线高1.87倍。

Conclusion: TaSQ有效缓解KV缓存内存瓶颈，在极端压缩下实现优越性能与高效推理。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache becomes a major memory bottleneck in long-context LLM inference, placing substantial pressure on memory capacity and bandwidth. To mitigate this bottleneck, vector quantization (VQ) has emerged as a promising approach for aggressive KV cache compression. However, existing VQ methods degrade substantially in the 1-bit regime. At such extreme compression, each codebook must represent a larger group of channels with a limited set of centroids, making effective use of its capacity increasingly challenging. To address this, we introduce $\textbf{TaSQ}$, which tailors the VQ target space by combining query-guided channel weighting, cross-head normalization, and covariance-aware channel grouping to better reflect the error sensitivity and statistical structure of cached activations. Since these transforms are RoPE-compatible and can be easily merged into projection weights and codebooks, TaSQ preserves the conventional VQ lookup structure and adds negligible serving overhead. Across general, long-chain-of-thought reasoning, and long-context retrieval benchmarks, TaSQ consistently outperforms existing low-bit KV cache VQ baselines while preserving reasoning stability. On a single RTX 6000 Ada GPU, its SGLang implementation supports up to $14\times$ larger batch sizes and achieves $1.87\times$ higher peak throughput compared to the BF16 baseline.

</details>


### [113] [WebFovea: When the Model Is Right but the Click Is Wrong -- Reliable Round Trips for Vision-Based Web Agents on Live Websites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03036)
*Jiangang Han*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: WebFovea是一个基于视觉的网页智能体，在2026年WebRetriever挑战赛中获得第二名（57.0/100），其核心贡献是强调模型与页面之间的执行框架（harness）对任务成功率的关键影响，并通过加固四个执行阶段将隐藏集得分从31.0提升至57.0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型是网页智能体的必要条件但非充分条件；在真实网站上，许多失败发生在模型智能体与浏览器交互的四个执行阶段（解析、执行、反馈、信息呈现）而非模型推理本身，因此需要系统性地加固harness。

Method: 提出四阶段执行视图：模型回复解析为动作、动作在页面生效、结果准确回传、模型获得所需信息。WebFovea针对每个阶段进行加固，并添加规则与预算护栏。修复了坐标空间不匹配、原生下拉菜单/iframe/文本框静默失败、自生成聊天模板token污染等问题。

Result: 在WebRetriever基准Protocol III的官方隐藏集上，使用相同模型但改进harness，得分从31.0提升至57.0，最终在挑战赛中获得第二名（57.0/100）。观察到4.9%的任务片段受到自生成token污染，坐标错误导致点击偏移至3/4位置。

Conclusion: 四阶段视图不依赖于特定模型，但部分修复依赖模型；harness改进显著提升性能。论文提供了设计、各组件证据（含负面结果）、失败分析、局限性以及未来路线图（如将不同步骤路由到不同模型）。

Abstract: We present WebFovea, a vision-based web agent that placed 2nd in the WebRetriever Challenge 2026 with a final score of 57.0 out of 100. The challenge evaluates agents end to end on Protocol III of the WebRetriever benchmark (arXiv:2607.06118): starting from an entry URL on a live website, the agent must operate the site's own interface and return a verifiable answer. A capable multimodal large language model (LLM) is necessary for this, but not sufficient. The model's decisions reach the browser through the harness, the code between the model and the page. At every step, four things must go right: the model's reply must be parsed into the intended action, the action must take effect on the page, the result must be reported back accurately, and the model must be shown the information it needs. On real websites, many of the failures we observed occurred at one of these four stages rather than in the model's reasoning. A coordinate-space mismatch placed every click at 3/4 of its intended coordinates; actions on native dropdowns, inside iframes, and in text boxes failed silently; and self-generated chat-template tokens contaminated 4.9% of task episodes. WebFovea hardens each stage and surrounds the loop with guardrails that keep the agent within the rules and its budget. The four-stage view does not depend on the model, although some individual fixes do. Because we used the same model in all four submissions, the rise of our official hidden-set score from 31.0 to 57.0 reflects changes to the harness, up to run-to-run variance on live sites. We describe the design, the evidence for each component (including negative results), a failure analysis, the limitations, and a roadmap that includes routing different steps to different models.

</details>


### [114] [When Does Synthetic Relational Data Teach Models to Use Relations? Tracing Predictive Structure from Pretraining Data to Model Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03057)
*Shivam Dubey,Mohamed Bouadi,Nassim Bouarour,Varun Kulkarni,Aditya Tanna,Vinay Kumar Sankarapu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文研究合成数据库的何种性质能诱导关系型基础模型学习关系结构计算，通过四个数据生成器训练的Relational Transformer对比实验进行数据归因分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关系型基础模型虽在合成数据库上预训练，但下游基准难以解释为何某一合成语料能产生更优模型；强性能可能仅源于逼真的行级统计，而非模型真正学会利用关系结构。

Method: 以数据归因为视角，使用架构、初始化、目标函数和算力预算完全相同的四个Relational Transformer检查点，分别在四种关系数据生成器产生的语料上训练，追踪数据的可测量属性到所学计算及下游行为。假设当跨表信息对被掩码单元的预训练目标具有预测必要性时，关系机制才会涌现。

Result: RelDiff从外键链接父表获得最大的预测增益，其对应模型对未见数据库上的外键干预具有独特敏感性；该依赖性通过随机初始化对照检验，随损坏链接比例单调增长，并定位于一条串行跨表通路。

Conclusion: 在下游推理中破坏同一机制会消除RelDiff在关系任务上的优势，而结构不敏感模型几乎不变；结果将合成训练数据的属性与所学机制及下游行为通过干预联系起来。

Abstract: Relational foundation models are increasingly pretrained on synthetic databases, yet downstream benchmarks reveal little about why one synthetic corpus produces a better model than another. In particular, strong performance may arise from realistic row-level statistics without the model ever learning to use relational structure. We study this as a data-attribution problem: which property of synthetic pretraining data induces relational computation? Using four Relational Transformer checkpoints trained with the same architecture, initialization, objective, and compute budget on corpora produced by four relational data generators, we trace a measurable property of the data to learned computation and downstream behavior. We hypothesize that relational mechanisms emerge when cross-table information is predictively necessary for the masked-cell pretraining objective. RelDiff exhibits by far the largest predictive gain from foreign-key-linked parents, and its corresponding model is uniquely sensitive to foreign-key interventions on unseen databases. This dependence survives a random-initialization control, grows monotonically with the fraction of corrupted links, and localizes to a serial cross-table pathway. Finally, disrupting the same mechanism during downstream inference removes RelDiff's advantage on relational tasks while leaving structure-insensitive models nearly unchanged. These results connect a property of synthetic training data to a learned mechanism and, through intervention, to downstream behavior.

</details>


### [115] [Learning Transferable Policies from Action-free Time Series Through Dynamical Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03065)
*Niklas Emonds,Georgia Koppe*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出分层基于模型的强化学习框架，利用相关系统间的共享结构，从无动作记录中学习特定系统的控制策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从无动作记录中学习控制存在挑战：干预效果不可观测，且策略可能利用重构动态中的误差。

Method: 采用分层动态系统重构模型，通过低维嵌入捕捉共享动态与个体差异；嵌入用于参数化共享策略与价值网络，策略在显式干预模型（含加性潜在扰动）下完全通过模拟训练；使用分段线性循环神经网络进行机制分析，解码器约束使干预效果在观测空间可解释，并支持跨模态干预保护。

Result: 在Lorenz-63和双摆系统上，分层策略比独立训练策略迁移更好；在Lorenz-63上，平均奖励高于使用相同重构模型的重复规划，与使用受控交互训练的方法相当，且仅通过嵌入推断即可泛化到策略训练中未见的系统；在神经行为记录中，约束神经扰动下预测运动被抑制。

Conclusion: 共享动态表示支持可迁移控制，并能从无动作记录中进行机制假设生成。

Abstract: Learning control from action-free recordings is challenging because intervention effects are unobserved and policies may exploit errors in reconstructed dynamics. We present a hierarchical model-based reinforcement learning framework that uses shared structure across related systems to learn system-specific control policies from action-free recordings. A hierarchical dynamical system reconstruction model captures shared dynamics and individual variation through low-dimensional embeddings. These embeddings are then reused to parameterize shared policy and value networks, linking differences in reconstructed dynamics to differences in control. Policies are trained entirely via simulation under an explicit intervention model with additive latent perturbations. Piecewise-linear recurrent neural networks enable mechanistic analyses of the controlled dynamics, while decoder-based constraints make the immediate effects of interventions interpretable in observation space and permit interventions on one modality while protecting another from direct manipulation. On Lorenz-63 and double-pendulum systems, hierarchical policies improve transfer over independently trained policies. On Lorenz-63, they also achieve a higher mean reward than repeated planning with the same reconstructed models, perform comparably to methods trained with controlled interactions, and generalize to systems absent from policy training after embedding inference alone. Applications to neural-behavioral recordings demonstrate suppression of predicted movement under constrained neural perturbations. Together, these findings show how shared dynamical representations support transferable control and mechanistic hypothesis generation from action-free recordings.

</details>


### [116] [Learn Feasibility Once, Optimize All Objectives: Derivative-Free Diffusion Models for Chance-Constrained Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03071)
*Ziwen Liu,Yan Liu,Congying Han,Tiande Guo,Yao Yan,Weichen Zhao*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出D³Opt框架，通过解耦约束建模与目标优化，实现无需重新训练的无导数优化，可处理非凸、非光滑目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机会约束规划方法在优化非凸或非光滑目标以及固定机会约束下适应不同目标时仍面临挑战。

Method: 训练一个风险条件扩散模型，仅基于约束过滤后的决策学习机会可行结构，并冻结为可复用先验；推理时在冻结的反向扩散过程中引入退火、基于粒子的Feynman-Kac校正，仅用函数评估优化后指定目标。

Result: 在线性高斯CCP、目标迁移任务和机会约束经济调度实验中，有效优化了光滑与非光滑目标（包括非凸情形），并在固定机会约束下无需重新训练即可泛化到新目标。

Conclusion: D³Opt通过解耦约束与目标，实现了无需目标特定重训练的无导数优化，并证明了可行性保持与优化误差界。

Abstract: Chance-constrained programs (CCPs) optimize decisions under uncertainty by limiting the probability of constraint violation. Despite advances in traditional and learning-based approaches, optimizing non-convex or non-smooth objectives and adapting to different objectives under fixed chance constraints remain challenging. In this paper, we propose a \textbf{D}erivative-free \textbf{D}iffusion-based framework that \textbf{D}isentangles constraint modeling from objective optimization, termed \textbf{D$^3$Opt}. We learn the chance-feasible structure once, independently of any particular objective, by training a risk-conditioned diffusion model solely on constraint-filtered decisions and freezing it as a reusable prior for post-specified objectives. At inference time, we propose an annealed, particle-based Feynman--Kac correction along the frozen reverse diffusion process to optimize post-specified objectives using only function evaluations. This enables derivative-free optimization of non-convex and non-smooth objectives without objective-specific retraining. We prove that the correction preserves feasibility when this property holds for the frozen prior, and derive an optimization-error bound separating learned-prior coverage, finite-particle approximation, and finite-temperature effects. Experiments on linear Gaussian CCPs, objective-transfer tasks, and chance-constrained economic dispatch demonstrate effective optimization across smooth and non-smooth objectives, including non-convex cases, and objective generalization under fixed chance constraints without retraining.

</details>


### [117] [Coverage You Can Steer: Online Conformal Calibration for RL-Driven Hardware-Aware NAS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03127)
*Pedro Brandimarte,Nerea Aranjuelo,Marcos Nieto,Oihana Otaegui*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 该论文针对硬件感知神经架构搜索中奖励评估成本高的问题，用在线反馈控制替代一次性分位数估计，恢复共形预测过滤器的覆盖率可控性，从而在无精度损失下剪枝25-50%的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件感知NAS中每个架构必须训练才能获得奖励，评估成本主导；共形预测过滤器可剪枝候选，但其分布无关保证假设校准与测试候选可交换，而RL循环中策略提案随搜索改进且逐层构建时每个episode内发生偏移，违反该假设。

Method: 用在线反馈控制（自适应共形推断，含无调参、局部自适应和组条件变体）替代一次性分位数估计，实现可调、单调、可复现的覆盖率控制；在三种神经网络架构族、单步与顺序搜索（三个种子）上评估，并与静态校准和高斯过程基线对比。

Result: 该方法对所有请求水平跟踪误差约10^-3，剪枝25-50%的评估且无测量精度损失；静态校准失去覆盖率控制，高斯过程基线无论请求如何都保持保守；在一个约束测试台上作为采集函数，乐观界优于随机搜索，在线校准进一步锐化增益。

Conclusion: 在线自适应共形推断能有效恢复RL驱动NAS中候选剪枝的覆盖率可控性，显著降低评估成本而不损失精度，并可作为采集函数提升搜索效率；源代码已公开。

Abstract: Hardware-aware neural architecture search (NAS) is dominated by evaluation cost: every architecture must be trained before its reward is known. Conformal-prediction filters cut this cost by pruning candidates whose predicted-reward upper bound misses a threshold, with a distribution-free guarantee that at most a fraction $δ$ are wrongly discarded. That guarantee assumes exchangeability between calibration and test candidates, which the surrounding reinforcement-learning (RL) loop violates: the policy's proposals improve as search proceeds and, in layer-by-layer construction, shift within every episode. We replace one-shot quantile estimation with online feedback control (Adaptive Conformal Inference, with tuning-free, locally-adaptive, and group-conditional variants), restoring steerable coverage: dialing the target delivers it, monotonically and reproducibly, for arbitrary sequences. Across three neural-network architecture families and both single-step and sequential search (three seeds), it tracks every requested level to within ${\sim}10^{-3}$ while pruning 25-50% of evaluations at no measured accuracy cost, whereas static calibration loses control of its coverage and a Gaussian-process baseline stays conservative regardless of the request. Finally, used as an acquisition function on one constrained testbed, the same optimistic bound beats random search, a gain that fixed optimism already carries and online calibration sharpens. The source code is available at https://github.com/Vicomtech/rl-hw-nas.

</details>


### [118] [Page-EntroKV: Hardware-Aligned, Entropy-Weighted KV-Cache Eviction under Grouped-Query Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03135)
*Inbasekaran S*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出Page-EntroKV框架，在GQA物理组粒度上进行KV缓存淘汰，通过sink隔离的Renyi-2熵加权池化头分数并映射到PagedAttention页帧，消除组内选择分歧导致的缓存膨胀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态淘汰方法按查询头独立评分选token，与GQA多个查询头共享同一物理KV缓冲的分配方式不匹配：各头选择分歧迫使引擎保留并集，缓存膨胀最高达组比r倍；而算术平均池化会稀释承载事实召回的专业检索头。

Method: 在各物理组内，用基于sink隔离碰撞（Renyi-2）熵推导的权重池化各头分数，每头仅一次内积、预填充时计算且无需校准，使sink头无法伪装成检索头；将池化分数投影到PagedAttention页帧，在硬件元组（层、组、页）上执行淘汰。形式化并集开销比（UOR）与组内分歧，证明两头组下的精确恒等式及任意组比的双侧界，证明严格预算保持与有限上下文needle保留界，并给出精确的逐层页核算。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct（r=6）试点架构上，对2,240次组测量做头独立重放，2%预算下并集开销最高达4.75倍，而Page-EntroKV的UOR恒为1.000；sink隔离消除13倍sink伪装；20%预算下needle召回率100%，均值池化为0%；各页大小下保留基数均精确；20%保留率下QA与代码任务仍可求解。

Conclusion: Page-EntroKV在GQA实际分配的粒度上实现无并集膨胀的KV淘汰，保持预算并优于算术平均池化，为长上下文自回归模型服务提供有效方案。

Abstract: Serving long-context autoregressive language models is constrained by the key-value (KV) cache. Most dynamic eviction methods score token importance per query head and choose tokens independently. This fits poorly with grouped-query attention (GQA), where several query heads share one physical KV buffer: divergent per-head selections force the serving engine to retain the union of their choices - inflating the cache by up to the group ratio r - while arithmetic mean pooling dilutes the specialized retrieval heads that carry factual recall. We introduce Page-EntroKV, a formal framework for KV-cache eviction operating at the granularity GQA serving actually allocates. Heads within each physical group are pooled by weights derived from sink-isolated collision (Renyi-2) entropy - one inner product per head, computed once at prefill with no calibration - so sink heads cannot masquerade as retrieval heads. Pooled scores are projected onto PagedAttention page frames, and eviction executes at the hardware tuple (layer, group, page). We formalize the union overhead ratio (UOR) and intra-group disagreement, prove an exact identity linking them for two-head groups alongside two-sided bounds at every group ratio, prove strict budget preservation and a finite-context needle-retention bound that arithmetic mean pooling provably violates, and give exact per-layer page accounting. On a pilot architecture (Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct, r=6), head-independent replay over 2,240 group measurements yields union overhead up to 4.75x at a 2% budget, while Page-EntroKV holds UOR exactly 1.000; sink isolation removes a 13x sink masquerade; needle recall is 100% versus 0% for mean pooling at a 20% budget; retained cardinality is exact for every page size; and QA and code tasks remain solvable at 20% retention.

</details>


### [119] [AdaStep: Adaptive Step Credit Weighting for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03223)
*Xin Wang,Wenhao Wu,Menghao Zhang,Zhi Wang,Kun Shao,Jian Luan*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出AdaStep，一种自适应步骤信用加权方法，通过最优收缩系数控制局部优势对轨迹级信号的修正，在低计算成本下提升长时程LLM智能体训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程LLM智能体通常使用稀疏结果奖励训练，轨迹级目标太粗糙，无法区分单个决策的贡献；而步骤级信用分配虽能提供更细粒度监督，但其估计不可靠，因为观测回报还取决于后续动作、环境转移和轨迹长度。

Method: 将步骤信用加权建模为潜在步骤优势的均方误差估计问题，在显式条件采样假设下推导出最优的逐状态收缩系数；该系数具有信噪比解释，当回报变化可归因于所选动作时保留局部信用，当变化由下游随机性主导时抑制局部信用。AdaStep仅需轻量标量计算，无需critic、额外rollout或额外模型推理。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和ScienceWorld上，使用三种模型骨干进行实验，相比基线在低计算成本下取得一致改进。

Conclusion: AdaStep通过自适应步骤信用加权有效提升长时程LLM智能体训练性能，且计算开销低、易于集成。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents are typically trained with sparse outcome rewards, making trajectory-level objectives too coarse to distinguish the contribution of individual decisions. Step-level credit assignment provides finer-grained supervision, but its estimates can be unreliable because observed returns also depend on subsequent actions, environment transitions, and trajectory length. We propose AdaStep, an Adaptive Step-credit weighting method that controls how strongly each group-derived local advantage modifies the trajectory-level signal. We formulate this weighting as a mean-squared-error estimation problem for the latent step advantage and, under an explicit conditional sampling assumption, derive an optimal per-state shrinkage coefficient. The coefficient admits a signal-to-total-variance interpretation: it preserves local credit when return variation is attributable to the selected action and suppresses it when variation is dominated by downstream randomness. AdaStep requires only lightweight scalar computation, with no critic, additional rollouts, or extra model inference. Experiments with three model backbones on ALFWorld, WebShop, and ScienceWorld show consistent improvements over baselines at low computational cost.

</details>


### [120] [PaMIR: Open Benchmark of Public Credit-Default Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03259)
*Mikhail Liashkov,Ilyas Varshavskiy,Shuhratjon Khalilbekov,Azizjon Azimi,Bonu Boboeva*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 发布PaMIR，一个用于标签稀缺且延迟到达的信用违约预测的公开基准，包含19个公开数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准研究通常使用八个数据集，但仅有两到四个是公开的，缺乏统一、可复现且防泄漏的评估平台。

Method: 从固定源快照重建19个公开二分类违约标签数据集（124万贷款、企业和卡账户，来自九个国家），采用经过泄漏审计的单一处理流程且不重新分发；模型为单一函数，在重复i.i.d.划分和标签延迟流下评分，按标签预算报告AUC；合成数据测试框架防止生成器接触留出数据。

Result: PaMIR发布0.4.0版，据称是目前同类中唯一的基准；在不完整评分时扣留整体均值。

Conclusion: PaMIR为标签稀缺且延迟的信用违约预测提供了一个可复现、防泄漏的公开基准，并持续更新。

Abstract: We release PaMIR (Public Arrival-ordered Measurement for Inference in Risk), an open benchmark for credit-default prediction when labels are scarce and arrive late. The field's reference benchmark studies use eight datasets each, only two or four of them public. PaMIR brings together 19 public datasets with binary default labels -- 1.24M loans, firms and card accounts from nine countries -- rebuilt from pinned source snapshots by one leakage-audited recipe and never redistributed; to our knowledge it is the one of its kind as of today. Every model is a single function, scored under a repeated i.i.d. split and a label-delayed stream in which each application is scored on arrival, with AUC reported by label budget; fleet means are withheld unless every dataset is scored. A synthetic-data harness tests generated training rows without letting a generator see held-out rows. This report describes release 0.4.0 of this living benchmark.

</details>


### [121] [SCAD: Structured Credit Assignment and Distillation for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03372)
*Shangyang Wu,Shuai Zhao,Ziyue Zhu,Jinyang Wu,Anh Tuan Luu,Haoran Luo*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出SCAD方法，通过结合结果信用分配与教师指导蒸馏，提升长时域智能体的规划与执行性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域智能体稀疏终端奖励难以区分中间贡献，而在线策略蒸馏在学生生成历史增长时会丢失教师指导信息。

Method: SCAD将交互分为规划与有界子任务执行，在局部上下文中蒸馏执行，通过跨轨迹子任务前缀树细化规划信用，规划获得完整终端信用，执行获得正终端信用与教师指导。

Result: 在所有评估基准上，SCAD比最强训练基线在文本任务上宏平均准确率提高4.48个百分点，多模态任务提高4.19个百分点。

Conclusion: SCAD有效结合基于结果的信用分配与教师指导蒸馏，改善了长时域智能体的规划与执行。

Abstract: Training long-horizon agents to solve complex tasks requires effective supervision over extended interaction sequences. However, sparse terminal rewards obscure intermediate contributions, while on-policy distillation can lose informative teacher guidance as student-generated histories grow. To address this problem, we introduce SCAD, which organizes interactions into planning and bounded subtask execution, distills execution in local contexts, and refines planning credit through cross-rollout subtask prefix trees, with planning receiving full terminal credit and execution receiving positive terminal credit and teacher guidance. Across all evaluated benchmarks, SCAD improves macro-average accuracy over the strongest training baseline by 4.48 percentage points for text tasks and 4.19 points for multimodal tasks. SCAD effectively combines outcome-based credit assignment with teacher-guided distillation to improve planning and execution in long-horizon agents.

</details>


### [122] [Bidirectional Voronoi-biased Exploration Curriculum for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03395)
*Juri Pfammatter,Kaixian Qu,Clemens Schwarke,Victor Klemm,Marco Hutter*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出双向Voronoi偏置探索课程BVER，同时从起点与终点两端扩展课程，以解决稀疏奖励长时域目标条件强化学习的探索瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏奖励长时域任务中，策略从初始状态出发难以到达目标，缺乏学习信号；参考动作、手工课程与塑形奖励需演示或任务特定工程，而自动起止状态/目标课程通常仅从单侧扩展，须由该侧覆盖全部距离。

Method: 受双向RRT规划启发，BVER同时生长：从目标向外扩展起始状态，从初始状态分布向外扩展目标，二者均偏向未探索任务空间并相互趋近，在两端上训练同一个目标条件策略。

Result: 在质点迷宫、四足机器人爬箱与机械臂环套柱任务上，BVER比所有对比的无参考课程学习更快；爬箱任务中约减少65%迭代达到0.4 m箱95%成功率，是唯一学会爬0.7 m箱的方法，且策略对起点、目标与偏航变化鲁棒；无演示时，在0.4 m箱与环套柱任务上接近基于参考课程的样本效率；消融显示双向扩展优于任一单向。

Conclusion: 双向同时扩展的自动课程可有效缓解稀疏奖励探索瓶颈，并在多个任务上超越单侧无参考课程，逼近参考型课程的样本效率。

Abstract: Long-horizon tasks with sparse rewards pose an exploration bottleneck for goal-conditioned reinforcement learning: a policy started from the initial state rarely reaches the goal and receives no learning signal. Reference motions, hand-designed curricula, and shaped rewards supply this signal but require demonstrations or task-specific engineering; automatic start-state and goal curricula avoid this but typically expand from one side only, so the full distance to the target must be covered from that side. We propose the Bidirectional Voronoi-biased Exploration curriculum for Reinforcement learning (BVER), which expands from both ends at once. Inspired by bidirectional RRT planning, BVER grows start states outward from the goal and goals outward from the initial state distribution, biases both toward unexplored task space, and steers them toward each other, training one goal-conditioned policy on both. On point-mass mazes, quadrupedal box climbing, and robot-arm ring-on-peg transfer, BVER learns faster than all compared reference-free curricula. On box climbing, it reaches 95% success on a 0.4 m box in roughly 65% fewer iterations than the best of them, is the only one of them to learn to climb a 0.7 m box, and yields a policy robust to start, goal, and yaw variation. Without a demonstration, it approaches the sample efficiency of reference-based curricula on the 0.4 m box and on ring-on-peg transfer. Ablations show that expanding from both ends outperforms either direction alone.

</details>


### [123] [16-bit Precision of Convolutional Neural Networks on Microcontroller Units for 8-bit Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03402)
*Rui Liu,Benjamin Paaßen*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出W16A16高精度16位量化方法，在Armv7E-M架构上实现更快、更节能的推理，且量化误差远低于8位方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在边缘硬件上部署深度神经网络需要高效推理方案，同时保持高精度，现有量化方法在精度与效率间存在权衡。

Method: 提出W16A16（16位权重和激活）量化方法，分析Armv7E-M架构，解释16位方法性能优势的原理，并在回归和分类任务上评估量化误差及MCU部署的时间与能耗。

Result: 与8位量化方案相比，量化误差降低约10倍，同时推理时间和能耗相当或更优。

Conclusion: W16A16是一种高精度、快速、低能耗的量化方案，适用于边缘硬件上的深度神经网络部署。

Abstract: To deploy deep neural networks on edge hardware, highly efficient inference schemes are necessary that retain high accuracy. This work presents W16A16, a high precision (16-bit), fast speed, low energy quantization method. On a widely applied microcontroller architecture Armv7E-M, our proposed approach achieves faster speed and lower energy consumption on layer- and model-level compared to alternative quantization schemes. We analyze the architecture of Armv7E-M, explain the underlying principles behind the performance advantages of 16-bit approaches, and evaluate the empiric quantization errors for regression and classification tasks, as well as empiric time- and energy consumption in MCU deployment. We observe ca.\ 10 times lower quantization errors compared to 8-bit quantization schemes while achieving similar or better inference times and energy consumption.

</details>


### [124] [AIBL: Augmented Instance-Based Learning with Structured Memory and Neural Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03413)
*Radha Poovendran,Andrea Stocco,Linda Bushnell*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出AIBL，将实例学习扩展到学习向量空间，用于高维序列数据，保留实例存储和激活加权检索，并增加惊喜记忆与毕业机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统IBLT依赖符号知识表示和手工匹配规则，难以处理文本、图像等高维数据，需要学习相似度而非预设规则。

Method: AIBL在学习向量空间中用神经嵌入相似度替代符号情境匹配，保留实例存储、激活加权检索和效用混合；记忆分为活跃、遗忘和惊喜存储，惊喜存储隔离弱匹配或分布外样本，并通过观察驱动的毕业算法将重复出现的惊喜实例提升到活跃记忆。

Result: 在五个机器学习任务和三个受控仿真任务上，与经典IBLT变体及任务特定基线比较，AIBL准确率提升6到17个百分点。

Conclusion: 向量空间检索优于符号匹配，新增记忆机制有效管理新颖性检测、冷启动、概念漂移适应和奖励学习。

Abstract: Sequential learning systems often make decisions from accumulated experience while receiving high-dimensional inputs whose distribution may change over time. Instance-Based Learning Theory (IBLT) provides a principled case-based framework for such settings through stored situation-decision-utility instances, partial matching, activation, and blending. IBLT relies on symbolic knowledge representation in dictionary-like formats, but text, images, transaction vectors, and user-item histories often require learned similarity rather than hand-specified matching rules. In this paper, we introduce AIBL (Augmented Instance-Based Learning), an instance-learning model formulated in a learned vector space for high- dimensional sequential data. AIBL generalizes symbolic situation matching to neural embedding similarity while retaining instance storage, activation- weighted retrieval, and utility blending. The AIBL model organizes memory into active, forgotten, and surprise stores. Surprise memory separates weakly matched, possible out-of-distribution, or corner-case observations from active memory, reducing forced fitting to the nearest available cases. An observation-driven graduation algorithm promotes recurring surprise instances to active memory, allowing the memory to incorporate repeated novel patterns that may arise under concept drift. We evaluate the same implementation on five machine learning tasks and three controlled simulation tasks, comparing AIBL with classical IBLT variants and task-specific baselines where appropriate. AIBL improves accuracy by 6 to 17 percentage points. The results show where vector-space retrieval improves over symbolic matching and how the added memory mechanisms govern novelty detection, cold-start handling, drift adaptation, and reward learning under the tested protocols.

</details>


### [125] [Deep Bayesian REFoCUS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03419)
*Simon Penninga,Ruud van Sloun*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文将超声多基地恢复问题建模为贝叶斯推断，并提出Deep Bayesian REFoCUS方法，利用深度生成先验解决经典线性REFoCUS解码器在秩亏情形下的失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典线性REFoCUS解码器在秩亏（rank-deficient）条件下无法有效恢复多基地超声数据，且只提供点估计，无法表达不确定性。

Method: 针对多基地数据集训练深度生成先验，将任意发射序列下的超声多基地恢复表述为贝叶斯推断问题，从而在秩亏情形下进行后验推断。

Result: 在所有秩亏程度和噪声水平下均优于线性基线；当反演精确时退化为线性解码；能够在采集的零空间中表达不确定性；在仿真与在体数据之间存在分布偏移时，无需微调或自适应即表现出显著的泛化能力。

Conclusion: Deep Bayesian REFoCUS为秩亏多基地超声恢复提供了优于线性方法的贝叶斯解决方案，兼具不确定性量化与跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: In this work we formulate ultrasound multistatic recovery from arbitrary transmit sequences as a Bayesian inference problem. To that end, we train a deep generative prior on multistatic data sets to tackle the rank-deficient regime in which classical linear REFoCUS decoders fail. This appproach, which we term Deep Bayesian REFoCUS, outperforms the linear baselines for all regimes of rank-deficiency and noise levels, and regresses to linear decoding when inversion is exact. The model also expresses uncertainty in the null space of the acquisitions, whereas the linear REFoCUS decoders only provide point estimates. Finally, we analyze the impact of distribution shift between simulation and in-vivo acquisitions, showing remarkable generalization ability without any fine-tuning or adaptation.

</details>


### [126] [Measure Less, Know More: Self-Supervised Test-Time Feature Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03454)
*Eeshaan Jain,Linus Bleistein,Bart Deplancke,Charlotte Bunne*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出任务无关、自监督的多模态顺序获取原则ECHO-k，利用预训练模型内部表征作为代理目标，并通过强化学习策略实现成本感知的测试时模态选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时获取全部特征或模态成本高且冗余，而当下游任务或预测目标未知时，顺序选择信息量大的模态极具挑战。

Method: 以深度模型（如基础模型）的预训练内部表征作为总结跨模态信息的代理目标，在风格化线性设定下给出理论保证，并据此设计强化学习策略进行顺序模态选择。

Result: 在任务无关与无标签获取基线中，ECHO-k在多种基础模型后端上持续提升预算受限的下游性能。

Conclusion: 该方法为成本感知的测试时部署提供了原则性路径，适用于测量昂贵、时间受限且下游任务未知的多模态系统。

Abstract: Recent progress in multimodal, high-dimensional learning has enabled foundation models to process heterogeneous, large-scale data. However, at test time, acquiring all features or modalities can be prohibitively costly and often redundant. Sequentially selecting informative modalities is therefore critical, yet challenging when the downstream task or prediction target is unknown. To this end, we introduce ECHO-$k$, a task-agnostic and self-supervised learning principle for modality acquisition: we use a deep model's internal pretrained representations (e.g., from a foundation model) as proxy targets that summarize cross-modal information. We provide theoretical guarantees in a stylized linear setting that motivate a reinforcement learning (RL) policy for sequential modality selection. Across task-agnostic and label-free acquisition baselines, ECHO-$k$ consistently improves budgeted downstream performance across diverse foundation-model backends. Our method provides a principled route to cost-aware test-time deployment, with implications for any multimodal system where measurements are expensive or time-constrained, and downstream tasks unknown a priori.

</details>


### [127] [Beyond Random Splits: Evaluating Drug-Target Affinity Models Under Chemically and Biologically Motivated Distribution Shifts Copy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03456)
*Minjae Chung,Clara Li,Malar Paavai Muthukumaran,Shaunna Wang,Aniket Ramkrishnan Iyer,Shaun Qien Yeau Tan,Harinishree Sathu,Micky C. Nnamdi,J. Ben Tamo,Benoit Louis Marteau,May Dongmei Wang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 该论文研究DTA预测中不同分布偏移（OOD）如何影响模型架构选择，指出仅靠单一数据划分评估不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DTA模型常在单一数据划分下比较，但实际部署需外推到新化学系列、新蛋白靶点或二者兼有，因此需探究评估时的分布偏移是否改变最佳架构。

Method: 从ChEMBL和BindingDB整理718,800个唯一药物-蛋白对；以Morgan指纹+蛋白CNN为基线，对比12种受控架构（4种药物表示×3种ESM-2交互模式）；采用scaffold、fingerprint-cluster、protein-cluster和dual OOD评估，并用留出评估、重复种子、分组感知bootstrap和规模匹配对照验证。

Result: 验证RMSE从scaffold和fingerprint-cluster OOD的0.950、0.945升至protein-cluster和dual OOD的1.299、1.321；两种化学偏移下模型排名相似（tau=0.79），但与scaffold OOD一致性在protein OOD下降至tau=0.39，在dual OOD下反转至tau=-0.55。

Conclusion: 架构选择取决于外推形式，而不仅是平均误差或训练集大小；DTA基准应匹配部署时预期的化学和目标偏移。

Abstract: Drug-target affinity (DTA) prediction is widely used to prioritize candidate compounds before costly experimental screening. DTA models are often compared under a single data split, even though deployment may require extrapolation to new chemical series, new protein targets, or both. We ask whether the distribution shift used for evaluation changes which architecture appears best. We curate 718,800 unique drug-protein pairs from the ChEMBL and BindingDB datasets. We compare a Morgan-fingerprint + protein-CNN baseline with 12 controlled architectures that combine four drug representations with three ESM-2 interaction modes. Mean validation RMSE increases from 0.950 and 0.945 under scaffold and fingerprint-cluster OOD to 1.299 and 1.321 under protein-cluster and dual OOD. Model rankings are similar across the two chemical shifts (tau = 0.79), but agreement with scaffold OOD falls under protein OOD (tau = 0.39) and reverses under dual OOD (tau = -0.55). Held-out evaluation, repeated seeds, group-aware bootstrap analysis, and a size-matched control support the same conclusion: architecture selection depends on the form of extrapolation, not only on average error or training-set size. DTA benchmarks should therefore match the chemical and target shifts expected at deployment.

</details>


### [128] [Metropolis-Hastings Dominates Importance Resampling for Policy Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03480)
*Alexey Kurennoy,Ramil Yarullin,Fergal Reid*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文分析了基于独立性Metropolis-Hastings（MH）的迭代校正方法，用于在解码时组合多个奖励策略，以调整权衡而不需重新训练。主要证明在相同rollout预算下，MH的输出分布比采样-重要性重采样（SIR）更接近目标分布（以任意凸f-散度衡量）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练LLM常需权衡多个奖励，但针对每个权衡重新训练代价高昂。解码时策略组合可在推理时通过合并奖励特定策略来调整权衡，但标准实现直接合并下一词元概率，会引入采样偏差。

Method: 分析一种已知的基于独立性Metropolis-Hastings（MH）的迭代校正方法。理论推导MH与SIR在凸f-散度下的逼近程度比较，并推导MH相对于未校正解码器在共识目标上的改进下界，以及两种渐近情形下的采样误差。实验覆盖可枚举设置和LLM规模设置。

Result: 对于任意rollout预算，MH产生的输出分布以所有凸f-散度衡量至少与同预算SIR一样接近目标分布；给出了MH在共识目标上相对于未校正解码器的改进下界；刻画了奖励策略趋于一致和长响应下对数比波动增大两种渐近情形下的采样误差。

Conclusion: MH校正在分布逼近和共识目标上优于SIR和未校正解码器，且其采样误差在特定渐近条件下可被刻画，为解码时多奖励策略组合提供了理论保证。

Abstract: Post-training a large language model (LLM) often requires exploring trade-offs between multiple rewards, but retraining for each trade-off is expensive. Decoding-time policy composition allows these trade-offs to be adjusted by combining reward-specific policies at inference time. This composition targets a weighted product of the policies' probabilities over complete responses, but standard implementations combine their next-token probabilities, generally introducing sampling bias. We analyze a known iterative correction based on independence Metropolis-Hastings (MH). Our main result shows that, for every rollout budget, MH produces an output distribution at least as close to the target as sampling-importance-resampling (SIR) with the same budget, as measured by every convex f-divergence. We also derive a lower bound on MH's improvement over the uncorrected decoder in a consensus objective measuring agreement with the supplied policies. We further characterize the correction's sampling error in two asymptotic regimes: when the reward-specific policies approach agreement, and when the log ratio between target and uncorrected-decoder probabilities fluctuates increasingly widely, as can happen for long responses. We complement our analysis with experiments in enumerable and LLM-scale settings.

</details>


### [129] [ZeroMAG: Zero-Shot Multimodal Adapter Generation for Plug-and-Play EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03546)
*Yubo Wang,Jingying Ma,Xinliang Zhou,Yangxuan Zhou,Jiquan Wang,Sha Zhao,Yiyuan Yang,Yi Ding,Ziyu Jia,Chenyu Liu,Cuntai Guan*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: ZeroMAG是一个零样本多模态适配器生成框架，利用无标签目标记录扩展冻结的EEG基础模型，无需目标标签或目标侧优化即可融合伴随生理信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG记录常伴随其他生理信号，可提供互补信息，但现有EEG基础模型仅支持EEG接口，如何在无目标标签情况下保留预训练知识并扩展至异构多模态记录是核心挑战。

Method: 提出ZeroMAG：围绕配置不变的适配器组织伴随模态，从无标签记录与任务上下文构建模态-被试-任务条件，在从源适配器学习到的函数约束潜空间中生成适配器权重；目标数据集完全留出。

Result: 在六个留出目标数据集和三种EEG基础模型骨干上，ZeroMAG较仅EEG推理提升平衡准确率7.22个百分点，较直接权重回归提升4.89个百分点，平均仅比有监督多模态适配低0.50个百分点。

Conclusion: 消融实验表明，去除表示学习或条件生成中的函数监督均会降低生成适配器性能，证实两个组件的贡献；ZeroMAG能有效实现零样本多模态适配。

Abstract: EEG foundation models (EFMs) capture reusable knowledge from large-scale EEG data, while many EEG recordings also include companion physiological signals that provide complementary information beyond the EEG-only interface. The challenge is to preserve this pretrained knowledge while extending the EFM to heterogeneous multimodal recordings through an adaptation inferred from unlabeled target data. We introduce ZeroMAG, a zero-shot multimodal adapter generation framework that extends a frozen EEG encoder and prediction head using unlabeled target recordings, without target labels or target-side optimization. The target datasets are held out from all model training and selection in the ZeroMAG pipeline. ZeroMAG organizes companion modalities around a configuration-invariant adapter, constructs a modality-subject-task condition from unlabeled recordings and task context, and generates adapter weights in a function-constrained latent space learned from source adapters. Across six held-out target datasets and three EFM backbones, ZeroMAG improves balanced accuracy by 7.22 percentage points over EEG-only inference and 4.89 points over direct weight regression, while coming within 0.50 points of supervised multimodal adaptation on average. Ablations further show that removing functional supervision from either representation learning or conditional generation degrades generated-adapter performance, confirming the contribution of both components.

</details>


### [130] [Cephalonauts One: A deep fMRI dataset for decoding naturalistic speech in the human brain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03558)
*Antoine Collas,Louis Jalouzot,Géraud Ilinca,Corentin Caris,Romain Valabrègue,Ahmed Hassayoune,David Goncalves,Madeleine Hueber,Thaddée Delebarre,Julien Savatovsky,Clara Fonteneau,Charles Maussion,Bertrand Thirion,Alexis Thual*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 发布首个基于3T fMRI的深度自然语音数据集Cephalonauts One，含每被试30小时数据，并提供音频段检索解码基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有fMRI数据集在自然语音刺激下的数据深度不足，难以支持大规模脑解码研究。

Method: 3名健康被试聆听母语播客，每人多次扫描，每次5个15分钟run；采集全脑3T fMRI，配对音频、转录注释和刺激嵌入；构建音频段检索解码基准，提供标准划分、评估指标和基线解码器。

Result: 每被试30小时fMRI数据，为当前基于自然语音刺激的最深fMRI数据集；缩放分析显示解码性能随训练数据量持续提升。

Conclusion: 该数据集与基准为自然语音脑解码研究提供了深层资源和标准化评估框架，且性能随数据量增加而改善。

Abstract: Cephalonauts One is a whole-brain 3 Tesla (3T) functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) dataset recorded while subjects listened to audio podcasts. Three healthy subjects underwent multiple scanning sessions, each consisting of five 15-minute runs, while listening to podcasts in their native language. With 30 hours of fMRI data per subject, the current release is the deepest available fMRI dataset using naturalistic speech stimuli. The dataset pairs brain activity with the corresponding podcast audio, transcript annotations, and derived stimulus embeddings. Furthermore, we introduce a brain decoding benchmark formulated as audio segment retrieval: given fMRI activity from a held-out session, the decoder must identify the corresponding time-aligned podcast audio segment among candidate segments. We provide standardized splits, evaluation metrics, and baseline decoders for this task. Finally, a scaling analysis shows that decoding performance improves continuously with the amount of training data per subject.

</details>


### [131] [What Should World Models Forget? Stratified Retention for Continual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03713)
*Nishit Anand,Ramani Duraiswami,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文指出持续世界模型不应将旧知识遗忘视为失败，而应按不变性时间尺度分层保持：物理规律等必须永不变，实例级事实应随环境变化及时修正；并提出差分保持指标，联合报告不变性回归测试与修正延迟，不进行聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习传统将旧数据性能下降视为失败，源于预测目标平稳的假设；但世界模型的预测目标（环境）非平稳，旧知识过时后需要被修正而非保留；现有概念漂移与时序事实性研究未针对世界模型，且世界模型还包含必须永不变更的知识；标准遗忘指标无法区分正确修正与灾难性遗忘，会错误地将冻结模型评为最优，而现有物理推理基准仅评估冻结检查点。

Method: 提出按不变性时间尺度分层的保持策略，区分不变式（如物理、客体永久性）与实例级事实；提出差分保持（differential retention）评估方法，在适应流上联合报告不变式回归测试与修正延迟，且不做聚合。

Result: 标准遗忘指标无法区分正确修正过时知识的世界模型与遭受灾难性遗忘的模型，并因此将冻结模型排为最高；现有物理推理基准仅评估冻结检查点。

Conclusion: 持续世界模型需要按不变性时间尺度分层的保持机制，并应采用差分保持指标联合评估不变式回归与修正延迟，而非传统聚合遗忘指标。

Abstract: Continual learning treats degradation on previously seen data as evidence of failure, a convention inherited from settings with a stationary prediction target, where a correct label remains correct indefinitely. World models do not satisfy this condition. Their prediction target is the environment, which changes, so knowledge that was accurate when acquired may later become false, and discarding it is required behavior rather than a defect. Non-stationary ground truth is well studied in the concept drift literature and in the temporal factuality of language models, but has not been formulated for world models, which are distinctive in that they also encode knowledge that must never be revised. We argue that continual world models require retention stratified by invariance timescale, separating invariants such as physics and object permanence, which must never be revised, from instance-level facts that should be revised as soon as the environment changes. Standard forgetting metrics cannot distinguish a world model that has correctly revised outdated knowledge from one that has suffered catastrophic forgetting, and consequently rank a frozen model highest, while existing physical-reasoning benchmarks evaluate only frozen checkpoints. We propose differential retention, which reports invariant regression testing across the adaptation stream jointly with revision latency, without aggregation.

</details>


### [132] [IDRF: Inverse-Distilled Reward Fine-tuning of Masked Discrete Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03641)
*Vladislav Gromadskii,David Li,Samson Gourevitch,Yazid Janati,Eric Moulines,Maxim Panov,Alexander Korotin*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: IDRF是一个针对少步掩码离散扩散生成器进行奖励微调的框架，用逆向蒸馏正则替代不可解的序列级KL惩罚，实现高效奖励优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码离散扩散模型是自回归生成的有力替代，但迭代采样成本高，且序列似然不可解导致奖励微调困难。

Method: 从标准反向KL正则目标出发，将不可解的序列级KL惩罚替换为逆向蒸馏正则；证明在最优辅助去噪器下，种群逆向蒸馏损失上界参考分布的序列级KL散度；优化该损失的轨迹代理且无需参考模型 rollout，保留学生自身少步采样器；将少步生成视为有限时域马尔可夫决策过程，在学生轨迹上用裁剪策略梯度目标优化奖励。

Result: 在DNA、图像和文本生成任务中，IDRF以比参考模型少多达32倍的去噪步数获得高奖励，同时缓解奖励劫持并保持样本质量。

Conclusion: IDRF为少步掩码离散扩散生成器提供了一种有效且高效的奖励微调方法，在显著降低采样成本的同时兼顾奖励与生成质量。

Abstract: Masked discrete diffusion models offer a promising alternative to autoregressive generation, but iterative sampling can be costly, and intractable sequence likelihoods complicate reward fine-tuning. We introduce IDRF, a framework for reward fine-tuning of few-step masked discrete diffusion generators. Starting from a standard reverse-KL-regularized objective, IDRF replaces the intractable sequence-level KL penalty with inverse-distillation regularization. With an optimal auxiliary denoiser, we prove that the population inverse-distillation loss upper-bounds the sequence-level KL divergence to the reference distribution. IDRF optimizes a trajectory-based surrogate of this loss without reference-model rollouts, so the student keeps its own few-step sampler. We view few-step generation as a finite-horizon Markov decision process and optimize reward with a clipped policy-gradient objective over the student's trajectories. Across DNA, image, and text generation, IDRF achieves high reward with up to $32\times$ fewer denoising steps than the reference while mitigating reward hacking and preserving sample quality.

</details>


### [133] [When May a Bandit Leave Its Anchor? E-Process-Authorized Thompson Sampling under Non-stationarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03646)
*Mayand Gulati,Kerong Wang,WeiChen Au*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出e-ATS算法，用e-process授权折扣状态并配合可逆相关性评分控制其影响，以决定何时允许遗忘历史。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平稳环境下记忆有益，但环境变化后同一历史可能误导，因此需要判断何时允许遗忘。

Method: e-process-authorized Thompson sampling (e-ATS)为每个臂维护全历史和折扣Beta状态；先用随时有效的e-process授权折扣状态，再由可逆相关性评分控制其影响；未授权前完全遵循乐观Thompson采样(OTS)；在Beta-Bernoulli先验预测平稳模型下无需拟合阈值。

Result: 在平稳模型下，e-ATS偏离OTS的概率至多等于所选α_E；相比e-ATS，移除授权使注册套件上的平均归一化动态伪后悔增加38.4%，但在文献衍生的回放套件上降低7.5%。

Conclusion: 证据控制的是自适应何时开始，而非自适应是否总能带来收益。

Abstract: Stationarity rewards memory, but after a change the same history can mislead. We ask when forgetting should be permitted. E-process-authorized Thompson sampling (e-ATS) gives each arm full-history and discounted Beta states. An anytime-valid e-process first authorizes the discounted state, then a reversible relevance score controls its influence. Before authorization, e-ATS exactly follows optimistic Thompson sampling (OTS). Under a Beta-Bernoulli prior-predictive stationary model, e-ATS's probability of ever departing from OTS is at most the chosen $α_E$, without fitted thresholds. Relative to e-ATS, removing authorization increased mean normalized dynamic pseudo-regret by $38.4\%$ on the registered suite but reduced it by $7.5\%$ on the literature-derived replay suite. Therefore, evidence controls when adaptation begins, not whether it always helps.

</details>


### [134] [Forecasting from Counterfactual Simulator Rollouts: A Sim2Real Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03662)
*Angel Wang,Dominique Perrault-Joncas,Alvaro Maggiar,Dean Foster,Carson Eisenach*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 该论文研究新决策策略部署下的预测冷启动问题，通过仿真生成反事实数据训练预测模型，并在两个真实库存控制场景中验证Sim2Real迁移与在线校准的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新策略部署时，目标变量依赖于策略动作，而历史数据反映旧策略、新策略真实观测尚未获得，导致预测模型面临冷启动问题。

Method: 利用仿真器在新策略下滚动生成反事实轨迹，训练预测模型，并从仿真保真度、零样本迁移到真实行为、以及真实目标策略观测积累后的自适应三个角度进行回测评估；使用两个真实库存控制部署案例。

Result: 仿真训练预测器的点估计MAPE低于在历史真实数据上训练的相同架构，Study 1降低1.2-3.1个百分点，Study 2降低12.5-18.7个百分点；部署后利用早期真实观测进行轻量校准可进一步降低误差最多2.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 仿真生成的反事实数据可支持新策略下的冷启动预测，且随着真实部署数据积累，模型可进一步优化。

Abstract: Deploying a new decision policy creates a cold-start problem for prediction models whose targets depend on the policy's actions: historical observations reflect earlier policies, while real observations under the new policy are not yet available. Simulation offers a way to address this gap by rolling out the target policy across counterfactual scenarios and using the resulting trajectories to learn how the system responds to those controls. The simulation-to-reality (Sim2Real) transfer of this simulator-trained model can then be backtested by evaluating it against real observations from past deployments. Using two real-world inventory-control deployments, we evaluate this process from three angles: simulator fidelity, zero-shot transfer to real behavior, and adaptation as real target-policy observations accumulate. The simulator-trained forecaster achieves lower point-estimate mean absolute percentage error (MAPE) than the same architecture trained on historical real data, reducing MAPE by 1.2-3.1 percentage points in Study 1 and 12.5-18.7 points in Study 2. After deployment, lightweight calibration using early real observations further reduces error by up to 2.5 percentage points. These results provide empirical evidence that simulator-generated counterfactual data can support cold-start forecasting under a new policy, and the resulting model can be further refined as real deployment data become available.

</details>


### [135] [Pivot-SD: Efficient Self-Distillation for Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03665)
*Seo Hyun Kim,Sunwoo Hong,Younwoo Choi,Chen-Hao Chao,Se-Young Yun,Rahul G. Krishnan*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出Pivot-SD离线自蒸馏框架，仅训练高影响力承诺（pivots），用200题4次采样提升LLaDA-8B-Instruct在数学和代码基准上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型面临信用分配挑战，去噪中少数承诺大幅降低剩余掩码位置的不确定性并决定回答，但现有后训练方法未利用该信号选择训练token。

Method: 基于信息增益度量选择pivots，即衡量对剩余掩码位置不确定性降低的承诺；成功轨迹的pivots用交叉熵训练，失败轨迹的pivots用定向非似然训练，其余失败轨迹不处理。

Result: 仅用200个问题和每个4次rollout，Pivot-SD在数学和代码基准上优于全序列SFT及预算匹配的扩散RL基线，提升LLaDA-8B-Instruct性能。

Conclusion: Pivot-SD通过聚焦高影响力承诺实现高效后训练，为扩散语言模型的信用分配提供有效方案。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (dLMs) offer a promising parallel alternative to autoregressive models for complex reasoning. However, they face a distinct credit-assignment challenge, since a few commitments during denoising sharply reduce the uncertainty over the remaining masked positions and shape much of the response. Most post-training recipes for dLMs do not use this signal to decide which tokens to train on: they typically train on the final text or assign rewards to whole denoising steps, rather than selecting the individual commitments that shape the response. We introduce Pivot-SD, an efficient offline self-distillation framework that supervises only these high-impact commitments (pivots). Pivot-SD selects pivots using an information-gain metric measuring uncertainty reduction over the remaining masked positions. Pivots from successful trajectories are trained with cross-entropy, and pivots from failed trajectories with targeted unlikelihood, leaving the rest of the failed trajectory untouched. Using only 200 questions and four rollouts each, Pivot-SD improves LLaDA-8B-Instruct over full-sequence SFT and budget-matched diffusion RL baselines across math and code benchmarks.

</details>


### [136] [Planning to Learn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03667)
*Ian Osband*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文指出精确策略梯度在分类任务中因短视而劣于交叉熵，提出通过截断总误差得到的“horizon loss”，在图像分类上提升精度且对标签噪声更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 策略梯度在强化学习和LLM后训练中至关重要，但分类任务无探索、信用分配和动作采样噪声问题，且正确标签已知使策略梯度精确平滑，然而精确策略梯度仍不如交叉熵，作者旨在解释并解决这一反常现象。

Method: 将分类器视为策略，期望奖励为期望准确率；分析发现精确策略梯度是短视的，只关注当前更新收益而忽略对后续学习起点的价值。将交叉熵解释为“耐心的准确率”（log-odds以单位速度无限上升时的总误差），精确策略梯度为其零视界极限；据此提出截断剩余学习量的horizon loss，随训练结束从交叉熵向精确策略梯度过渡。

Result: 在简单分配模型中，horizon loss可证明避开两个端点的陷阱；在MNIST及ImageNet上使用ResNet-50、ResNet-101和ViT-S/16，固定学习率下horizon loss相对交叉熵提升top-1准确率，且标签噪声越大增益越明显。

Conclusion: horizon loss通过一行改动融合交叉熵与精确策略梯度，兼顾即时与未来学习价值，在分类任务中优于交叉熵，尤其适合标签噪声场景。

Abstract: Policy-gradient methods are central to modern reinforcement learning, including LLM post-training. When they struggle, the usual suspects are exploration, credit assignment and action-sampling noise. Classification has none of them. A classifier is a policy whose expected reward, its \emph{expected accuracy}, is the probability it assigns to the correct label, and because that label is known, the policy gradient is exact and smooth. Yet exact policy gradient loses to cross-entropy, even on expected accuracy. The exact gradient is myopic: it values an update only by what it buys now, but each update also sets where the next one starts, so an update's value depends on how much learning remains. Viewed this way, cross-entropy is patient accuracy, the total error an example would pay if its log-odds rose at unit speed forever, while exact policy gradient is the zero-horizon limit. Truncating this total at the learning that remains yields the horizon loss, a one-line change that moves from cross-entropy toward exact policy gradient as training runs out. In a simple allocation model, it provably escapes the trap that catches each endpoint. On MNIST and on ImageNet with ResNet-50, ResNet-101 and ViT-S/16, the horizon loss improves top-1 accuracy over cross-entropy at a flat learning rate, and the gain grows with label noise.

</details>


<div id='Model'></div>

# Model [[Back]](#toc)

### [137] [The AI Risk Observatory: What Can We Learn from AI Disclosures in Annual Reports About Societal Resilience?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02281)
*Bart Jaworski*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 该研究利用大语言模型对英国上市公司年报进行两阶段分类，评估企业AI披露信号，发现AI风险披露大幅增长但大多缺乏实质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会韧性研究需要可操作数据，核心问题是能否用LLM大规模处理年报，获取企业如何披露AI响应的有用信号。

Method: 构建可复现的两阶段分类流水线，处理1,362家英国上市公司2020-2025年（含2026部分数据）的9,821份年报；先用474个人工标注段落验证方法，再提出实质性分类评估披露质量。

Result: 验证显示高召回率与中等标签级一致性；2020-2025年AI风险提及率从2.8%升至41.2%，AI采用披露从13.8%升至45.2%，供应商提及集中于以微软为首的主要厂商；披露因关键国家基础设施行业和市场板块而异，AIM报告AI风险披露率远低于主板，能源和数据基础设施行业滞后；损害披露几乎缺失（全语料仅7份）；2025年41.2%报告提及AI风险，但仅4.3%为实质性披露。

Conclusion: 年报经LLM大规模处理可提供企业AI披露的有用信号，但多数AI风险披露缺乏实质性，需借助实质性分类区分披露质量。

Abstract: Societal resilience research relies on access to useful and actionable data, which motivates our main research question: Can annual reports, processed at scale with LLMs, provide a useful signal about how companies disclose their response to AI? We test this by applying a reproducible two-stage classification pipeline to 9,821 annual reports from 1,362 UK listed companies (2020-2025, with partial 2026 data). We first validate the method against 474 human-annotated passages, finding high recall and moderate label-level agreement. We then report three empirical patterns: (i) between 2020 and 2025, the share of reports mentioning AI risk rose from 2.8% to 41.2%, while AI adoption disclosure also rose, from 13.8% to 45.2%, and named vendor mentions cluster around a small set of major providers led by Microsoft; (ii) disclosure varies substantially by Critical National Infrastructure sector and market segment: AIM reports disclose AI risk at far lower rates than Main Market reports, and sectors such as Energy and Data Infrastructure lag behind the rest in AI risk disclosure; and (iii) harm disclosures are near-absent (seven reports across the entire corpus). We develop a substantiveness classification to assess the quality of the disclosure and find that most AI risk disclosure is not substantive: in 2025, 41.2% of all reports mention AI as a risk, but only 4.3% contain AI risk disclosure we classify as substantive.

</details>


### [138] [The Price of Greenwashing: Algorithmic Verification and Market Discipline using Conformal Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02225)
*Sourav Bose,Taoufik Bouraoui*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 该研究利用SEC财务数据与EPA设施级温室气体登记数据，通过梯度提升和Mondrian保形预测构建算法排放基线，提出CWCD指标量化企业自报排放与物理基线的偏差，并实证发现该偏差与后续市场估值（Tobin's Q）和盈利能力（ROA）显著负相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业可持续性强制要求扩展，但金融市场依赖自报排放数据导致普遍漂绿；现有文献多依赖主观ESG评级或文本情感分析，缺乏客观量化物理气候现实的计量经济学方法。

Method: 融合美国SEC财务基本面与EPA设施级温室气体登记数据，建立企业物理排放的数学保证基线；采用梯度提升架构和Mondrian保形预测，将自报数据与算法基线的差距量化为保形加权连续散度（CWCD）指标；通过横截面领先-滞后计量经济学设计评估该散度。

Result: 算法排放散度与后续市场估值（Tobin's Q）和运营盈利能力（ROA）呈严重且统计显著的负相关关系。

Conclusion: 研究证明机构资本并非对环境欺骗视而不见，而是将其定价为根本性公司管理不善的领先指标，为资产管理者和监管者大规模部署算法审计基础设施提供了定量依据。

Abstract: While corporate sustainability mandates are expanding, the systemic reliance on self-reported emissions data exposes financial markets to pervasive greenwashing. Current literature relies heavily on subjective ESG ratings or textual sentiment analysis, leaving a critical econometric gap in objectively quantifying physical climate realities. To resolve this information asymmetry, we fuse U.S. SEC financial fundamentals with facility-level EPA greenhouse gas registries to establish a mathematically guaranteed baseline of physical corporate emissions. Leveraging a gradient boosting architecture and Mondrian Conformal Prediction, we quantify the shortfall between self-reported data and this algorithmic baseline into a novel Conformal-Weighted Continuous Divergence (CWCD) metric. Evaluating this divergence via a cross-sectional lead-lag econometric design, we uncover a robust mechanism of market discipline: algorithmic emissions divergence exhibits a severe, statistically significant negative relationship with subsequent market valuation (Tobin's Q) and operational profitability (ROA). Providing definitive evidence against the market blindness hypothesis, this study proves that institutional capital actively prices environmental deception not merely as an ethical lapse, but as a leading indicator of fundamental corporate mismanagement. Ultimately, these findings provide the quantitative justification necessary for asset managers and regulators to deploy algorithmic auditing infrastructure at scale.

</details>


### [139] [Unifying Privacy Accounting: Information Equivalence and Information Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02414)
*Buxin Su,Qiaoshi Yang,Yiding Su,Chendi Wang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文在统一信息论框架下研究四类主流基于曲线的差分隐私概念，证明它们在固定输出分布对下信息等价，并量化zCDP压缩造成的损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同差分隐私概念的选择会影响隐私分析与效用，因此需要厘清这些概念之间的关系及信息损失。

Method: 建立统一信息论框架，比较(ε,δ)-DP的两个方向隐私剖面、假设检验权衡函数对、扩展隐私损失分布以及精确RDP曲线，并针对标准噪声机制量化RDP曲线与zCDP界之间的信息损失。

Result: 在RDP曲线某阶有限时，这些概念属于同一等价类；取方向隐私剖面最大值或压缩为单一zCDP参数会损失信息。该损失对高斯噪声为零，对高斯混合、Laplace、离散高斯和Poisson子采样高斯机制一般为正，并随独立组合机制数量线性增长。

Conclusion: 保留完整RDP曲线比使用zCDP更优：在相同认证隐私水平下，高斯混合噪声所需方差最多降低45%；在Poisson子采样DP-SGD中，RDP隐私会计师比zCDP会计师最多提升8.73个百分点测试准确率。

Abstract: Differential privacy (DP) admits several notions, but the choice among them may affect both privacy analysis and utility. In this paper, we consider four mainstream curve-based privacy notions within a unified information-theoretic framework. For a fixed ordered pair of output distributions, we establish information equivalence among the two directional privacy profiles of $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP, the pair of hypothesis-testing trade-off functions, and the extended privacy-loss distribution. The exact Rényi differential privacy (RDP) curve joins this equivalence class whenever it is finite at some order greater than one. Under this mild condition, choosing among these notions changes only their semantic interpretation and computational requirements. In contrast, taking the maximum of the directional privacy profiles or compressing the RDP curve into a single zero-concentrated differential privacy (zCDP) parameter can lose information. We quantify the information loss between the exact RDP curve and its zCDP bound for standard noise mechanisms. This gap is zero for Gaussian noise but generally positive for Gaussian-mixture, Laplace, discrete Gaussian, and Poisson-subsampled Gaussian mechanisms. Moreover, this gap grows linearly with the number of independently composed mechanisms. Our information-theoretic perspective has practical consequences. At the same certified privacy level, retaining the full RDP curve rather than using zCDP reduces the required noise variance by up to $45\%$ for Gaussian-mixture noise in workloads comparable in size to the American Community Survey.
