Specifically, we introduce Functional Progress Estimation (FPE) to measure each modality's update-induced function-space response and calibrate it against a loss-aligned unimodal reference, producing a comparable progress signal. Based on this signal, Functional Response Control (FRC) redistributes modality-level function-space budgets and realizes the target responses through tensor-wise learning-rate adjustment. Theoretical analysis establishes a one-step target-contraction property of FRC under bounded controller-state mismatch, and extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of FGMO across multiple multimodal benchmarks.

</details>


### [178] [Light Entropic Optimal Transport on Riemannian Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03085)
*Xavier Aramayo-Carrasco,Petr Mokrov,Alexander Korotin*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出ManifoldLightOT，一种在常见流形上直接学习核诱导熵最优传输耦合的轻量方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有熵最优传输求解器多针对欧氏空间，流形扩展有限且依赖昂贵迭代、模拟动力学或通用神经模型，未能充分利用底层几何结构。

Method: 基于EOT解的核形式，为球面、环面、SO(3)和SE(3)构建几何特定的Gibbs核及兼容的势参数化，获得闭式归一化和可直接采样的条件分布，并自然扩展到流形乘积；势参数通过蒙特卡洛估计学习目标从样本直接优化。

Result: 在合成和真实世界实验中，ManifoldLightOT常优于现有流形OT方法，同时保持直接采样能力。

Conclusion: 该工作为常见流形及流形乘积上的熵最优传输提供了一种轻量、可直接采样且性能优越的学习框架。

Abstract: Entropic Optimal Transport (EOT) has become a practical framework for learning stochastic couplings between complex distributions, with applications in generative modeling and domain adaptation. However, most EOT solvers are designed for Euclidean spaces, while manifold extensions remain limited and often rely on costly iterative methods, simulated dynamics, or generic neural models that do not fully exploit the underlying geometry. We introduce ManifoldLightOT, a light approach for learning kernel-induced EOT couplings directly on common manifolds. Using the kernel form of the EOT solution, we construct geometry-specific Gibbs kernels together with compatible potential parameterizations for spheres, tori, $\mathrm{SO}(3)$, and $\mathrm{SE}(3)$. These choices yield closed-form normalization and directly sampleable conditional distributions. Our formulation naturally extends to products of manifolds, making it applicable to more complex geometries. The parameters of the potentials are optimized directly from samples using Monte Carlo estimates of the learning objective. Through synthetic and real-world experiments, we show that ManifoldLightOT often outperforms existing manifold OT methods while retaining direct sampling.

</details>


### [179] [ULTRADISCOVERY: Abductive Exploration in an Interconnected, Epistemically Open Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03092)
*Weihan Li,Tianshi Zheng,Yangqiu Song,Ginny Y. Wong,Simon See*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 论文提出ULTRADISCOVERY交互式基准，以5个领域、2×2设计分离表征开放性与证据分布对溯因科学发现的影响；发现现有模型难以从证据积累过渡到可迁移的表征构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准很少将溯因探索中两个独立需求——在认识开放世界中构建解释所需的表征、以及在结构互联世界中整合分散证据——分开或独立控制，导致无法定位科学发现困难的根源。

Method: 构建ULTRADISCOVERY交互世界，包含5个领域；智能体需修正初始成功的理论并预测未见跨域干预结果。采用2×2设计：表征开放或披露、证据分散或对齐，同时固定潜在动力学。评估11个模型、两个供应商框架系统，限制200次付费动作，并比较更大预算。

Result: 表征开放时，多数模型撤回被教公理，无一引入未观测实体或重写替换所需变量；披露使干预请求增至三倍并仅增加约18项发现中的1项，对齐增益更小。两个供应商框架系统将发现扩展到更多领域，其中一个在Open情节重写变量。200次付费动作内无系统精确预测；更大预算下，双重辅助仅出现一次精确预测，所有Open情节仍不精确。

Conclusion: 困难关键在于从积累证据到将其组合成可迁移表征的步骤，而非单纯证据获取；表征开放性与证据分布对发现的影响不对称，现有系统在表征重构上存在根本局限。

Abstract: Scientific discovery often begins when scattered clues call for a new way of describing the world. Such abductive exploration can require constructing the representation in which an explanation is stated, when the world is epistemically open, and composing evidence scattered across contexts, when it is structurally interconnected. Existing benchmarks rarely separate these two demands or control them independently. We introduce ULTRADISCOVERY, an interactive world of five domains in which an agent revises an initially successful theory and predicts the outcome of an unseen cross-domain intervention. A $2 \times 2$ design leaves the representation open or discloses it, and leaves the evidence distributed or aligns it, with the latent dynamics fixed. With the representation open, agents across eleven models often retract the axiom they were taught, and none introduces the unobserved entity or rewrites the variables that a replacement requires. Disclosure triples intervention requests and adds about one of the eighteen findings the world affords, and alignment adds less. Two vendor-harness systems carry discovery into more domains, and one of them rewrites the variables in Open episodes. No system makes the exact prediction within 200 paid actions. At larger budgets one exact prediction appears with both aids, while every Open episode remains inexact. The results locate the difficulty in the step from accumulating evidence to composing it into a representation that transfers.

</details>


### [180] [Sample complexity of variance-reduced policy gradient: weaker assumptions and lower bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03165)
*Gabor Paczolay,Matteo Papini,Alberto Maria Metelli,Istvan Harmati,Marcello Restelli*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出防御性策略梯度算法（Defensive Policy Gradient），在无重要性权重方差假设下达到O(ε^{-3})样本复杂度，并建立下界证明其最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于重要性采样的方差缩减REINFORCE算法虽达到O(ε^{-3})样本复杂度，但依赖对重要性权重方差的不现实假设，需要去除该假设。

Method: 提出基于防御性重要性采样的防御性策略梯度算法；在隐藏状态动作、奖励可依赖参数的广义黑盒策略优化模型中建立下界；在标准策略正则条件下分析REINFORCE与所提算法的上界。

Result: 在黑盒模型中，有界方差单策略反馈的最优率为Θ(ε^{-4})，均方光滑耦合双策略反馈的最优率为Θ(ε^{-3})；REINFORCE和防御性策略梯度分别实现O(ε^{-4})和O(ε^{-3})上界。

Conclusion: 防御性策略梯度在无重要性权重方差假设下达到O(ε^{-3})，且下界分析提供oracle级证据表明该更快收敛率是最优的，与普通策略梯度存在本质分离。

Abstract: Several variance-reduced versions of REINFORCE based on importance sampling achieve an improved $O(ε^{-3})$ sample complexity to find an $ε$-stationary point, under an unrealistic assumption on the variance of the importance weights. In this paper, we propose the \algo (Defensive Policy Gradient) algorithm, based on defensive importance sampling, which achieves the same rate without any assumption on the variance of ordinary importance weights. We also establish lower bounds in a generalized black-box policy-optimization model that hides states and actions and permits parameter-dependent rewards. In this model, the optimal rates are $Θ(ε^{-4})$ with bounded-variance one-policy feedback and $Θ(ε^{-3})$ with mean-square-smooth coupled two-policy feedback. Under standard policy-regularity conditions, REINFORCE and \algo realize the corresponding oracle conditions and attain the $O(ε^{-4})$ and $O(ε^{-3})$ upper bounds, respectively. Although the lower bounds do not apply directly to the classical MDP interaction model in which these algorithms operate, this correspondence provides oracle-level evidence that the faster rate of \algo is optimal and genuinely separated from that of vanilla policy gradient.

</details>


### [181] [HyperFuse: Fast Self-Supervised Node Embeddings for Attributed Hypergraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03211)
*Megha P,Harshit Kumar,Srajan Agarwal,Anirban Banerjee,Olaf Wolkenhauer,Saptarshi Bej*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出无标签快速超图表示学习流程HyperFuse，在保持分类与聚类竞争力的同时实现显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督超图表示学习方法需训练深层编码器数百轮，即使数千节点超图嵌入生成代价也很高，难以支持大规模或动态演化超图的嵌入需求。

Method: HyperFuse包含三部分：(i) 基于Banerjee超图邻接与线性代价无矩阵算子，最大化超图模块度的谱松弛以计算结构节点坐标；(ii) 在特征与成员掩码下依据成员稳定性构造多尺度特征摘要，并为超边分配有界效用权重；(iii) 用不变性-去相关目标训练轻量效用加权超图编码器100轮。

Result: 在9个公开超图、6个下游分类器与k-means聚类上对比TriCL、SE-HSSL、VilLain、HypeBoy；8个全部方法完成的数据集上平均8.7秒/数据集，较基线几何平均加速13-179倍；6个分类器中5个取得最高平均准确率，分类与聚类性能与TriCL、SE-HSSL无显著差异；相比HypeBoy快13倍且所有分类器准确率高2.1-4.1个百分点。

Conclusion: HyperFuse为快速、可重复的超图嵌入生成提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Self-supervised hypergraph representation learning can produce informative node embeddings, but existing methods often require deep encoders trained for hundreds of epochs, making embedding generation costly even for hypergraphs with a few thousand nodes. This limits applications requiring embeddings for many or evolving hypergraphs. We present HyperFuse, a label-free pipeline for fast hypergraph representation learning. HyperFuse (i) computes structural node coordinates by maximizing a spectral relaxation of hypergraph modularity using Banerjee's hypergraph adjacency and a matrix-free operator with cost linear in node-hyperedge incidences; (ii) constructs multi-scale feature summaries and assigns bounded utility weights to hyperedges based on member stability under feature and membership masking; and (iii) trains a lightweight utility-weighted hypergraph encoder for 100 epochs using an invariance-decorrelation objective. We compare HyperFuse with TriCL, SE-HSSL, VilLain, and HypeBoy on nine public hypergraphs using six downstream classifiers and k-means clustering. On the eight datasets where all methods completed, HyperFuse required 8.7 s per dataset on average, achieving 13-179x geometric-mean speed-ups over the baselines. It achieved the highest average accuracy with five of six classifiers, while classification and clustering performance was not significantly different from TriCL and SE-HSSL. Compared with HypeBoy, HyperFuse was 13x faster and 2.1-4.1 percentage points more accurate across all classifiers. HyperFuse provides a practical approach for fast, repeated hypergraph embedding generation.

</details>


### [182] [Kernel Singular Value Decomposition with Extension to Multiple Data Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03216)
*Xinjie Zeng,Qinghua Tao,Johan Suykens*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出了扩展核奇异值分解（eKSVD），将KSVD从单一非对称核矩阵推广到多数据源，通过联合非线性特征学习实现多源信息融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KSVD仅能处理由两个数据源诱导的非对称核矩阵，无法应对多个数据源的联合特征学习需求，因此需要扩展以捕获多源间的成对耦合与最大信息。

Method: 在原始形式中联合学习各数据源的投影并引入成对耦合；利用拉格朗日乘子与KKT条件推导对偶优化，得到Lanczos分解定理中移位特征值问题的推广；进一步导出协方差框架，并用神经网络实现显式特征映射。

Result: 数值实验表明，eKSVD在处理多数据源时优于基于Mercer核的方法，且神经网络部署为核方法提供了极大灵活性。

Conclusion: eKSVD成功将KSVD推广至多数据源，兼具核方法与神经网络的优势，为多源非线性特征学习提供了有效且灵活的框架。

Abstract: Kernel Singular Value Decomposition (KSVD) learns a pair of singular vectors w.r.t. an asymmetric kernel matrix, which can be induced by two data sources, e.g., the queries and keys in self-attention or the rows and columns of a given matrix. In this work, we extend KSVD to multiple data sources, namely eKSVD, which conducts joint nonlinear feature learning upon asymmetric kernels. In the primal formulation, the projections associated with each data source are jointly learned to capture maximal information, while incorporating pair-wise couplings. With the Lagrangian and its Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) conditions, the optimization in the dual leads to a generalization of the shifted eigenvalue problem in Lanczos decomposition theorem of KSVD. Further, a covariance-based framework is derived together with using neural networks (NNs) for explicit feature mappings, complementary to the kernel-based interpretation and optimization. Numerical experiments verify the effectiveness of our eKSVD compared to methods based on Mercer kernels for tackling multiple data sources, and our innovation of deploying NNs demonstrates great flexibility for kernel methods.

</details>


### [183] [The Neuro-Physical Inverter: A Modular Framework for Magnetotelluric Inversion Coupling Ensemble Conditioning with Residual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03225)
*Jae Deok Kim,Sai Ravela,Rob. L. Evans*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出神经-物理反演器（NPI）框架，将集合条件化与约束残差学习耦合，用于地球物理反演，并以一维大地电磁（MT）作为受控测试平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统地球物理反演在不确定性量化与物理解约束间难以兼顾，需要一种模块化、不确定性感知的框架，既能降低反演误差又能保持集合离散度，且可扩展至高维参数化。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段用集合条件高斯过程（EnsCGP）将先验电阻率模型集合条件化于观测响应，生成物理可行的参考集合；第二阶段用残差学习神经网络预测对参考集合的定向修正，基于合成数据训练，并通过物理耦合目标函数逐台站微调用于野外数据。集合在两阶段均被条件化、细化与传播，因此每个估计都携带集合离散度。

Result: 合成实验表明NPI系统性降低集合均值误差且不破坏集合稳定性；应用于美国内华达州Gabbs Valley地热区宽带MT数据，NPI降低了中周期频段跨台站平均拟合差，同时保持相当的集合离散度；传播集合给出的不确定性因子可作为假定模型类内约束程度的操作度量。

Conclusion: NPI两阶段公式具有维度无关性，所建立的设计原则有望扩展至更高维参数化，为不确定性感知的地球物理反演提供可扩展框架。

Abstract: We present the Neuro-Physical Inverter (NPI), a modular, uncertainty-aware framework for geophysical inversion that couples ensemble-based conditioning with constrained residual learning, demonstrated in the 1D magnetotelluric (MT) setting as a controlled testbed. The framework operates in two stages. An Ensemble-Conditional Gaussian Process (EnsCGP) conditions a prior ensemble of resistivity models on the observed response, producing a physically admissible reference ensemble. A residual-learning neural network then predicts targeted corrections to this reference, trained on synthetic data and fine-tuned per station for field application through a physics-coupled objective. Because an ensemble is conditioned, refined, and propagated through both stages, every estimate carries an associated ensemble spread. Synthetic experiments show that NPI systematically reduces ensemble-mean error without destabilizing the ensemble. Applied to broadband MT data from the Gabbs Valley geothermal region (Nevada, USA), NPI reduces the across-station mean misfit over the mid-period band while retaining comparable ensemble spread. The propagated ensemble yields a factor of uncertainty that serves as an operational measure of constraint within the assumed model class. Both stages are dimension-agnostic in formulation, and the design principles established here are intended to scale to higher-dimensional parameterizations.

</details>


### [184] [D2K-Bench: Can LLM Agents Turn Expert Designs into Efficient GPU Kernels?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03226)
*Daifeng Li,Huiqiang Jiang,Chengruidong Zhang,Wei Wu,Xudong Guo,Jianhong Tu,Jianwei Zhang,Binhang Yuan,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出诊断基准 D2K-Bench，量化大模型智能体将专家设计指导转化为高效 GPU 内核的能力，发现指导显著提升正确率与性能但仍有设计属性未被实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体生成的 GPU 内核效率往往低于专家实现，仅凭运行时间无法揭示性能差距与设计发现、实现之间的关系，因此需要一个能区分设计翻译与实现能力的诊断性评测。

Method: 构建包含 26 个任务、85 个工作负载的 D2K-Bench，将专家指导分为 L1 高层算法洞见、L2 数据流设计、L3 底层优化技巧三个层级并考虑层级依赖；采用有/无指导的配对实验，保持任务描述、负载、工具、硬件与 350 轮预算一致，并辅以独立设计方案与生成代码中已实现设计属性的评估。

Result: 在 NVIDIA B200 上测试五个模型、130 个模型-任务对，指导使正确率从 93.1% 提升至 98.5%，全部 26 个任务的 Performance Score 从 1.46 升至 1.95；三个前沿模型（GPT-6-Astra、Claude-Opus-4.8、GPT-5.6-Sol）几何平均加速比从 1.69× 升至 2.49×；五个模型平均综合实现得分从 57 升至 70（满分 100）。

Conclusion: 专家设计指导对 GPU 内核生成的正确性与性能具有显著价值，同时揭示了部分设计属性仍未被模型实现，为后续改进指明方向。

Abstract: GPU kernels generated by large language model (LLM) agents can remain less efficient than expert implementations, but runtime alone does not reveal how the gap relates to design discovery and implementation. We introduce D2K-Bench, a diagnostic benchmark of 26 tasks and 85 workloads that measures how effectively agents translate expert design guidance into efficient GPU kernels. The guidance covers L1: high-level algorithmic insights, L2: dataflow design, and L3: low-level optimization tricks, including dependencies among these levels. Pairwise runs with and without guidance share task descriptions, workloads, tools, hardware, and a 350-turn budget. Complementary assessments examine independently proposed designs and the design properties implemented in generated code. Across five models on NVIDIA B200 GPUs, guidance raises correctness over 130 model-task pairs from 93.1% to 98.5% and increases the Performance Score over all 26 tasks from 1.46 to 1.95. For the three frontier models with correct submissions on all 26 tasks in both runs (GPT-6-Astra, Claude-Opus-4.8, and GPT-5.6-Sol), geometric mean speedup increases from $1.69\times$ to $2.49\times$. Across all five models, the mean combined implementation score increases from 57 to 70 out of 100. These results show the value of expert design guidance while identifying design properties that remain unimplemented.

</details>


### [185] [Cross-cohort TB classification using clinical data gathered in Uganda and South Africa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03256)
*Joshua M. Jansen van Vüren,Devendra S. Parihar,Daphne Naidoo,Marisa Klopper,Frank Cobelens,Lutz Kolbe,Kimsey Zajac,Willy Ssengooba,Moses Joloba,Grant Theron,Thomas R. Niesler*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本研究首次评估机器学习结合患者临床与人口学数据用于结核病筛查，在CAGE-TB数据集上比较了逻辑回归、多层感知机和卷积神经网络，并采用贪心特征选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别可从昂贵分子检测中获益的结核病疑似患者，以优化筛查流程并降低成本。

Method: 基于南非和乌干达社区中心的CAGE-TB数据集，采用三种神经网络架构（LR、MLP、CNN）与贪心特征选择，并为CNN提出联合优化特征选择与排序的策略。

Result: 特征选择使开发集AUROC提升2-7%；LR在留出乌干达和南非数据上AUROC分别为0.8和0.84；MLP和CNN在开发集优于LR但留出集趋势不一致，LR性能与最佳模型差距1-2%，敏感性距WHO最低要求差4-9%。

Conclusion: 基于神经网络的结核病筛查分类器具有可行性，但深层网络泛化性需进一步验证，LR表现稳健且接近临床要求。

Abstract: We present a first evaluation of machine learning applied to patient clinical and demographic data gathered in two different countries for the purpose of tuberculosis (TB) screening to identify people who would benefit from expensive molecular testing. Experiments are based on the recently-compiled CAGE-TB dataset, which includes sub-cohorts of people with presumptive TB presenting at community health care centres in South Africa and Uganda. Three neural network architectures (logistic regression (LR), multilayer perceptrons (MLP) and convolutional neural networks (CNN)) are considered in conjunction with greedy feature selection. For the convolutional neural network, a strategy that jointly optimises feature selection and feature ordering is proposed and shown to lead to consistent development and test set improvements. For all three models, development set area under the receiver operating characteristic (AUROC) curve is improved by 2-7% using feature selection. LR after feature selection achieves an AUROC of 0.8 [0.75,0.86] (95% CI) and 0.84 [0.78,0.9] when testing on the held-out Ugandan and South African data respectively. Although outperforming LR on the development cohort, the deeper networks (MLP, CNN) show inconsistent trends on the held-out cohorts, while LR achieves performance within 1-2% of the best achieved in terms of AUROC. LR narrowly misses the WHO minimum requirements by 4-9% in sensitivity even though the network is being evaluated on a completely held-out cohort. The development of neural-network based classifiers for TB screening therefore appears viable.

</details>


### [186] [Mapping and Advancing the Scalability-Accuracy Frontier of Nonlinear Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03258)
*Hendrik Suhr,Sascha Xu,Jilles Vreeken*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了可扩展非线性因果发现的四类主流方法，并针对组合搜索中重复局部评分的瓶颈提出SPADE，一种基于样条的可复用充分统计量评分方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展非线性因果发现需兼顾灵活机制估计与大图空间高效搜索，但现有方法在精度与运行时间上的权衡尚不清晰，尤其是组合搜索受重复冗余局部评分拖累。

Method: 实证对比可微分结构学习、摊销结构学习、分数匹配和组合搜索四类方法；提出SPADE，通过一次性编译充分统计量并在组合搜索中复用，其高斯变体在有界入度下将复杂度从O(nd^3)降至O(nd^2+d^3)。

Result: 可微分与摊销方法扩展性好但存在精度差距；分数匹配在低维准确但随特征增加迅速退化；组合方法准确但受重复评分拖慢。SPADE在秒级求解100变量/160K样本问题、分钟级求解1600变量/2.5K样本问题，并保持高结构精度。

Conclusion: SPADE显著推进了组合搜索的实际可扩展规模，表明评估可扩展因果发现方法应关注完整的精度-运行时间前沿。

Abstract: Scalable nonlinear causal discovery requires methods that combine flexible mechanism estimators with efficient search over large graph spaces. Several algorithmic families have been proposed to address this challenge, yet their accuracy-runtime trade-offs remain poorly understood. We empirically compare the four major approaches: differentiable structure learning, amortized structure learning, score-matching, and combinatorial search. Our results reveal complementary bottlenecks: differentiable and amortized methods scale well but exhibit an accuracy gap, score-matching methods can be accurate in low dimensions but degrade quickly for increasing feature sizes, and combinatorial methods remain accurate but are slowed by repeated and redundant local scoring. Motivated by this bottleneck, we develop SPADE, a spline-based score-evaluation scheme that compiles sufficient statistics once and reuses them throughout combinatorial search. Under bounded indegree, its Gaussian variant reduces algorithmic complexity from O(nd^3) to O(nd^2+d^3). Empirically, SPADE shifts the observed scalability-accuracy frontier by orders of magnitude: it solves 100-variable problems with 160K samples in seconds and 1600-variable problems with 2.5K samples in minutes, while retaining high structural accuracy across synthetic and real-world benchmarks. These results reveal a substantial shift in the practical scale of combinatorial search and highlight the importance of evaluating scalable causal-discovery methods along the full accuracy-runtime frontier.

</details>


### [187] [Causal Representation Learning with Instantaneous and Lagged Relations via Nonstationarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03452)
*Tatsuya Yamada,Hiroshi Morioka,Yoshinobu Kawahara*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文研究时间序列数据的因果表示学习，旨在从观测中识别潜在状态及其因果结构，同时处理滞后因果、瞬时因果和非平稳性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以联合处理时间序列中的滞后因果、区间内瞬时因果以及非平稳性，且缺乏可识别性保证。

Method: 利用与转移噪声分布变化相关的辅助变量（如时间或条件标签），建立潜在状态及瞬时/滞后因果结构的可识别性条件；提出iCReN框架，通过对比学习处理离散或连续辅助变量，学习潜在表示并估计因果结构。

Result: 在合成数据上准确恢复潜在状态以及瞬时和滞后因果结构；在真实数据上学习到的表示对下游预测任务具有实用价值。

Conclusion: iCReN能够在非平稳时间序列中联合识别潜在状态与瞬时、滞后因果结构，并提升下游预测性能。

Abstract: Causal representation learning for time-series data aims to identify latent states and their causal relations from observations. In this setting, an important challenge is to model both lagged causal relations across observation intervals and faster causal effects that appear as instantaneous relations within an interval, while accounting for nonstationarity in time-series data. However, methods that jointly handle these causal relations and nonstationarity remain limited. To address this gap, we establish sufficient conditions for identifying latent states up to component permutation and component-wise invertible transformations, and their instantaneous and lagged causal structures up to the same permutation, using an observed auxiliary variable, such as time or a condition label, associated with changes in transition-noise distributions. Based on these results, we propose iCReN, a framework that uses contrastive learning with discrete or continuous auxiliary variables to learn latent representations and estimate their instantaneous and lagged causal structures. Experiments demonstrate accurate recovery of latent states and both instantaneous and lagged causal structures on synthetic data and the utility of the learned representations for downstream forecasting on real-world data.

</details>


### [188] [Dual-Context Analog Retrieval for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03491)
*Jung Min Choi,Ngoc Son Le,Ibram Abdelmalak,Vijaya Krishna Yalavarthi,Lars Schmidt-Thieme*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出DuoTS模型，通过双上下文（当前上下文与细节上下文）逐未来片段精炼预测，结合检索类比证据提升长期时间序列预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期时间序列预测模型单次映射整个预测范围，未显式识别历史状态与不同未来片段的相关性，也未利用这些状态后续轨迹；单纯类比预测的最近邻匹配不可靠，重叠片段易产生冗余候选。

Method: DuoTS先通过并行片段编码器和线性预测头生成基础预测，再逐未来片段精炼：每个精炼步骤融合当前上下文（关注近期token捕捉最新动态）和细节上下文（提供不同检索类比及其后续轨迹），实现跨预测范围平衡两视角并关联合适证据。

Result: 在多个真实数据集上，DuoTS达到最先进性能；消融实验证实各上下文的贡献；精炼机制与模型无关，仅需编码的回看窗口和未来片段位置，可集成到现有预测模型。

Conclusion: DuoTS通过双上下文逐片段精炼有效利用检索证据，提升长期时间序列预测，且精炼机制通用可扩展。

Abstract: Most long-term time-series forecasting models map the look-back window directly to the full horizon in a single pass. While efficient, this design does not explicitly identify which historical states are most relevant to different future segments or exploit what followed those states. Analog forecasting addresses this by retrieving past states similar to the present and using their observed continuations, but single nearest matches can be unreliable and overlapping patches may produce redundant candidates. We propose DuoTS, a Dual-Context Time Series forecasting model that uses retrieved evidence without relying on it exclusively. DuoTS first produces a base forecast with a parallel patch encoder and linear prediction head, then progressively refines it one future patch at a time. Each refinement combines two views: a current context that attends to recent tokens and captures the latest dynamics, and a detail context that provides distinct retrieved analogs together with their subsequent trajectories. Patch-wise refinement allows the model to balance these views across the forecast horizon and associate each future segment with evidence appropriate to its temporal distance from the present. Experiments on multiple real-world datasets show that DuoTS achieves state-of-the-art performance, while ablations confirm the contribution of each context. The refinement mechanism is also model-agnostic, requiring only an encoded look-back window and the future-patch position, and can therefore be integrated into existing forecasting models.

</details>


### [189] [Most-Recent Anchoring with Recurrent Ordering for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03494)
*Jung Min Choi,Ngoc Son Le,Ibram Abdelmalak,Vijaya Krishna Yalavarthi,Lars Schmidt-Thieme*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出MARO模型，通过从最近到最旧的递归顺序处理回看窗口，以最近补丁为锚点，实现长期时间序列预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期预测模型对回看窗口内所有补丁使用相同的固定计算栈，忽略了近期证据与遥远上下文对预测贡献的差异。

Method: MARO采用最近锚定与递归顺序，从最近补丁扫描到最旧补丁；最近补丁初始化潜在状态并条件化每一步，共享模块复用不增加参数，保留中间状态供预测头分别加权短长期历史。

Result: 在多个真实世界时间序列数据集上，MARO在长期和短期预测任务中均达到最先进性能，消融实验验证了主要架构组件的贡献。

Conclusion: 通过递归细化顺序表达近期性，MARO有效区分近期与遥远信息，且不增加参数，在长期和短期预测中均表现优异。

Abstract: Long-term forecasting models commonly process all patches in a look-back window using the same fixed stack. Older contextual patches and recent evidence therefore receive the same computational depth. Yet the information closest to the forecast and the more distant context do not contribute equally. Uniform processing leaves this distinction unexpressed in the architecture. We propose MARO, a Most-Recent Anchoring with Recurrent Ordering model that processes the look-back window from the most recent patch to the oldest. The most recent patch serves as the anchor. It initializes the latent state and conditions each subsequent step, so older patches are folded into a representation that remains centered on recent evidence. A single shared module is reused at every step, so extending the scan further into the past introduces no additional parameters. Intermediate states retained during the scan allow the forecast head to weigh short and long portions of the history separately. This expresses recency through the order of recurrent refinement. Extensive experiments across multiple real-world time series datasets show that MARO achieves state-of-the-art performance on both long-term and short-term forecasting tasks.Ablation studies examine the contribution of the main architectural components.

</details>


### [190] [An Automated and Reproducible Workflow for Crack Identification and Damage Assessment of Fusion Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03505)
*Rinkle Juneja,Viktor Reshniak,Richard K. Archibald,John W. Duggan,Gregory R. Watson,Cory D. Hauck,Gary M. Staebler*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出并实现了基于Galaxy工作流的自动化SEM图像裂纹识别与损伤评估方法，在钨材料数据集上验证其跨条件适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚变材料辐照后显微分析是材料认证的关键，但手动分析无法应对现代聚变材料实验数据的规模、异质性和多分辨率特性，亟需可复现的自动化流程。

Method: 在Galaxy科学工作流环境中实现自动化工作流，处理SEM图像和实验元数据，识别裂纹、量化损伤，并保留中间产物和处理历史以保证可复现性；方法设计为无需针对图像特定参数调优，可跨钨牌号、微结构、放大倍数和损伤状态运行。

Result: 在包含418张图像、114个实验、覆盖5种钨牌号和3种微结构状态的稀疏电子束热冲击数据集上演示了该工作流；输出包括裂纹掩膜、骨架化裂纹网络、质量控制可视化和标量损伤描述符，并定义了裂纹密度描述符。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是一个端到端、可共享、计算可移植的工作流，连接了实验表征、自动图像分析、初步损伤预测和模拟引导的数据采集，为聚变材料研究提供标准化输入和可扩展模块。

Abstract: Post-exposure microscopy is central to qualification of fusion materials. However, manual analysis does not scale to the volume, heterogeneity, and multiresolution character of modern fusion-materials campaigns. To address this challenge, we present a reproducible workflow, implemented in the Galaxy scientific workflow environment, for automated crack identification and quantitative damage assessment from scanning electron microscopy images. The workflow processes SEM images and experimental metadata to identify cracks, quantify damage, and retain the intermediate products and processing history needed for reproducibility. Outputs include crack masks, skeletonized crack networks, quality-control visualizations, and scalar damage descriptors. The method is designed to operate without image-specific parameter tuning across tungsten grades, microstructures, magnifications, and damage states. We demonstrate the workflow on a sparse electron-beam thermal-shock dataset containing 418 images from 114 experiments spanning five tungsten grades and three microstructural states. We define a crack-density descriptor, which provides standardized inputs for downstream machine-learning prediction and physics-based crack simulation. These predictive components are exposed in the same Galaxy environment and are intentionally treated here as extensible workflow modules. The principal contribution is therefore an end-to-end, shareable, and computationally portable workflow that links experimental characterization, automated image analysis, preliminary damage prediction, and simulation-guided data acquisition for fusion-materials research.

</details>


### [191] [Objects Without Morphisms: What LLMs for Mathematics Do Not Represent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03551)
*Yanli Wang,Suijin Wang,Xiaopeng Yuan,Haohan Wang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 该研究考察大语言模型在无外部评判标准时进行的子领域方言间翻译，发现模型系统性地将翻译偏向一般化框架，扩大量化域而丢失源文本隐含假设，且该不对称性不受模型能力影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在竞赛数学中的表现依赖于大量采样与外部筛选，仅优化最终答案而不改变模型内部表征。作者希望探究在缺乏外部评判标准时，模型是否真正理解内容的一般性层级，因此选择相邻子领域方言间的翻译任务，其中忠实性取决于对内容断言一般性层级的恢复。

Method: 提出一种评估工具，将真值、内容与范围分别编码到独立的盲评队列中，使用无评判者（judge-free）指标衡量改写是否陈述了源文本隐含的假设，并通过植入阳性对照验证其敏感性。在来自四个模型家族的七个模型上，测试向一般化框架和具体框架两个方向的翻译。

Result: 向一般化框架翻译时，60.6%的改写扩大了量化域，无一缩小；向具体框架翻译时，28.3%缩小、0.3%扩大。防止该偏差的假设在模型改写中仅21.6%被陈述，人类陈述中仅4.2%。该不对称性在所有测试模型中出现，最强模型扩大最少。结果在预注册规则留出的基准一半及数学家撰写的陈述上可复现。指示模型陈述其所需的所有假设可提高陈述率，但不改变对翻译方向的敏感性。

Conclusion: 这些系统已获得子领域词汇间的对象级对应关系，但缺乏理论间翻译将假设映射到假设所需的约束，导致翻译偏向一般化并丢失隐含假设。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have reached expert-level performance on competition mathematics largely through the volume of search placed around them: candidate solutions are sampled in quantity and retained only when an external criterion accepts them. Such a procedure improves the outcome that survives it while leaving untouched what the model represents. We examine that question where no external criterion exists: translating statements between the dialects of neighbouring subfields, where fidelity turns on the level of generality at which content is asserted. The source leaves that level implicit in its vocabulary, so a faithful translation must recover it from the relation between the theories. We introduce an instrument that codes truth, content and scope in separate blind queues, with a judge-free measure of whether a rewrite states the hypothesis implicit in its source, and establish its sensitivity with a planted-positive control. Across seven models from four families, translating towards the general framing widens the domain of quantification in 60.6% of rewrites and narrows it in none; translating towards the concrete framing narrows it in 28.3% and widens it in 0.3%. The hypothesis that would prevent it is stated in 21.6% of model rewrites and 4.2% of human statements. Capability does not govern the asymmetry: it appears in every model tested, and the most capable widens least. It replicates on the half of the benchmark held out by a pre-registered rule, and on statements written by mathematicians. Instructing a model to state every hypothesis it requires raises that rate but not its sensitivity to direction. We argue that these systems have acquired an object-level correspondence between subfield vocabularies without the constraint under which a translation between theories carries hypotheses to hypotheses.

</details>


### [192] [RNADyn: A Benchmark for Generating and Understanding RNA Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03712)
*Yiming Huang,Lennart Bastian,Hanqun Cao,Luis Vollmers,Tolga Birdal*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出RNA分子动力学标准基准RNADynBench及统一模型RNADynNet，可同时生成轨迹并提取动力学指纹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RNA功能依赖构象变化，但静态结构无法完全刻画；大规模标准化RNA动力学数据有限，且现有方法将轨迹生成与动力学理解割裂为独立目标。

Method: 构建含2585条质控后100纳秒全原子轨迹、防泄漏划分的RNADynBench；基于该基准开发RNADynNet，采用共享骨干网络，融合坐标去噪、单帧到轨迹对齐与物理约束，统一实现轨迹生成与动力学指纹提取。

Result: 在包括高柔性挑战集的两个测试集上，生成轨迹的RMSF相关性达0.875和0.766；单构象预测与MD动力学结果具有可比一致性；物理约束同时提升生成动力学质量与指纹可恢复的物理信息。

Conclusion: RNADynBench与RNADynNet共同为RNA动力学的生成与理解建立了标准化基准和统一建模框架。

Abstract: Ribonucleic acid (RNA) functions through conformational changes that are not fully captured by static structures. However, large-scale standardized RNA dynamics data remain limited, and existing approaches typically treat trajectory generation and dynamics understanding as separate objectives. Here, we introduce RNADynBench, a standardized RNA molecular dynamics (MD) benchmark with 2585 quality-controlled 100-ns all-atom trajectories and leakage-controlled splits. Building on RNADynBench, we develop RNADynNet, a unified model for RNA dynamics learning that uses a shared backbone for both trajectory generation and dynamics fingerprint extraction from a single conformer. It combines coordinate denoising, single-frame-to-trajectory alignment, and physical grounding to connect all-atom trajectory generation with dynamics representation learning. Physical grounding improves both generated dynamics and the physical information recoverable from these fingerprints. Across both test sets, including the high-flexibility challenge set, the generated trajectories achieve RMSF correlations of 0.875 and 0.766, while single-conformer predictions show comparable agreement with MD-derived dynamics. RNADynBench and RNADynNet together establish a benchmark and unified modeling framework for generating and understanding RNA dynamics.

</details>


<div id='Sec'></div>

# Sec [[Back]](#toc)

### [193] [Fast Models, Slow Evidence: A Paired and Self-Audited Evaluation of System-1 Decision Models for LLM Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02267)
*Jiawei Li*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 该论文对两个System-1决策模型（开权重Laya和托管Jev）在11个智能体决策点上进行配对评估，并审计自身分析流程，发现多个部署声明因分析错误而被扭曲。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: System-1模型通过单次前向传播回答智能体中的小决策，有望大幅节省成本与延迟；但需要严格评估其准确性与鲁棒性，并检查评估本身的可信度。

Method: 基于18个公开来源构建11个决策点，含7,283个基础案例和6,640个鲁棒性变体；使用字节级相同输入、配对测试、跨硬件与跨天可复现性检查；对Laya与Jev进行对比，并审计分析流程中的错误与混杂因素。

Result: Jev在11个决策点中的9个显著更准确（提升10.8至46.0个百分点）；两者在零样本模型路由上均未超随机，在RAG相关性门控上持平；Laya在选项顺序反转时改变30%答案，在50个最近邻工具时准确率降至31%，而Jev在唯一正确工具项上为98%；审计发现三个分析错误和一个设计混杂扭曲了部署结论，如预筛选成本遗漏（报告节省23.9%实际4.3%）、门控准确率误报为端到端质量（58%对98%）、样本内阈值（目标5%但留出集漏报达17%）、以及通道效应消失。

Conclusion: System-1模型在部分决策点有优势，但零样本路由和RAG门控仍不可靠；Laya鲁棒性差；评估流程需严格审计以避免夸大部署收益。

Abstract: Agent harnesses make many small, typed decisions per task: which model to call, which tool to use, whether retrieved text is relevant, whether an input carries an injection. System-1 decision models answer such questions in a single forward pass with class probabilities, promising large cost and latency savings over LLM calls. We present a paired evaluation of an open-weight (Laya) and a hosted (Jev) System-1 model on 11 agent decision points built from 18 public sources: 7,283 base cases plus 6,640 robustness variants, with byte-identical inputs, paired tests, and cross-hardware and cross-day reproducibility checks. Jev is significantly more accurate on 9 of 11 decision points (+10.8 to +46.0 pp). Neither model beats chance on zero-shot model routing, and they tie on RAG relevance gating. Laya changes 30% of its answers when the option order is reversed and degrades sharply with many or similar candidates (31% at 50 nearest-neighbour tools, vs. 98% for Jev on items with a unique correct tool). We also audit our own pipeline. Three analysis errors and one design confound distorted headline deployment claims: an omitted pre-screen cost (reported 23.9% saving, actual 4.3%), gate accuracy reported as end-to-end quality (58% vs. 98%), in-sample thresholds (5% target, up to 17% held-out misses), and a "channel effect" on injection false positives that vanishes with channel-native content. Two other suspected confounds did not change the conclusions. All cases, raw outputs and analysis code are available at https://github.com/David-DL-Space/sys1-eval.

</details>


### [194] [Keep It CALM: Analyzing the Limits of Global Unsafety in Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02300)
*NaHyeon Park,Minhyun Lee,Hyunjung Shim*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出CALM，一种免训练的文生图安全防护方法，用提示局部反事实校正替代全局不安全信号去除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免训练防护依赖可复用的全局安全信号（如不安全方向或全局有害子空间），但存在覆盖度-选择性权衡：紧凑子空间无法覆盖异构不安全语义，而扩大聚合又会扭曲与安全相邻的良性提示。

Method: 通过受控几何分析揭示全局不安全假设的上述权衡；据此提出CALM，利用匹配的不安全-良性锚点，将每个提示路由到活跃的不安全类别，仅对违规token表示向安全侧做最小编辑，并抑制正向对齐的不安全残差分量。

Result: 在广泛评估中，CALM显著提升不安全内容抑制效果，同时保持良性提示的可用性。

Conclusion: 局部反事实校正比全局不安全信号去除更具选择性，是免训练文生图安全防护的有效替代方案。

Abstract: Training-free safeguards for text-to-image generation often rely on a reusable safety signal, such as an unsafe direction or global toxic subspace, applied broadly across prompts. We provide a controlled geometric analysis of this global-unsafety assumption and reveal a consistent coverage-selectivity trade-off: compact unsafe subspaces fail to cover heterogeneous unsafe semantics, whereas broader aggregation increasingly distorts safety-adjacent benign prompts. Motivated by this finding, we propose CALM (Counterfactual Adaptive Local Modulation), a training-free safeguard that replaces uniform global removal with prompt-local counterfactual correction. Using matched unsafe-benign anchors, CALM routes each prompt to active unsafe categories, minimally edits only violating token representations toward the safe side, and suppresses positively aligned unsafe residual components. Across broad evaluation, CALM significantly improves unsafe content suppression while preserving benign utility, demonstrating that local counterfactual correction provides a more selective alternative to global unsafe signal removal.

</details>


### [195] [Intent-Hiding Jailbreaks: An Information-Theoretic Framework for Compositional Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02302)
*Fengwei Tian,Ravi Tandon*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 从信息论角度研究组合式意图隐藏越狱攻击，通过先验-后验匹配选择辅助任务以掩盖有害意图，分析查询无关与查询相关两种设置，并实验验证其效果与权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型易受组合式越狱攻击，有害请求嵌入看似良性的更大查询中可能绕过拒绝。现有研究缺乏对意图隐藏机制的信息论刻画，本文旨在形式化分析该现象并量化其影响。

Method: 将每个任务关联为被判定有害的估计概率：全集平均为先验概率，含目标任务的捆绑集平均为后验概率。通过选择辅助任务使先验与后验匹配（先验-后验匹配），保持估计意图不变。研究查询无关设置（任务选择独立于最终查询表达）和查询相关设置（联合优化任务选择与查询构造）。查询无关下证明精确匹配在捆绑大小约束下计算困难，给出分数权重的最优注水解，并刻画满足安全阈值的最小捆绑；查询相关下评估意图隐藏与目标保留。

Result: 在查询无关设置中，精确先验-后验匹配是计算困难的，但通过注水法可得分数权重最优解，并确定了满足安全阈值的最小捆绑大小。实验跨捆绑大小、查询生成器和多个开源模型评估越狱效果与目标行为保留，显示组合查询在给定搜索预算下能引发超越直接请求基线的目标行为，同时揭示权衡：随着捆绑大小增加，多个模型的响应级目标保留趋于下降。

Conclusion: 组合式意图隐藏越狱可从信息论角度有效建模，先验-后验匹配能掩盖有害意图并提升攻击成功率，但捆绑大小增大会降低目标行为保留，存在效果与保留之间的权衡。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that large language models (LLMs) can be vulnerable to jailbreak attacks in which harmful intent is obscured through composition with benign tasks. A harmful request refused in isolation may elicit a different response when embedded within a larger, seemingly benign query. We study these compositional intent-hiding jailbreaks from an information-theoretic perspective. Our formulation associates each task with an estimated probability of being judged harmful: the average over the full task collection defines the prior probability of harmful intent, while the average over a selected bundle containing the target defines the posterior. Selecting auxiliary tasks so that these averages agree, which we call prior-posterior matching, leaves the estimated intent unchanged even though the harmful target remains in the bundle.
  We study two settings that differ in whether query construction is part of the optimization. In the query-independent setting, tasks are selected without regard to how they will be expressed in the final query. We show that exact prior-posterior matching under a bundle-size constraint is computationally hard, derive an optimal water-filling solution for fractional weights, and characterize the smallest bundle satisfying a prescribed safety threshold. In the query-dependent setting, task selection and query construction are considered jointly, and intent concealment and target preservation are evaluated on the resulting query. We evaluate jailbreak effectiveness and preservation of the target behavior across bundle sizes, query generators, and several open-source models. These results show that compositional queries can elicit target behaviors beyond the direct-request baseline under the evaluated search budgets, while revealing a trade-off: as bundle size increases, response-level target preservation tends to decrease for several models.

</details>


### [196] [Hybrid Machine Learning-Assisted Raman Spectroscopy with Generative Feature Augmentation for Pharmaceutical Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02224)
*Quach Thi Thai Binh,Ton Nu Quynh Trang,Thang B. Phan,Vu Thi Hanh Thu,Nguyen Tuan Hung*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出HyMLRaman混合拉曼光谱框架，结合深度特征提取、生成模型和经典机器学习分类器，实现六种药物残留的快速识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速可靠识别药物残留对公共健康、食品安全及拉曼筛查应用至关重要，但有限数据条件下性能受限。

Method: 将拉曼光谱转为光谱图像，用EfficientNet-B3提取1536维嵌入，训练SVM、KNN、逻辑回归、随机森林、XGBoost和ANN分类器（分层10折交叉验证）；在PCA降维的EfficientNet-B3潜空间引入DDPM生成模型进行特征增强。

Result: EfficientNet-B3--SVM达到96.31%准确率和96.36%宏F1，优于独立CNN；DDPM增强在低数据量下对KNN等有选择性提升，效果依赖分类器；应用级拉曼药物分析器验证了嵌入交互工作流的可行性。

Conclusion: HyMLRaman为快速、可解释的拉曼药物筛查提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Rapid and reliable identification of pharmaceutical residues is important for safeguarding public health, ensuring food safety, and enabling practical Raman-based screening. In this study, we propose HyMLRaman, a hybrid Raman spectroscopy framework that combines deep spectral feature extraction, generative models, and classical machine-learning classifiers to identify six pharmaceutical compounds, including amoxicillin, chloramphenicol, ciprofloxacin, tetracycline, ibuprofen, and paracetamol. Raman spectra are converted into spectral images and encoded with several deep neural-network backbones, among which EfficientNet-B3 yields the most effective representation. The resulting 1536-dimensional embeddings are then used to train downstream classifiers, including SVM, KNN, logistic regression, random forest, XGBoost, and ANN, using stratified 10-fold cross-validation. The hybrid EfficientNet-B3--SVM configuration achieves the strongest baseline performance, reaching 96.31% accuracy and a macro-F1 score of 96.36%, outperforming the standalone CNN baseline. To address limited-data conditions, a generative model, a DDPM-based feature augmentation, is introduced in a PCA-reduced EfficientNet-B3 latent space. The low-data ablation results show that DDPM augmentation provides selective benefits, particularly for KNN with reduced training fractions, and that its effect remains classifier-dependent. Finally, an application-level Raman Pharmaceutical Analyzer demonstrates the feasibility of embedding the trained model into an interactive Raman analysis workflow. These results suggest that HyMLRaman provides a practical and interpretable route for rapid Raman-based pharmaceutical screening.

</details>


### [197] [MIRROR: Multipath Quorum Integrity for LLM Multi-Agent Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02349)
*Ryuichi Yamafuji Lun,Jingzhen Wang,Shreyas Kolte,Ruiteng Li*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出MIRROR通信层完整性原语，通过k条逻辑路由的多数派摘要一致来抵御LLM多智能体系统中的中间人攻击，在α<0.5时ASR降至0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-MAS中智能体间通信存在Agent-in-the-Middle攻击风险，现有防御（语义验证、传输层加密）存在额外推理开销、误伤良性输出或无法应对合法终止TLS的中介等问题。

Method: MIRROR将单一规范化载荷复制到k条逻辑路由，仅当严格多数路由报告相同摘要时接受消息；使用无密钥哈希，安全性来自诚实路由占多数的假设，摘要用于使见证路由定长并绑定载荷与法定值。

Result: 在MMLU、HumanEval、MBPP上，跨两个框架和四种通信拓扑，并在MetaGPT部署中对抗生产API，α<0.5时ASR降至0%，LLM token成本为1倍；LLM-as-a-Judge成本为35倍且最多阻断44.2%良性输出。

Conclusion: MIRROR在路由妥协界α<0.5下提供完整性保证，并扩展至相关路由情形，关键量为最大共享失败组大小；可用性与完整性在同一阈值退化，低于α=0.5时法定人数拒绝和丢包攻击无法阻断诚实流量。

Abstract: Inter-agent communication is central to Large Language Model Multi-Agent Systems (LLM-MAS), but it introduces an underexplored vulnerability: Agent-in-the-Middle (AiTM) attacks that manipulate messages in transit without compromising the agents themselves. Prior work reports Attack Success Rates (ASR) approaching 100% on structured tasks. Existing defenses rely on semantic validation, which requires additional inference and can block benign outputs, or on transport-layer encryption, which does not help when an intermediary legitimately terminates TLS. We present MIRROR, a communication-layer integrity primitive that replicates a single canonicalized payload across k logical routes and accepts a message only when a strict majority of routes report the same digest. MIRROR uses unkeyed hashing and so authenticates nothing on its own, since an active on-path adversary can always recompute a digest over a payload it has modified. All integrity derives from the assumption that honest routes form a majority. The digest serves only to make witness routes constant-size and to bind the recovered payload to the quorum-agreed value under second-preimage resistance. We give the guarantee under a route-compromise bound alpha < 0.5, and extend it to correlated routes, where the quantity that matters is the size of the largest shared-failure group and not the route count. We further show that availability and integrity degrade at the same threshold: below alpha = 0.5, quorum-denial and message-dropping adversaries cannot block honest traffic. Across MMLU, HumanEval, and MBPP on two frameworks and four communication topologies, and in a MetaGPT deployment against a production API, MIRROR reduces ASR to 0% below the threshold at 1x LLM token cost. LLM-as-a-Judge costs 35x in the same deployment, and blocks up to 44.2% of benign outputs in the topology sweep.

</details>


### [198] [Hop-Decayed Influence: New Vulnerabilities of Structural Auxiliary Indexing in GraphRAG Pipelines with LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02373)
*Jisung Park,John Le,Heath Cooper*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文定义GraphRAG中辅助模式级实体这一新型攻击面，提出3S框架及HDI攻击，通过后索引篡改极少辅助结构实现高成功率攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有攻击仅针对实例级组件（节点、边、三元组），忽视了离线索引构建的模式级辅助结构（语义摘要、层次边、预计算分数），这些结构决定查询时的检索优先级，存在隐式信任与验证缺失。

Method: 形式化辅助模式级实体为攻击面，提出3S框架（语义、结构、评分）；HDI攻击利用查询感知影响传播识别高影响目标，并在索引后破坏其辅助结构。

Result: 在HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA两个基准和Microsoft GraphRAG、HippoRAG2两种架构上，HDI以仅修改0.016%辅助结构达到88-94%攻击成功率；每次修改影响多达6.00个查询（模式杠杆率），展现1:N放大效应；对困惑度与复述防御的规避率超过99%。

Conclusion: 辅助模式级实体因语言连贯的系统生成特性而获得隐式信任且无运行时验证，构成当前GraphRAG防御的结构性盲点。

Abstract: GraphRAG pipelines construct auxiliary structures during offline indexing--semantic summaries, hierarchical edges, and pre-computed scores--that determine how retrieval is prioritised at query time. Prior attacks target only instance-level components (nodes, edges, triples), overlooking these schema-level structures. We formalise Auxiliary Schema-Level Entity as a novel attack surface and propose the 3S Framework (Semantics, Structure, Scoring) for its systematic exploitation. Our Hop-Decayed Influence (HDI) attack identifies high-impact targets through query-aware influence propagation and corrupts their auxiliary structures post-indexing. Across two benchmarks (HotpotQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA) and two architectures (Microsoft GraphRAG, HippoRAG2), HDI achieves 88-94% attack success rate while modifying as few as 0.016% of auxiliary structures. Each modification affects up to 6.00 queries (Schema Leverage Ratio), demonstrating 1:N amplification unavailable to instance-level attacks. Manipulated structures evade perplexity and paraphrase defenses with over 99% evasion rate, as they remain linguistically coherent system-generated artifacts. These results reveal that auxiliary schema-level entities receive implicit trust without runtime validation, constituting a structural blind spot in current GraphRAG defenses. https://github.com/Jisung-Pacific/HDI-GraphRAG-Attack.

</details>


### [199] [A Multi Method Importance and Performance Efficiency Analysis of Topological Metrics for Natural Visibility Graph Based Cyber Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02342)
*Ali Melih Kanca,Ilker Turker*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 该研究通过共识排序策略对21个NVG拓扑度量进行重要性分析，发现紧凑的Top3子集在CICIDS2018数据集上取得比全集更高的分类性能和显著降低的运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NVG分析中并非所有拓扑描述符对网络攻击分类贡献相同，提取大量度量会增加计算成本，因此需要评估是否能以紧凑子集保持分类能力并提高效率。

Method: 评估21个NVG派生拓扑度量，集成SHAP、分组排列重要性、Boruta和RFE四种重要性分析方法，通过共识排序生成Full21、Top15、Top10、Top7、Top5和Top3配置，使用CICIDS2018数据集、CNN分类器和分层5折交叉验证进行评价。

Result: 排名最高的三个度量为avg_clustering_coeff_median、avg_clustering_coeff_std和avg_clustering_coeff_mean。Top3取得最高平均性能：准确率97.148%、加权F1分数97.055%、MCC 0.9675，而Full21分别为95.999%、95.521%、0.9549；总运行时间从14,961.39秒降至589.22秒，减少96.06%。

Conclusion: 重要性引导的度量减少可在评估设置下提供紧凑的NVG表示，具有更高的观测平均预测性能和显著更低的计算成本。

Abstract: Natural Visibility Graph (NVG) based analysis characterizes network traffic through topological descriptors reflecting different structural properties. However, not all descriptors contribute equally to cyber-attack classification, and extracting a large metric set can increase computational cost. This study evaluates 21 NVG derived topological metrics and investigates whether a compact subset can preserve classification capability while improving computational efficiency. Four importance analysis methods SHAP, grouped Permutation Importance, Boruta, and Recursive Feature Elimination (RFE) are integrated through a Consensus Ranking strategy. Based on this ranking, Full21, Top15, Top10, Top7, Top5, and Top3 configurations are evaluated using the CICIDS2018 dataset, a CNN classifier, and stratified 5 fold cross validation. The three highest ranked metrics are avg_clustering_coeff_median, avg_clustering_coeff_std, and avg_clustering_coeff_mean. Top3 achieved the highest observed mean performance, with 97.148% accuracy, 97.055% weighted F1 score, and an MCC of 0.9675, compared with 95.999%, 95.521%, and 0.9549 for Full21, respectively. It also reduced total runtime from 14,961.39 s to 589.22 s (96.06%). These results indicate that importance guided metric reduction can provide a compact NVG representation with higher observed mean predictive performance and substantially lower computational cost under the evaluated setting.

</details>


### [200] [Mitigating Private Data Leakage in LLMs with Whiteout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02418)
*Anna Yoo Jeong Ha,Ronik Bhaskar,Haitao Zheng,Ben Y. Zhao*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出工具Whiteout，通过精心设计的混淆样本覆盖敏感个人信息，防止大语言模型泄露真实PSI，并在多模型上验证其有效性、低副作用和强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在庞大未过滤数据上训练，常记忆并再现出生日期、电话、住址等个人敏感信息，对高管、政治人物、法官等高风险个体构成隐私威胁；现有机器遗忘方法过度删除信息、损害模型效用与安全性且易受攻击。

Method: Whiteout在个人请求下，用精确设计的混淆样本覆盖目标PSI，从而阻止模型复述真实敏感信息。

Result: 在多种规模与厂商的现代LLM（包括某广泛使用的OpenAI模型）上，Whiteout有效防止目标PSI泄露，对模型效用与安全性影响可忽略，优于现有替代方案；在从黑盒越狱到白盒重学习与量化等自适应攻击的广泛对抗测试下仍表现良好。

Conclusion: Whiteout是实用且鲁棒的PSI保护工具，并讨论了其安全与伦理影响。

Abstract: Modern large language models (LLMs) are trained on massive, largely unfiltered datasets, including content scraped from nearly every accessible website and user inputs. As a result, LLMs often memorize and reproduce personally sensitive information (PSI) such as birth dates, phone numbers, and home addresses. This leads to significant privacy risks, particularly for high-profile individuals such as executives, politicians, and judges. Existing mitigations largely rely on machine unlearning. However, these methods often remove more information than needed, degrade model utility and safety, and are highly vulnerable to attacks.
  This paper presents Whiteout, a practical tool that, upon requests by individuals, prevents LLMs from regurgitating their genuine PSIs, by overwriting them using precise and carefully designed obfuscation samples. We evaluate Whiteout on modern LLMs of varying sizes and makers, including a widely-used OpenAI model. Results show that Whiteout effectively prevents disclosure of the targeted PSIs, has negligible impact on model utility and safety, and outperforms existing alternatives. We also test Whiteout against a wide range of countermeasures, from black-box attacks like jailbreaking to white-box adaptive attacks like relearning and quantization. Finally, we conclude with a discussion on the security and ethical implications of Whiteout.

</details>


### [201] [Evaluating and Improving the Robustness of Large Language Models to Input Sequence Variations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02432)
*Narek Maloyan*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本论文针对LLM生产系统面临的提示注入、后门和自动质量指标操控等对抗性输入序列变化，开发了评估与提升鲁棒性的模型、方法与算法，涵盖生成鲁棒性度量、局部与非局部攻击理论、LLM-as-a-Judge攻击、后门检测、多层防御绕过系统化、异构模型委员会防御以及基于MCP的智能体系统安全机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的大语言模型面临提示注入、木马（后门）以及自动质量指标被操纵等安全威胁，亟需系统性地评估和提升模型对对抗性输入序列变化的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出基于Jensen-Shannon散度的生成鲁棒性度量R_stab(f)；针对局部攻击证明V(h) <= 1 - R_class(h)；针对非局部攻击提出校准经验模型；开发自适应进化黑盒攻击ASA；在Trojan Detection Challenge 2023上研究替代触发器；在SaTML CTF 2024上系统化四类多层防御绕过；使用5-7个异构模型委员会防御；针对基于MCP的智能体系统提出AttestMCP（HMAC保护的工具调用认证，单次调用<0.1ms）和Commit Boundary隔离模式。

Result: ASA攻击成功率达73.8%，开放模型间迁移率达62.6%；在Pythia-1.4B上，替代触发器的REASR约0.99，而真实触发器召回率仅约0.17（基线约0.14）；SaTML CTF 2024中四类绕过将ASR从90%降至15-25%；5-7个异构模型委员会将Gemma-3-4B的ASR降低47-55个百分点，7个模型时降至19.3%；MCPBench 847个场景中，平均ASR从53.7%降至12.4%。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法分别实现在JudgeGuard、TrojanArmor软件套件和MCPSec模块中，有效提升了LLM在生成、判断、后门检测和智能体工具调用等多种场景下对对抗性输入序列变化的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) in production systems face prompt injections, trojans (backdoors), and manipulation of automatic quality metrics. This thesis develops models, methods, and algorithms for evaluating and improving LLM robustness to adversarial input sequence variations. We propose R_stab(f), a generative robustness metric based on the Jensen-Shannon divergence between per-step output distributions under small input perturbations. For localized attacks we prove V(h) <= 1 - R_class(h), where R_class(h) is the probability that a decision operator h keeps its decision under small perturbations. For non-localized attacks we propose a calibrated empirical model. For LLM-as-a-Judge systems we develop ASA, an adaptive evolutionary black-box attack that reaches an attack success rate (ASR) of up to 73.8%, with transfer between open models up to 62.6%. On Trojan Detection Challenge 2023 data (Pythia-1.4B), surrogate triggers reach REASR ~0.99 while recall of the true triggers is ~0.17 against a baseline of ~0.14. On SaTML CTF 2024 we systematize four classes of bypasses of multi-layer defenses, which reduce the ASR from 90% to 15-25%. Committees of 5-7 heterogeneous models reduce the ASR for Gemma-3-4B by 47-55 percentage points, to 19.3% with 7 models. For agentic systems based on the Model Context Protocol (MCP), we propose AttestMCP, which attests tool calls with HMAC-protected packets at under 0.1 ms per call, and the Commit Boundary isolation pattern. On the MCPBench benchmark of 847 scenarios they reduce the average ASR from 53.7% to 12.4%. The methods are implemented in the JudgeGuard and TrojanArmor software suites and the MCPSec module.

</details>


### [202] [THPL: A Vision-to-Language Decision Support Framework for Rainbow Trout Feeding Management in RAS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02378)
*Meng Liang,Guanbo Feng,Haozhuang Chi,Shilong Zhao,Zhixin Xiong,Yuhang He,Wenfeng Han,Tianhao Zhao,Zhihong Ma,Ying Liu*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出THPL生成式投喂决策框架，通过鱼类行为轨迹与专家知识对齐，实现可解释的精准投喂决策，显著提升决策准确率与文本生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环水养殖系统中精准投喂对降低成本、提升鱼类福利至关重要，但现有方法缺乏鱼类行为与管理知识之间的认知对齐，难以转化为可执行、可解释的投喂决策。

Method: THPL框架包含三部分：Fishsort提取轨迹并建立活动系数（AC）量化摄食强度；分层行为编码器（HBE）利用时序Transformer和集合Transformer建模个体时序与群体动态，将轨迹张量转化为显式物理与隐式软令牌的双证据表示；将令牌与环境参数、元数据及专家规则结合，通过LoRA微调LLM，并采用反事实多模态直接偏好优化（mDPO）强化因果推理。

Result: AC与专家标注摄食强度呈显著单调正相关（Spearman ρ=0.925，p<0.001）。消融实验表明，双证据令牌使决策准确率从纯文本基线的33.33%提升至93.33%。相比标准LoRA，反事实mDPO将决策准确率从93.33%提升至96.67%，METEOR从58.10%提升至85.30%，Self-BLEU-2从58.79%降至52.88%，Distinct-3从6.68%提升至7.81%，抑制模板化与执行偏差，增强因果一致性与操作安全性。

Conclusion: 通过将连续运动学与LLM推理相结合，本研究为精准水产养殖提供了一种新颖的决策支持范式。

Abstract: In Recirculating Aquaculture Systems (RAS), precision feeding is critical for minimizing costs and improving fish welfare. However, existing methods lack cognitive alignment between fish behaviors and management knowledge, impeding translation into executable, interpretable feeding decisions. To address this, we propose THPL, a generative feeding decision framework tailored for rainbow trout (Oncorhynchus mykiss) in RAS. First, Fishsort extracts trajectories to establish an Activity Coefficient (AC) quantifying feeding intensity. Second, a Hierarchical Behavior Encoder (HBE) models individual temporal progression and collective dynamics using Temporal and Set Transformers, transforming trajectory tensors into dual-evidence representations of explicit physical and implicit soft tokens. Finally, these tokens are integrated with environmental parameters, metadata, and expert rules to fine-tune an LLM via LoRA, followed by counterfactual multimodal Direct Preference Optimization (mDPO) to reinforce causal reasoning. Results show that AC exhibits a statistically significant monotonic positive correlation with expert-annotated feeding intensity (Spearman $ρ= 0.925$, $p < 0.001$). Ablations indicate that decision accuracy improves from 33.33% (text-only baseline) to 93.33% with dual-evidence tokens, confirming that continuous spatiotemporal tokens provide necessary physical grounding for LLMs. Compared with standard LoRA, counterfactual mDPO elevates decision accuracy from 93.33% to 96.67%, advances METEOR from 58.10% to 85.30%, reduces Self-BLEU-2 from 58.79% to 52.88%, and increases Distinct-3 from 6.68% to 7.81%, suppressing templating and actuation biases while reinforcing causal consistency and operational safety. Overall, by integrating continuous kinematics with LLM reasoning, this study provides a novel decision support paradigm for precision aquaculture.

</details>


### [203] [SoK: Stablecoins in the Quantum Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02435)
*Panagiotis Chatzigiannis,Navid Alamati,Suvradip Chakraborty,Duc V. Le*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文是对后量子稳定币的系统化知识（SoK）研究，提出了稳定币特定的威胁模型，映射密码依赖到货币控制面，并从授权主体、变更位置和迁移类型三个维度分类迁移选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定币在数字资产生态中支持支付、交易、抵押和跨链结算，但集中了发行方、托管、升级、预言机和桥接密钥的价值，同时继承了底层链账户、共识、rollup和隐私系统的量子脆弱性。因此需要系统化研究后量子稳定币的迁移与安全问题。

Method: 开发稳定币特定的威胁模型；将密码依赖映射到货币控制面；沿三个维度（谁可授权变更、变更必须发生在哪里、迁移是混合、后量子原生还是经典组件封装）分类迁移选择；回顾相关密码原语并区分一般区块链故障与稳定币特定影响；考察近期区块链和发行方控制的互操作性提案；将迁移选择与当前稳定币监管下的赎回、连续性和干预要求关联。

Result: 识别出权威-责任缺口：能迁移组件的方往往不同于在失败时承担损失的用户、交易所、协议或发行方。还发现开放问题：聚合与阈值授权、操作特定的安全级别和成本、休眠和包装供应、私有和合规启用的转移、以及量子脆弱稳定币暴露的测量。

Conclusion: 后量子稳定币迁移需要解决权威与责任不匹配、聚合授权、操作安全成本、休眠供应、隐私合规转移及暴露测量等开放问题，并需与监管要求协调。

Abstract: Stablecoins support payments, trading, collateral, and cross-chain settlement across the digital-asset ecosystem. They also concentrate value behind issuer, custody, upgrade, oracle, and bridge keys while inheriting the quantum vulnerabilities of host-chain accounts, consensus, rollups, and privacy systems. This paper presents a Systematization of Knowledge (SoK) on post-quantum stablecoins. We develop a stablecoin-specific threat model, map cryptographic dependencies to monetary control surfaces, and classify migration choices along three dimensions: who can authorize a change, where the change must occur, and whether it is hybrid, post-quantum native, or an encapsulation of a classical component. We emphasize an authority-liability gap: the party able to migrate a component is often different from the holders, exchanges, protocols, or issuers that bear the loss if it fails. We review the relevant cryptographic primitives, but distinguish general blockchain failures from their stablecoin-specific effects. We also examine recent blockchain and issuer-controlled interoperability proposals, and relate migration choices to redemption, continuity, and intervention requirements under current stablecoin regulation. Our findings identify open problems in aggregate and threshold authorization, operation-specific security levels and costs, dormant and wrapped supply, private and compliance-enabled transfers, and measurement of quantum-vulnerable stablecoin exposure.

</details>


### [204] [SideKernel: A Usable microVM Sandbox for AI Coding Agents on macOS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02456)
*Dimitrios Prasakis*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 该论文针对AI编码代理作为不可信组件却需在开发者机器上自主运行的矛盾，提出并评估了一个面向macOS的本地开源微虚拟机沙箱SideKernel。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码代理需要本地自主权但不可信，现有本地开源macOS沙箱方案少且难用；用户调查显示不足40%的用户在沙箱中运行代理，并识别出主要可用性障碍。

Method: 通过在线用户调查确定可用性障碍；开发基于微虚拟机的开源本地macOS沙箱SideKernel；整理市场沙箱并按五项标准筛选，基于23项能力测试与符合条件的沙箱进行对比分析。

Result: 仅有少数沙箱与SideKernel相似；在可用性相关能力特征上，Docker Sandboxes和SideKernel得分最高。

Conclusion: SideKernel是面向AI编码代理的可用性优先本地开源微虚拟机macOS沙箱，并附带对现有本地开源微虚拟机macOS沙箱解决方案空间的综述。

Abstract: AI coding agents are untrusted system components, yet they require autonomy on the developer machines they run on. This contradiction is a security problem. Sandboxes provide an isolated environment, but for local macOS development, the existing local, open-source options for AI coding agents are few in number and cumbersome to use. I conducted a formative online user survey which indicates that fewer than 40% of AI coding agent users run their agents in a sandbox and identifies the top usability barriers hindering AI coding agent sandbox adoption. These findings are used to develop SideKernel: an open-source, local, microVM-based macOS sandbox for AI coding agents designed for usability. To evaluate SideKernel, I compiled a list of sandboxes available on the market and filtered it against five inclusion criteria. Then I performed a comparative analysis between SideKernel and the sandboxes that satisfy these criteria, across 23 capability tests derived from the usability barriers revealed by the user survey. I discovered that only a few sandboxes are similar to SideKernel, and that among those, Docker Sandboxes and SideKernel score highest on capability features related to usability. A secondary contribution of this paper is a survey of the existing solution space for local, open-source, microVM-based macOS sandboxes for AI coding agents.

</details>


### [205] [MACTS-EM: Multi-Agent Collaborative Time Series Forecasting with Emergent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02255)
*Ahmad Shahi,Mamehgol Yousefi*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出MACTS-EM多智能体协作时间序列预测框架，集成专业智能体、元认知层、涌现记忆、多模态上下文与对抗鲁棒性，在多个领域超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序预测方法难以应对机制切换、跨域知识迁移和多模态数据集成等复杂现象。

Method: MACTS-EM架构包含：领域专业预测智能体（模式识别、异常检测、因果推断、不确定性量化）、动态智能体分配的元认知层、支持跨域模式迁移的涌现记忆机制、多模态上下文集成以及对抗鲁棒性组件。

Result: 在金融市场、气候模式、能源消耗和疫情传播评估中，预测精度提升8-12%，零样本迁移能力提升22-27%，机制切换韧性增强16-21%，分布偏移后恢复速度加快15-18%。

Conclusion: 协作式智能体方法在复杂现实场景中具有超越传统架构的潜力，尤其适用于需要多分辨率时间理解和上下文适应的任务。

Abstract: Time series forecasting remains a critical challenge across numerous domains. Despite significant advancements, existing approaches struggle with complex phenomena such as regime shifts, cross-domain knowledge transfer, and multimodal data integration. This paper introduces Multi-Agent Collaborative Time Series Forecasting with Emergent Memory (MACTS-EM), a novel framework where specialised agents collaborate to achieve superior forecasting performance. The MACTS-EM architecture integrates: (1) domain-specialised forecasting agents for pattern recognition, anomaly detection, causal inference, and uncertainty quantification; (2) a meta-cognitive layer for dynamic agent allocation; (3) an emergent memory mechanism enabling cross-domain pattern transfer; (4) multimodal contextual integration; and (5) adversarial robustness components. Evaluation across financial markets, climate patterns, energy consumption, and pandemic propagation demonstrates that MACTS-EM outperforms existing approaches in most scenarios, with 8-12% improvement in forecasting accuracy, 22-27% better zero-shot transfer capability, 16-21% enhanced resilience during regime shifts, and 15-18% faster recovery after distribution shifts. Our findings suggest that collaborative, agentic approaches to time series forecasting represent a promising direction beyond traditional architectures, particularly for complex real-world scenarios requiring multi-resolution temporal understanding and contextual adaptation.

</details>


### [206] [Out of Sync, Out of Sight: Phantom State Attacks against IIoT Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02552)
*Sabrine Ennaji,Elhadj Benkhelifa,Nadia Kabachi*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出Phantom State Attack（PSA），在被动零查询威胁模型下，利用IIoT入侵检测系统时间窗口聚合的同步依赖，通过注入有界时间漂移使观测跨越窗口边界，重构出与真实状态偏离的幻影状态，从而降低检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对机器学习入侵检测系统的对抗研究多依赖梯度访问、重复模型查询或学习正常流量模型，且假设工业监控管道中时间同步不成问题；然而IDS通过滑动/滚动窗口聚合遥测重构运行状态，其视图依赖于观测落入窗口的相对时刻，这一时间同步假设未被充分关注。

Method: PSA不修改数据包、不扰动特征、不查询分类器、也不拟合正常流量模型；而是根据攻击流自身的到达间隔变异性校准有界时间漂移，以最小偏移将观测移动到最近窗口边界另一侧，使IDS重构出偏离真实过程状态的幻影状态。在ToN-IoT和CIC IIoT 2025（DataSense）上，针对Random Forest、MLP和XGBoost评估检测退化、同步失真、隐蔽性和攻击成本。

Result: PSA对携带足够数据包、可进行窗口边界重分配的攻击流会显著降低检测性能，而对其他流几乎无影响，效果具有条件性；基于查询的基线原始成功率更高，但每个窗口需大量查询，而PSA无需任何查询。

Conclusion: 时间聚合是可在比先前规避技术更弱假设下触及的攻击面，PSA揭示了IIoT入侵检测系统中窗口同步机制的安全隐患。

Abstract: Machine learning-based intrusion detection systems (IDS) are critical for securing Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) environments. Most adversarial research against them perturbs the feature vector or the traffic that produces it, and depends on gradient access, repeated model queries, or a learned model of benign traffic. A smaller line of work reshapes packet timing without querying the detector, but makes malicious traffic mimic a learned model of benign timing. Across these approaches, one assumption of industrial monitoring pipelines has received little attention: temporal synchronization. An IDS reconstructs operational state by aggregating telemetry into sliding or tumbling windows, so its view depends not only on what is observed but on when each observation falls relative to a window boundary.
  We introduce the Phantom State Attack (PSA), which exploits that dependence under a passive, zero-query threat model. Rather than modifying packets, perturbing features, querying the classifier, or fitting any model of benign traffic, PSA injects bounded timing drift calibrated to the attack flow's own inter-arrival variability, moving observations across the nearest window boundary by the minimal shift needed. The IDS then reconstructs a phantom state that diverges from the true process state.
  We evaluate PSA on ToN-IoT and CIC IIoT 2025 (DataSense), against Random Forest, MLP and XGBoost, measuring detection degradation, synchronization distortion, stealth, and attacker cost. PSA degrades detection on flows carrying enough packets for window-boundary redistribution, and leaves others almost unchanged, so its effect is conditional. A query-based baseline reaches higher raw success but needs many queries per window, while PSA needs none. The results identify temporal aggregation as an attack surface reachable under weaker assumptions than prior evasion techniques.

</details>


### [207] [Pincer: Resource Authorization for Agents using a Digital Twin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02569)
*Mayank Rathee,Alexander Stepanov,Shalin Madabhavi,Jinhao Zhu,Raluca Ada Popa,Ion Stoica*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出 Pincer，一种在资源层与现有工具调用层防御协同工作的新型防御机制，通过数字孪生自动学习并执行用户特定的动态最小权限策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体日益长时程、自主并依赖通用 shell 与持久记忆，现有防御（类型化工具、信息流控制、策略预测引擎）牺牲过多功能；用户维护策略随时间衰减且导致权限请求疲劳，自动模式工具调用分类器不学习用户特定策略且不针对对抗性场景。

Method: 核心是数字孪生——一个隔离上下文模型，持续学习用户偏好，作为用户代理处理智能体的权限请求，自动学习并执行动态用户特定最小权限策略；为模拟学习阶段，构建了基于多日用户-智能体交互转录的用户中心数据集。

Result: 在安全性与实用性上均强于多个基线（包括 LLM 评判器变体与 Conseca（HotOS '25）改编版）；在 Pincer 设计带来显著安全提升的攻击类型上表现突出，对其他攻击类型也优于基线。

Conclusion: Pincer 可在资源层与工具调用层防御互补，在不牺牲过多功能的前提下，通过学习用户特定策略有效提升编码智能体对对抗性威胁的防御能力。

Abstract: Coding agents have become increasingly long-horizon, autonomous, reliant on general-purpose shell and maintain their own persistent memory for self-improvement. While these capabilities have made the agents powerful, they have also made them harder to defend against external adversaries. Defenses that restrict this architecture --- typed tools, information-flow control, or policy prediction engines --- give up too much functionality to be adopted. Agents deployed today (e.g. Claude, Codex) rely on a combination of user-mediated and automode sandboxing as their primary defense. In user-mediated sandboxing, user-maintained policies decay over time and repeated permission requests cause user fatigue, while auto mode's tool-call classifiers learn no user-specific policy and are not meant to defend against adversarial setups. Pincer is a new defense that operates at the resource layer and works alongside existing defenses at the tool-call layer like the auto mode. At the core of Pincer lies a digital twin, an isolated-context model that automatically learns and enforces dynamic user-specific least-privilege policies. The digital twin keeps continually learning the user's preferences allowing it to act as the user's proxy for the agent's permission requests. To emulate the learning phase, we propose a new usercentric dataset with examples following a multi-day transcript of user-agent interaction. Our evaluation shows that Pincer performs strongly on both security and utility in comparison to several baselines which includes variants of LLM judges and adaptations of Conseca (HotOS '25). We highlight attack types where Pincer's design leads to a significant security improvement compared to all other baselines, while outperforming the baselines even for other types of attacks.

</details>


### [208] [From TS-SUF-2 to TS-SUF-4: Practical Security Enhancements for FROST2 Threshold Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02805)
*Will Wang,Syh-Yuan Tan,Ryan Chow,Chanson Chan,Martin Zhao*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出FROST2+，通过增加预处理令牌验证，在相同假设下将FROST2的安全性从TS-SUF-2提升至TS-SUF-4，并保持高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FROST2作为实用的阈值Schnorr签名方案，在一次性离散对数假设、静态腐败和集中式密钥生成下仅达到TS-SUF-2安全性，易受TS-UF-3攻击，需增强其安全性。

Method: 在FROST2基础上集成额外的预处理令牌验证，以缓解TS-UF-3和TS-UF-4漏洞，同时保持实际效率；并在相同计算假设及分布式密钥生成协议（如PedPoP）初始化下分析其安全性。

Result: FROST2+在相同条件下及PedPoP初始化下均达到TS-SUF-4安全；基于ZCash的FROST库基准测试显示其性能与FROST2相当，启用预计算时比FROST快约64-79%。

Conclusion: FROST2+有效提升了FROST2的安全性至TS-SUF-4，且不牺牲效率，适用于区块链和分布式系统。

Abstract: Threshold signature schemes play a vital role in securing digital assets within blockchain and distributed systems. FROST2 stands out as a practical threshold Schnorr signature scheme, noted for its efficiency and compatibility with standard verification processes. However, under the one-more discrete logarithm assumption, with static corruption and centralized key generation settings, FROST2 has been shown by Bellare et al. (in CRYPTO 2022) to achieve only TS-SUF-2 security, which is a consequence of its vulnerability to TS-UF-3 attacks.
  In this paper, we address this security limitation by presenting an enhanced variant of FROST2, namely, FROST2+ which achieves the TS-SUF-4 security level under the same computational assumptions as the original FROST2. FROST2+ strengthens FROST2 by integrating additional pre-processing token verifications that help mitigate TS-UF-3 and TS-UF-4 vulnerabilities while maintaining practical efficiency. We show that FROST2+ can achieve TS-SUF-4 security not only under the same conditions as the original FROST2 analysis, but also when initialized with a distributed key generation protocol such as PedPoP. Our benchmark using ZCash's FROST library shows that the performance of FROST2+ is comparable to FROST2 and about 64-79% faster than FROST when precomputation is enabled.

</details>


### [209] [RMCW: A Deletion-Robust Watermark Based on Reed--Muller Codes for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02817)
*Yi Wang,Baicheng Chen,Yu Wang,Jian Zhao,Yilei Chen,Tianxing He*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出RMCW，基于Reed-Muller码的LLM水印方法，通过搜索存活的局部代数结构来提升删除攻击下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM水印在删除攻击下鲁棒性脆弱，因为删除会改变token位置并破坏观测token与原始水印位置的对齐。

Method: RMCW利用Reed-Muller码的仿射线限制诱导的Reed-Solomon一致性，生成时通过密钥词表划分注入Reed-Muller结构；检测时将文本映射到密钥词表桶，并用Berlekamp-Welch测试局部子序列的低次Reed-Solomon一致性。

Result: 在C4和ELI5数据集上，使用OPT-1.3B与Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct实验表明，RMCW保持强干净文本可检测性，并在多种删除与改写攻击下优于或匹敌基线方法。

Conclusion: RMCW通过局部代数结构恢复有效应对删除攻击，提升了LLM水印的鲁棒性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) watermarking provides a lightweight mechanism for identifying text generated by a specific model, but its robustness remains fragile under post-processing attacks. Deletion attacks are particularly challenging because they shift token positions and break the alignment between observed tokens and their original watermark positions. We propose Reed--Muller Code Watermarking (RMCW), an LLM watermarking method based on Reed--Muller codes. In contrast to global codeword recovery, RMCW searches for surviving local algebraic structure, leveraging the Reed--Solomon consistency induced by affine-line restrictions of Reed--Muller codewords. During generation, RMCW injects a Reed--Muller structure into the sequence via a secret-keyed vocabulary partition. During detection, it maps the given text to keyed vocabulary bins and tests local subsequences for low-degree Reed--Solomon consistency using Berlekamp--Welch tests. Experiments on C4 and ELI5 datasets with OPT-1.3B and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct show that RMCW preserves strong clean-text detectability and outperforms or matches the baseline methods under several deletion and rewriting attacks. Our code is available at https://github.com/BaichengDanny/RMCW.

</details>


### [210] [Containing the Autonomous Operator: A Defense-in-Depth Framework and Reference Architecture for Securing AI Agents on Kubernetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02861)
*Simhadri Podala Narasimha*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 该论文将大语言模型（LLM）代理在Kubernetes上的安全视为基础设施问题，而非模型边界问题，提出威胁模型、设计原则、七层纵深防御框架及参考架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理从聊天界面进入基础设施运维，能读取遥测、调用工具、执行代码并改变生产Kubernetes集群状态，这打破了云原生安全所假设的数据与控制边界：代理仅读取的内容（日志行、工单、工具描述）可重定向其行为。因此不能将模型视为安全边界，必须在代理被提示注入完全攻陷的假设下保证安全。

Method: 贡献包括：(i) 针对在Kubernetes上及内部运行的代理的威胁模型和十类威胁分类法，与OWASP代理应用指南对齐；(ii) 九项设计原则，核心是工具边界处的完全中介，以及打破不可信输入、敏感访问和外部出口的组合；(iii) 七层纵深防御框架，将原则映射到原生或广泛采用的Kubernetes机制：工作负载身份、RBAC与ValidatingAdmissionPolicy、通过SIG Apps Agent Sandbox项目的gVisor/Kata沙箱、FQDN感知出口策略、带策略即代码的代理/MCP网关（对工具参数）、eBPF运行时执行；(iv) 参考架构，包含具体策略工件和针对Amazon EKS、Azure Kubernetes Service、Google Kubernetes Engine的逐层绑定；(v) 定性评估，包括威胁控制覆盖矩阵和四个攻击走查，以及提出的实证方法。

Result: 未报告实测的攻击成功率或开销数据；相反，识别了残余风险以及验证框架所需的测量指标。

Conclusion: 代理在Kubernetes上的安全应作为基础设施问题处理，在假设代理被完全攻陷的前提下保证所有安全保证持续成立，并通过多层防御和未来实证测量来验证框架。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are moving from chat interfaces into infrastructure operations, where they read telemetry, call tools, generate and execute code, and change the state of production Kubernetes clusters. This collapses a boundary that conventional cloud-native security assumes: the boundary between data and control. Content that an agent merely reads (a log line, a ticket, a tool description) can redirect what it does. This paper argues that the model must not be treated as a security boundary and that agent safety on Kubernetes is therefore an infrastructure problem: every guarantee must continue to hold under the assumption that the agent is fully compromised by prompt injection. We contribute (i) a threat model and ten-class threat taxonomy for agents operating on and within Kubernetes, aligned with emerging OWASP guidance for agentic applications; (ii) nine design principles, centered on complete mediation at the tool boundary and on breaking the combination of untrusted input, sensitive access, and external egress; (iii) a seven-layer defense-in-depth framework that maps each principle to native or widely adopted Kubernetes mechanisms: workload identity, RBAC and ValidatingAdmissionPolicy, gVisor/Kata sandboxing via the SIG Apps Agent Sandbox project, FQDN-aware egress policy, an agent/MCP gateway with policy-as-code over tool arguments, and eBPF runtime enforcement; (iv) a reference architecture with concrete policy artifacts and per-layer bindings for Amazon EKS, Azure Kubernetes Service, and Google Kubernetes Engine; and (v) a qualitative evaluation comprising a threat-control coverage matrix and four attack walkthroughs, with a proposed empirical methodology. We report no measured attack-success or overhead figures; instead we identify residual risks and the measurements needed to validate the framework.

</details>


### [211] ["I just assumed that it would translate": examining MT risk awareness among healthcare staff with abbreviations as a use case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02496)
*Eleanor Taylor-Stilgoe,Félix do Carmo,Constantin Orăsan*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 英国医护人员使用机器翻译（主要是谷歌翻译）与患者沟通，但其对高风险场景下（尤其是涉及医疗缩写时）潜在风险的认知有限，本研究通过访谈21名医护人员探讨此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医护人员在语言障碍沟通中依赖机器翻译可能危及患者安全，但现有研究多关注人际交流或患者文档，缺乏对更高风险场景（如患者病历）中员工风险认知的探讨，且医疗缩写本身 monolingually 已增加患者风险。

Method: 从权威临床语料库中选取法语和西班牙语上下文数据示例，通过谷歌翻译，在半结构化访谈中呈现给21名不同角色和专科的医护人员，然后对结果进行定性分析和交叉分析。

Result: 摘要未明确报告具体结果，仅说明对访谈内容进行了定性分析和交叉分析。

Conclusion: 摘要未明确给出结论，仅指出研究旨在识别医疗缩写经机器翻译后可能带来的潜在风险。

Abstract: In the UK, public healthcare staff report turning to machine translation (MT) - predominantly Google Translate (GT) - to communicate with patients across language barriers. Though intended to support their duty of care, potentially uninformed reliance on MT in such contexts could have serious consequences for patient safety. Research nonetheless remains limited on staff awareness of the possible risks posed by higher-stakes MT use in general and with patient medical records in particular, most existing literature instead examining its use in interpersonal situations or with patient-oriented documentation. Moreover, medical abbreviations are well-documented as increasing patient risk even monolingually, with outcomes from their misuse and/or misinterpretation ranging from temporary harm to the death of the patient. Abbreviations were therefore selected as a use case for identifying the potential risks posed by their translation with MT. Contextualised French and Spanish data examples drawn from authoritative clinical corpora and translated via GT were presented during semi-structured interviews to 21 healthcare staff participants in diverse roles and specialties. The results were then subject to qualitative analysis and cross-analysis.

</details>


### [212] [AgentTrap: Stateful Feedback Deception against Autonomous Penetration Testing Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02869)
*Yuelin Wang,Jiongchi Yu,Yanbang Sun*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出首个面向自主渗透测试智能体的闭环蜜罐AgentTrap，通过哨兵端点、有状态欺骗与行为引导升级，在真实Web应用中降低攻击成功率并收集攻击者证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统蜜罐依赖静态诱饵和预定义响应，无法适应自主渗透测试智能体不断演变的攻击策略，因此需要能闭环自适应、持续交互的蜜罐。

Method: AgentTrap采用哨兵端点避免良性干扰，基于被保护应用构建有状态欺骗，并通过行为引导升级维持交互，在受控披露下收集智能体侧行为证据；在含真实端点和蜜罐端点的Web应用中，用三种防御策略对八个自主渗透测试智能体进行评估。

Result: 相比无防御，AgentTrap将真实目标总体攻击成功率从95.8%降至79.2%，并在18.8%的运行中成功诱出攻击者API密钥，优于静态欺骗和固定升级；追踪分析表明，抵抗此类反制同时依赖模型级对欺骗请求的识别和架构级敏感资源隔离。

Conclusion: AgentTrap验证了闭环自适应蜜罐对自主渗透测试智能体的防御有效性，并揭示有效反制需结合模型识别与架构隔离两方面能力。

Abstract: Autonomous penetration testing agents conduct multi-step attacks by continuously adapting their plans and actions to target responses. As a common defense, honeypots can be deployed to divert these agents from real assets by presenting decoy services, while also supporting attack tracing and active counterattacks. However, conventional honeypots rely primarily on static artifacts and predefined responses, leaving them unable to adapt to the evolving attack strategies of autonomous penetration testing agents.
